/**
 * True-resolution Scene2D desktop for Pinux Astra.
 *
 * The pixel-art world retains its 480x270 framebuffer; only the desktop
 * user interface renders after the game's framebuffer at native window size.
 * Scene2D owns layout, pointer hit testing, hover, focus, and animations.
 */
public final class PinuxDesktopStage {
  private final PinuxAstra shell;
  // The desktop owns game-IDE typing while Astra Code is focused. This prevents
  // Scene2D controls from swallowing tactile ByteScript input on Windows.
  private final Stage stage = new Stage(new ScreenViewport()) {
    @Override public boolean keyDown(int keycode) {
      if (shell != null && shell.codeTypingKey(keycode)) return true;
      return super.keyDown(keycode);
    }
    @Override public boolean keyTyped(char character) {
      if (shell != null && shell.isCodeFront()) return true;
      return super.keyTyped(character);
    }
  };
  private final Skin skin = new Skin();
  private Table topBar, dock, shortcuts, launcher;
  private Label clock, current;
  private Image wallpaper, wallpaperDim;
  private PinuxNativeWindows windows;
  private boolean visible;
  private float scale = 1f;

  public PinuxDesktopStage(PinuxAstra shell, Texture desktopTexture) {
    this.shell = shell;
    BitmapFont ui = font("fonts/hd/ui.fnt", .48f);
    BitmapFont small = font("fonts/hd/small.fnt", .48f);
    skin.add("ui-font", ui);
    skin.add("small-font", small);
    skin.add("dock-bg", patch("dock-bg", 11, 30, 34, 45, 242, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("wallpaper-dim", patch("wallpaper-dim", 0, 12, 17, 25, 137, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("shortcut-idle", patch("shortcut-idle", 7, 36, 41, 51, 0, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("shortcut-hover", patch("shortcut-hover", 7, 55, 63, 75, 145, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("dock-idle", patch("dock-idle", 5, 36, 41, 51, 0, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("dock-hover", patch("dock-hover", 5, 65, 77, 91, 180, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("top-bg", patch("top-bg", 0, 25, 28, 36, 247, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("hover-bg", patch("hover-bg", 12, 20, 49, 100, 86, 154), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("button-bg", patch("button-bg", 13, 20, 43, 105, 90, 159), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("launcher-bg", patch("launcher-bg", 9, 26, 30, 39, 252, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("window-bg", patch("window-bg", 7, 30, 33, 40, 253, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("pane-bg", patch("pane-bg", 11, 10, 18, 35, 246, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("input-bg", patch("input-bg", 8, 20, 32, 53, 245, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("window-header", patch("window-header", 4, 41, 45, 55, 255, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("title-button", patch("title-button", 7, 32, 40, 60, 176, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("title-button-hover", patch("title-button-hover", 7, 61, 71, 105, 235, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("title-close-hover", patch("title-close-hover", 7, 123, 52, 74, 248, 0), Drawable.class);
    // OneDark / Archcraft-inspired terminal palette. Keep separate from the
    // desktop and other applications until this single design is approved.
    skin.add("term-frame", patch("term-frame", 12, 46, 50, 60, 255, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("term-header", patch("term-header", 7, 41, 45, 55, 255, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("term-surface", patch("term-surface", 8, 30, 33, 40, 255, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("term-toolbar", patch("term-toolbar", 5, 35, 39, 48, 255, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("term-tab", patch("term-tab", 5, 47, 52, 62, 255, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("term-prompt", patch("term-prompt", 6, 41, 45, 55, 255, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("term-title-button", patch("term-title-button", 5, 41, 45, 55, 180, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("term-title-hover", patch("term-title-hover", 5, 72, 81, 97, 255, 0), Drawable.class);
    skin.add("term-title-close", patch("term-title-close", 5, 154, 59, 68, 255, 0), Drawable.class);

    addIcon("code", 93, 72, 188);
    addIcon("terminal", 60, 69, 111);
    addIcon("files", 193, 139, 87);
    addIcon("mail", 137, 94, 196);
    addIcon("settings", 91, 96, 139);

    wallpaper = new Image(new TextureRegionDrawable(new TextureRegion(desktopTexture)));
    wallpaper.setScaling(Scaling.stretch);
    wallpaper.setTouchable(Touchable.disabled);
    stage.addActor(wallpaper);
    wallpaperDim = new Image(skin.getDrawable("wallpaper-dim"));
    wallpaperDim.setTouchable(Touchable.disabled);
    stage.addActor(wallpaperDim);
    build();
    windows = new PinuxNativeWindows(stage, skin, shell);
    setVisible(false);
    resize(Gdx.graphics.getWidth(), Gdx.graphics.getHeight());
  }

  private BitmapFont font(String path, float amount) {
    BitmapFont f = new BitmapFont(Gdx.files.internal(path));
    f.getData().setScale(amount);
    f.setUseIntegerPositions(false);
    f.getRegion().getTexture().setFilter(Texture.TextureFilter.Linear, Texture.TextureFilter.Linear);
    return f;
  }

  private NinePatchDrawable patch(String id, int r, int red, int green, int blue,
                                  int alpha, int unused) {
    // NinePatch texture created at 3x target radius to keep silhouettes smooth.
    int size = 96, border = 32, radius = Math.min(38, r * 2);
    Pixmap pix = new Pixmap(size, size, Pixmap.Format.RGBA8888);
    for (int y = 0; y < size; y++) {
      float ay = Math.abs(y + .5f - size / 2f) - (size / 2f - radius);
      for (int x = 0; x < size; x++) {
        float ax = Math.abs(x + .5f - size / 2f) - (size / 2f - radius);
        float distance = (float)Math.hypot(Math.max(ax, 0), Math.max(ay, 0))
            + Math.min(0, Math.max(ax, ay)) - radius;
        float a = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, .5f - distance)) * alpha / 255f;
        pix.drawPixel(x, y, (red << 24) | (green << 16) | (blue << 8) | Math.round(a * 255));
      }
    }
    Texture t = new Texture(pix);
    t.setFilter(Texture.TextureFilter.Linear, Texture.TextureFilter.Linear);
    pix.dispose();
    skin.add(id + "-texture", t);
    NinePatch patch = new NinePatch(t, border, border, border, border);
    return new NinePatchDrawable(patch);
  }

  private void addIcon(String name, int r, int g, int b) {
    int n = 128;
    Pixmap pix = new Pixmap(n, n, Pixmap.Format.RGBA8888);
    // Archcraft uses understated monochrome symbols rather than oversized
    // glossy application cards. Leave the icon texture transparent.
    pix.setColor(0xf5f0ffff);
    // Crisp, recognizable native icons; no emoji font fallbacks.
    switch(name) {
      case "code" -> {
        stroke(pix,41,47,29,63,4); stroke(pix,29,63,41,79,4);
        stroke(pix,83,47,95,63,4); stroke(pix,95,63,83,79,4);
        stroke(pix,71,44,54,83,4);
      }
      case "terminal" -> {
        stroke(pix,30,47,49,62,4);stroke(pix,49,62,30,77,4);
        pix.fillRectangle(57,77,33,4);
      }
      case "files" -> {
        stroke(pix,29,53,29,90,5);
        stroke(pix,29,90,99,90,5);
        stroke(pix,99,90,99,53,5);
        stroke(pix,99,53,63,53,5);
        stroke(pix,63,53,55,44,5);
        stroke(pix,55,44,29,44,5);
        stroke(pix,29,44,29,53,5);
      }
      case "mail" -> {
        stroke(pix,28,47,99,47,4); stroke(pix,28,47,63,75,4);
        stroke(pix,99,47,63,75,4); stroke(pix,28,47,28,86,4);
        stroke(pix,99,47,99,86,4); stroke(pix,28,86,99,86,4);
      }
      case "settings" -> {
        for (int rad=21; rad<=25; rad++) pix.drawCircle(64,64,rad);
        for (int rad=7; rad<=9; rad++) pix.drawCircle(64,64,rad);
        for(int i=0;i<8;i++){
          double angle=i*Math.PI/4;
          int ax=64+(int)(24*Math.cos(angle)), ay=64+(int)(24*Math.sin(angle));
          int bx=64+(int)(33*Math.cos(angle)), by=64+(int)(33*Math.sin(angle));
          stroke(pix,ax,ay,bx,by,5);
        }
      }
    }
    Texture t = new Texture(pix);
    t.setFilter(Texture.TextureFilter.Linear, Texture.TextureFilter.Linear);
    pix.dispose();
    skin.add("icon-" + name, t);
  }

  private static void stroke(Pixmap p, int x, int y, int ex, int ey, int size) {
    for (int dx = -size/2; dx <= size/2; dx++)
      for (int dy = -size/2; dy <= size/2; dy++)
        p.drawLine(x+dx, y+dy, ex+dx, ey+dy);
  }

  private Label label(String text, boolean secondary) {
    Label.LabelStyle style = new Label.LabelStyle(
        skin.getFont(secondary ? "small-font" : "ui-font"),
        Color.valueOf(secondary ? "c6bfd8" : "f5efff"));
    Label l = new Label(text, style);
    l.setAlignment(Align.center);
    return l;
  }

  private Image icon(String name) {
    Image img = new Image(new TextureRegionDrawable(new TextureRegion(skin.get("icon-" + name, Texture.class))));
    img.setScaling(Scaling.fit);
    return img;
  }

  private Button button(Runnable action) {
    Button.ButtonStyle style = new Button.ButtonStyle();
    style.up = skin.getDrawable("button-bg");
    style.over = skin.getDrawable("hover-bg");
    style.down = skin.getDrawable("hover-bg");
    Button b = new Button(style);
    b.setProgrammaticChangeEvents(false);
    b.addListener(new ChangeListener() {
      @Override public void changed(ChangeEvent e, Actor actor) {
        if (!b.isChecked()) return;
        b.setChecked(false);
        action.run();
      }
    });
    return b;
  }

  private Button shortcut(String icon, String title, App app) {
    Button.ButtonStyle style = new Button.ButtonStyle();
    style.up = skin.getDrawable("shortcut-idle");
    style.over = skin.getDrawable("shortcut-hover");
    style.down = skin.getDrawable("shortcut-hover");
    Button b = new Button(style);
    b.addListener(new ChangeListener() {
      @Override public void changed(ChangeEvent e, Actor actor) { shell.openApp(app); }
    });
    b.pad(3);
    b.add(this.icon(icon)).size(35,35).center();
    b.row();
    b.add(label(title, true)).minWidth(90).padTop(1).center();
    return b;
  }

  private Button dockButton(String icon, App app) {
    Button.ButtonStyle style = new Button.ButtonStyle();
    style.up = skin.getDrawable("dock-idle");
    style.over = skin.getDrawable("dock-hover");
    style.down = skin.getDrawable("dock-hover");
    Button b = new Button(style);
    b.addListener(new ChangeListener() {
      @Override public void changed(ChangeEvent e, Actor actor) { shell.openApp(app); }
    });
    b.pad(4);
    b.add(this.icon(icon)).size(27,27);
    b.addListener(new com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.utils.ClickListener() {
      @Override public void enter(com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.InputEvent e,
                                  float x, float y, int pointer, Actor fromActor) {
        b.clearActions();
        b.addAction(Actions.scaleTo(1.07f, 1.07f, .13f));
      }
      @Override public void exit(com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.InputEvent e,
                                 float x, float y, int pointer, Actor toActor) {
        b.clearActions();
        b.addAction(Actions.scaleTo(1,1,.15f));
      }
    });
    b.setTransform(true);
    return b;
  }

  private void build() {
    // Archcraft/Openbox-inspired Polybar: compact 30 px, workspace indicators,
    // calm secondary text and a real application launcher on the left.
    topBar = new Table();
    topBar.setBackground(skin.getDrawable("top-bg"));
    topBar.left().padLeft(20).padRight(20);
    Label menuIcon = label("A", false);
    menuIcon.setColor(Color.valueOf("5294E2"));
    topBar.add(menuIcon).width(20).left();
    Label distro = label("pinux", true);
    distro.setColor(Color.valueOf("DBE1E9"));
    topBar.add(distro).padLeft(5).padRight(26).left();
    Label workspaces = label("01    02    03", true);
    workspaces.setColor(Color.valueOf("8492A3"));
    topBar.add(workspaces).padRight(16);
    topBar.add().expandX();
    current = label("Рабочий стол", true);
    current.setColor(Color.valueOf("929BAB"));
    topBar.add(current).padRight(23);
    Label status = label("●", true);
    status.setColor(Color.valueOf("98C379"));
    topBar.add(status).padRight(10);
    Label session = label("ASTRA", true);
    session.setColor(Color.valueOf("8191A4"));
    topBar.add(session).padRight(15);
    clock = label("20:47", true);
    clock.setColor(Color.valueOf("D5DBE4"));
    topBar.add(clock);
    stage.addActor(topBar);

    // Desktop shortcuts remain usable but do not look like mobile app cards.
    shortcuts = new Table();
    shortcuts.top().left();
    shortcuts.add(shortcut("code", "Astra Code", App.CODE)).size(94,79).padBottom(2);
    shortcuts.row();
    shortcuts.add(shortcut("terminal", "Terminal", App.TERMINAL)).size(94,79).padBottom(2);
    shortcuts.row();
    shortcuts.add(shortcut("files", "Files", App.FILES)).size(94,79).padBottom(2);
    shortcuts.row();
    shortcuts.add(shortcut("mail", "TaskMail", App.MAIL)).size(94,79);
    stage.addActor(shortcuts);

    // Small centered dock: icons have no coloured square behind them.
    dock = new Table();
    dock.setBackground(skin.getDrawable("dock-bg"));
    dock.pad(6, 10, 6, 10);
    dock.defaults().padLeft(5).padRight(5);
    dock.add(dockButton("code", App.CODE)).size(40);
    dock.add(dockButton("terminal", App.TERMINAL)).size(40);
    dock.add(dockButton("files", App.FILES)).size(40);
    dock.add(dockButton("mail", App.MAIL)).size(40);
    dock.add(dockButton("settings", App.SETTINGS)).size(40);
    stage.addActor(dock);

    launcher = new Table();
    launcher.top().left();
    launcher.setBackground(skin.getDrawable("launcher-bg"));
    launcher.pad(16);
    Label heading = label("ПРИЛОЖЕНИЯ", true);
    heading.setColor(Color.valueOf("9FAAB8"));
    launcher.add(heading).width(255).height(28).padBottom(11).left();
    launcher.row();
    for (var entry : new Object[][] {
        {"code", "Astra Code", App.CODE},
        {"terminal", "Terminal", App.TERMINAL},
        {"files", "Files", App.FILES},
        {"mail", "TaskMail", App.MAIL},
        {"settings", "Settings", App.SETTINGS}
    }) {
      String ico = (String)entry[0], title = (String)entry[1];
      App app = (App)entry[2];
      Button b = button(() -> { launcher.setVisible(false); shell.openApp(app); });
      b.add(icon(ico)).size(22).pad(5);
      Label txt = label(title, true); txt.setAlignment(Align.left);
      b.add(txt).width(203).left().padLeft(8);
      launcher.add(b).width(267).height(40).padBottom(4);
      launcher.row();
    }
    launcher.setVisible(false);
    stage.addActor(launcher);
    topBar.addListener(new com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.utils.ClickListener() {
      @Override public void clicked(com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.InputEvent e, float x, float y) {
        if (x <= 152) {
          boolean show = !launcher.isVisible();
          launcher.setVisible(show);
          launcher.toFront();
        }
      }
    });
  }

  public Stage inputStage() { return stage; }
  public boolean isVisible() { return visible; }

  public void openNativeWindow(App app) {
    windows.open(app);
    current.setText(switch (app) {
      case TERMINAL -> "Terminal";
      case FILES -> "Files";
      case MAIL -> "TaskMail";
      case SETTINGS -> "Settings";
      case CODE -> "Astra Code";
    });
    raiseChrome();
  }

  public void raiseChrome() {
    topBar.toFront();
    dock.toFront();
    launcher.toFront();
  }

  public void setDesktopVisible(boolean showDesktop) {
    wallpaper.setVisible(showDesktop);
    wallpaperDim.setVisible(showDesktop);
    // Desktop launchers remain behind open applications but are not visually
    // duplicated inside maximized application workspaces.
    shortcuts.setVisible(showDesktop && !windows.hasOpen());
  }

  public boolean hasNativeWindows() { return windows.hasOpen(); }

  public boolean isCodeFront() { return windows.isFrontCode(); }

  public void cycleNativeWindows() { windows.cycleFocus(); }

  public void closeFrontNativeWindow() { windows.closeFront(); }

  public void resetNativeWindows() { windows.reset(); }

  public void setVisible(boolean show) {
    visible = show;
    stage.getRoot().setVisible(show);
    if (!show && launcher != null) launcher.setVisible(false);
  }

  public void resize(int w, int h) {
    stage.getViewport().update(w, h, true);
    float width = stage.getWidth(), height = stage.getHeight();
    scale = Math.max(.67f, Math.min(1.35f, height / 810f));
    skin.getFont("ui-font").getData().setScale(.37f * scale);
    skin.getFont("small-font").getData().setScale(.39f * scale);
    wallpaper.setBounds(0, 0, width, height);
    wallpaperDim.setBounds(0, 0, width, height);
    topBar.setBounds(0, height - 30 * scale, width, 30 * scale);
    topBar.padLeft(20 * scale);
    topBar.padRight(20 * scale);
    shortcuts.setTransform(true);
    shortcuts.setSize(94, 334);
    shortcuts.setScale(scale);
    shortcuts.setPosition(22 * scale, Math.max(74 * scale, height - 385 * scale));
    dock.invalidateHierarchy();
    dock.setTransform(true);
    dock.setSize(275, 50);
    dock.setScale(scale);
    dock.setPosition((width - 275 * scale) / 2, 14 * scale);
    launcher.setTransform(true);
    launcher.setSize(302, 325);
    launcher.setScale(scale);
    launcher.setPosition(18 * scale, height - 30 * scale - 335 * scale);
    launcher.invalidateHierarchy();
    if (windows != null) windows.resize(w, h);
  }

  public void show() {
    if (windows != null) windows.reset();
    setVisible(true);
    stage.getRoot().setColor(1,1,1,0);
    stage.getRoot().addAction(Actions.fadeIn(.22f));
  }

  public void draw(float delta) {
    if (!visible) return;
    clock.setText(shell.clockText());
    windows.tick();
    stage.act(Math.min(delta, .05f));
    stage.draw();
  }

  public void dispose() {
    stage.dispose();
    skin.dispose();
  }
}
END FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/ui/PinuxDesktopStage.java

FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/ui/PinuxNativeWindows.java

package life.ctrl.ui;

import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.Color;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Input;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.Actor;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.InputEvent;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.InputListener;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.Stage;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.actions.Actions;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.ui.Button;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.ui.Label;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.ui.ScrollPane;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.ui.Skin;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.ui.Table;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.ui.TextButton;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.ui.Window;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.utils.ChangeListener;
import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.Align;
import com.kotcrab.vis.ui.VisUI;
import com.kotcrab.vis.ui.widget.VisTextField;
import com.kotcrab.vis.ui.widget.VisWindow;
import java.util.EnumMap;
import life.ctrl.ui.PinuxWindowManager.App;

/**
 * Native-resolution windows using actual VisUI windows (not manually painted fake
 * rectangles). Layout and resizing belong to Scene2D. App state is simulated and
 * can never touch the host computer's filesystem or execute host commands.
 *
 * The architecture follows familiar KDE-style desktop patterns: one window per
 * application, z-order on focus, a task switcher/dock and independent contents.
 */
final class PinuxNativeWindows {
  private static final Color PRIMARY = Color.valueOf("e8eaf5");
  private static final Color MUTED = Color.valueOf("a9b5cd");
  private static final Color ACCENT = Color.valueOf("b8acff");
  // Archcraft bspwm/OneDark chroma, adapted rather than copying Linux UI files.
  private static final Color TERM_FG = Color.valueOf("ABB2BF");
  private static final Color TERM_MUTED = Color.valueOf("7F8998");
  private static final Color TERM_BLUE = Color.valueOf("5294E2");
  private static final Color TERM_GREEN = Color.valueOf("98C379");

  private final Stage stage;
  private final Skin skin;
  private final PinuxAstra shell;
  private final EnumMap<App, VisWindow> windows = new EnumMap<>(App.class);
  private final EnumMap<App, Boolean> maximized = new EnumMap<>(App.class);
  private final EnumMap<App, float[]> restored = new EnumMap<>(App.class);
  private final Window.WindowStyle windowStyle;
  private final Window.WindowStyle terminalWindowStyle;
  private VisTextField terminalInput;
  private AstraCodeApp codeApp;
  private Label terminalOutput;
  private float scale = 1;
  private int sequence;

  PinuxNativeWindows(Stage stage, Skin skin, PinuxAstra shell) {
    this.stage = stage;
    this.skin = skin;
    this.shell = shell;
    if (!VisUI.isLoaded()) VisUI.load();
    windowStyle = new Window.WindowStyle(
        skin.getFont("ui-font"), PRIMARY, skin.getDrawable("window-bg"));
    terminalWindowStyle = new Window.WindowStyle(
        skin.getFont("ui-font"), TERM_FG, skin.getDrawable("term-frame"));
  }

  private Label text(String value, boolean secondary) {
    Label result = new Label(value, new Label.LabelStyle(
        skin.getFont(secondary ? "small-font" : "ui-font"), secondary ? MUTED : PRIMARY));
    result.setAlignment(Align.left);
    return result;
  }

  private void heading(Table table, String overline, String title, String subtitle) {
    Label over = text(overline, true);
    over.setColor(ACCENT);
    table.add(over).left().padBottom(9);
    table.row();
    table.add(text(title, false)).left().padBottom(5);
    table.row();
    table.add(text(subtitle, true)).left().padBottom(22);
    table.row();
  }

  private TextButton action(String text, Runnable run) {
    TextButton.TextButtonStyle style = new TextButton.TextButtonStyle();
    style.font = skin.getFont("small-font");
    style.fontColor = PRIMARY;
    style.overFontColor = Color.WHITE;
    style.up = skin.getDrawable("button-bg");
    style.over = skin.getDrawable("hover-bg");
    style.down = skin.getDrawable("hover-bg");
    TextButton button = new TextButton(text, style);
    button.addListener(new ChangeListener() {
      @Override public void changed(ChangeEvent event, Actor actor) { run.run(); }
    });
    return button;
  }

  private TextButton chrome(String glyph, Runnable run, boolean danger, boolean terminal) {
    TextButton.TextButtonStyle style = new TextButton.TextButtonStyle();
    style.font = skin.getFont("small-font");
    style.fontColor = terminal ? TERM_MUTED
        : danger ? Color.valueOf("e6bac4") : Color.valueOf("acb9ce");
    style.overFontColor = Color.WHITE;
    style.up = skin.getDrawable(terminal ? "term-title-button" : "title-button");
    style.over = skin.getDrawable(terminal
        ? danger ? "term-title-close" : "term-title-hover"
        : danger ? "title-close-hover" : "title-button-hover");
    style.down = style.over;
    TextButton b = new TextButton(glyph, style);
    b.addListener(new ChangeListener() {
      @Override public void changed(ChangeEvent event, Actor actor) { run.run(); }
    });
    return b;
  }

  private VisWindow make(App app, String title, int width, int height, int x, int y) {
    boolean terminal = app == App.TERMINAL;
    VisWindow window = new VisWindow(title, terminal ? terminalWindowStyle : windowStyle);
    window.setMovable(true);
    window.setResizable(true);
    window.setResizeBorder(12);
    window.setKeepWithinStage(true);
    window.padTop(terminal ? 41 : 44);
    window.padLeft(terminal ? 10 : 19);
    window.padRight(terminal ? 10 : 19);
    window.padBottom(terminal ? 10 : 18);
    window.getTitleLabel().setColor(terminal ? TERM_FG : PRIMARY);
    window.getTitleLabel().setAlignment(Align.left);
    window.getTitleTable().setBackground(skin.getDrawable(
        terminal ? "term-header" : "window-header"));
    window.getTitleTable().add().expandX();
    window.getTitleTable().add(chrome("—", () -> minimize(app), false, terminal))
        .width(terminal ? 30 : 33).height(25).padRight(5);
    window.getTitleTable().add(chrome("[]", () -> toggleMaximize(app), false, terminal))
        .width(terminal ? 30 : 33).height(25).padRight(5);
    window.getTitleTable().add(chrome("×", () -> close(app), true, terminal))
        .width(terminal ? 30 : 33).height(25).padRight(5);
    window.setBounds(x, y, width, height);
    // Explicit resize capture fixes a libGDX/VisWindow corner hit-test conflict
    // when the content Table fills the body. The rest of the window stays native.
    window.addCaptureListener(new InputListener() {
      float startStageX, startStageY, initialX, initialY, initialW, initialH;
      boolean resizing;

      @Override public boolean touchDown(InputEvent e, float localX, float localY,
                                         int pointer, int button) {
        if (button != Input.Buttons.LEFT || localX < window.getWidth() - 24 || localY > 24)
          return false;
        resizing = true;
        startStageX = e.getStageX();
        startStageY = e.getStageY();
        initialX = window.getX();
        initialY = window.getY();
        initialW = window.getWidth();
        initialH = window.getHeight();
        e.stop();
        return true;
      }

      @Override public void touchDragged(InputEvent e, float localX, float localY, int pointer) {
        if (!resizing) return;
        float dx = e.getStageX() - startStageX;
        float dy = e.getStageY() - startStageY;
        float newW = Math.max(app == App.CODE ? 800 : 460,
            Math.min(stage.getWidth() - initialX - 10, initialW + dx));
        float desiredY = Math.max(95, initialY + dy);
        float newH = Math.max(app == App.CODE ? 470 : 300,
            initialH + initialY - desiredY);
        window.setBounds(initialX, desiredY, newW, newH);
        window.invalidateHierarchy();
      }

      @Override public void touchUp(InputEvent e, float localX, float localY,
                                    int pointer, int button) {
        resizing = false;
      }
    });
    windows.put(app, window);
    maximized.put(app, false);
    return window;
  }

  private VisWindow create(App app) {
    int offset = sequence++ % 4;
    int x = 195 + offset * 30, y = 122 + offset * 14;
    switch (app) {
      case CODE -> {
        // All gameplay panels are native Scene2D actors; no PixelCanvas fallback.
        int width = Math.max(800, Math.min(1180, (int)stage.getWidth() - 80));
        int height = Math.max(470, Math.min(720, (int)stage.getHeight() - 112));
        VisWindow w = make(app, "Astra Code", width, height, 50,
            Math.max(45, ((int)stage.getHeight() - height) / 2));
        codeApp = new AstraCodeApp(shell.game(), shell.life(), skin);
        w.add(codeApp.view()).grow().minWidth(0).minHeight(0);
        return w;
      }
      case TERMINAL -> {
        // Archcraft/OneDark: a quiet, flat kitty-style console with a real
        // Scene2D text field. Only this application receives the new skin.
        VisWindow w = make(app, "Terminal", 838, 537, 195, 130);
        Table surface = new Table();
        surface.top().left();
        surface.setBackground(skin.getDrawable("term-surface"));

        // A single quiet tab strip, as in Archcraft's terminal layouts.
        Table toolbar = new Table();
        toolbar.left().padLeft(15).padRight(18);
        toolbar.setBackground(skin.getDrawable("term-toolbar"));
        Table shellTab = new Table();
        shellTab.setBackground(skin.getDrawable("term-tab"));
        Label dot = text("●", true);
        dot.setColor(TERM_GREEN);
        shellTab.add(dot).padLeft(14).padRight(9);
        Label activeSession = text("shell  /  dev@astra", true);
        activeSession.setColor(TERM_FG);
        shellTab.add(activeSession).padRight(15);
        toolbar.add(shellTab).height(30).left();
        toolbar.add().expandX();
        Label workingDirectory = text("~ / projects", true);
        workingDirectory.setColor(TERM_MUTED);
        toolbar.add(workingDirectory).padRight(14);
        Label indicator = text("●", true);
        indicator.setColor(TERM_GREEN);
        toolbar.add(indicator);
        surface.add(toolbar).height(44).growX();
        surface.row();

        terminalOutput = text(shell.terminalHistory(), false);
        terminalOutput.setStyle(new Label.LabelStyle(skin.getFont("small-font"), TERM_FG));
        terminalOutput.setWrap(true);
        terminalOutput.setAlignment(Align.topLeft);
        ScrollPane.ScrollPaneStyle scrollStyle = new ScrollPane.ScrollPaneStyle();
        ScrollPane scroll = new ScrollPane(terminalOutput, scrollStyle);
        scroll.setFadeScrollBars(true);
        scroll.setOverscroll(false, false);
        scroll.setScrollingDisabled(true, false);
        surface.add(scroll).grow().padLeft(26).padRight(23).padTop(12).padBottom(16);
        surface.row();

        Table prompt = new Table();
        prompt.left().padLeft(18).padRight(11);
        prompt.setBackground(skin.getDrawable("term-prompt"));
        Label user = text("dev", true);
        user.setColor(TERM_GREEN);
        prompt.add(user);
        Label atHost = text("@astra", true);
        atHost.setColor(TERM_BLUE);
        prompt.add(atHost);
        Label directory = text("  ~  >", true);
        directory.setColor(TERM_FG);
        prompt.add(directory).padRight(13);

        VisTextField.VisTextFieldStyle inputStyle = new VisTextField.VisTextFieldStyle();
        inputStyle.font = skin.getFont("small-font");
        inputStyle.fontColor = TERM_FG;
        inputStyle.messageFontColor = TERM_MUTED;
        inputStyle.cursor = skin.newDrawable("term-title-button", TERM_BLUE);
        inputStyle.selection = skin.newDrawable("term-title-hover", TERM_BLUE);
        terminalInput = new VisTextField("", inputStyle);
        terminalInput.setMessageText("Type a command...");
        terminalInput.setEnterKeyFocusTraversal(false);
        terminalInput.setTextFieldListener((field, ch) -> {
          if (ch == '\r' || ch == '\n') {
            terminalOutput.setText(shell.terminalSubmit(field.getText()));
            field.setText("");
            scroll.layout();
            scroll.setScrollPercentY(1);
          }
        });
        prompt.add(terminalInput).growX().height(37).padRight(8);
        surface.add(prompt).height(48).growX().padLeft(22).padRight(22).padBottom(20);
        w.add(surface).grow();
        return w;
      }
      case FILES -> {
        VisWindow w = make(app, "Files", 705, 505, 668, 142);
        Table content = new Table();
        content.setBackground(skin.getDrawable("pane-bg"));
        content.top().left().pad(21);
        heading(content, "FILES   /   HOME", "Your workspace", "/home/dev/projects");
        for (String entry : new String[] {"▸   Projects", "▸   src", "◇   main.bs", "▤   README.md", "▸   .git"}) {
          content.add(text(entry, false)).height(44).left().growX();
          content.row();
        }
        content.add(action("OPEN IN ASTRA CODE", () -> shell.openApp(App.CODE)))
            .height(44).width(260).left().padTop(12);
        w.add(content).grow();
        return w;
      }
      case MAIL -> {
        VisWindow w = make(app, "TaskMail", 735, 518, 420, 142);
        Table inbox = new Table();
        inbox.setBackground(skin.getDrawable("pane-bg"));
        inbox.top().left().pad(22);
        heading(inbox, "INBOX   /   AVAILABLE CONTRACTS", "Find your next project", "Client requests and freelance jobs");
        String[] names = shell.offerSummaries();
        for (int i = 0; i < Math.min(4, names.length); i++) {
          inbox.add(text("●   " + names[i], false)).left().height(42).growX();
          inbox.row();
        }
        inbox.add(action("VIEW ALL CONTRACTS", shell::openJobBoard))
            .width(270).height(46).left().padTop(14);
        w.add(inbox).grow();
        return w;
      }
      case SETTINGS -> {
        VisWindow w = make(app, "Settings", 635, 455, 594, 160);
        Table pane = new Table();
        pane.setBackground(skin.getDrawable("pane-bg"));
        pane.top().left().pad(23);
        heading(pane, "SYSTEM   /   PERSONALIZATION", "Make it yours", "Display and accessibility");
        pane.add(text("Appearance", false)).left().padBottom(14);
        pane.row();
        pane.add(action("SWITCH DESKTOP THEME", shell::toggleTheme))
            .left().width(280).height(45).padBottom(24);
        pane.row();
        pane.add(text("Motion", false)).left().padBottom(14);
        pane.row();
        pane.add(action("TOGGLE ANIMATIONS", shell::toggleMotion))
            .left().width(280).height(45);
        w.add(pane).grow();
        return w;
      }
      default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException("Native window not implemented for " + app);
    }
  }

  void open(App app) {
    VisWindow w = windows.get(app);
    if (w == null) w = create(app);
    if (w.getStage() == null) stage.addActor(w);
    w.setVisible(true);
    if (app == App.CODE && codeApp != null) codeApp.invalidate();
    if (app != App.TERMINAL) stage.setKeyboardFocus(null);
    w.setTouchable(com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.Touchable.enabled);
    w.toFront();
    w.clearActions();
    w.getColor().a = 0f;
    w.addAction(Actions.fadeIn(.16f));
    if (app == App.TERMINAL && terminalInput != null) {
      terminalOutput.setText(shell.terminalHistory());
      stage.setKeyboardFocus(terminalInput);
    }
  }

  void close(App app) {
    VisWindow w = windows.get(app);
    if (w == null) return;
    stage.unfocus(w);
    w.clearActions();
    w.setTouchable(com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.Touchable.disabled);
    w.addAction(Actions.sequence(Actions.fadeOut(.12f), Actions.visible(false)));
  }

  void minimize(App app) {
    VisWindow w = windows.get(app);
    if (w == null) return;
    w.setVisible(false);
    stage.unfocus(w);
  }

  void closeFront() {
    Actor top = front();
    for (var entry : windows.entrySet())
      if (entry.getValue() == top) { close(entry.getKey()); return; }
  }

  Actor front() {
    Actor result = null;
    int index = -1;
    for (var e : windows.entrySet()) {
      VisWindow w = e.getValue();
      if (w.getStage() == null || !w.isVisible()) continue;
      int i = stage.getRoot().getChildren().indexOf(w, true);
      if (i > index) { index = i; result = w; }
    }
    return result;
  }

  boolean hasOpen() { return front() != null; }

  boolean isFrontCode() { return front() != null && front() == windows.get(App.CODE); }

  void tick() { if (codeApp != null && isVisible(App.CODE)) codeApp.tick(); }

  void cycleFocus() {
    Actor current = front();
    VisWindow candidate = null;
    int lowest = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
    for (VisWindow w : windows.values()) {
      if (w.getStage() != null && w.isVisible() && w != current) {
        int index = stage.getRoot().getChildren().indexOf(w, true);
        if (index < lowest) { lowest = index; candidate = w; }
      }
    }
    if (candidate != null) candidate.toFront();
  }

  boolean isVisible(App app) {
    VisWindow w = windows.get(app);
    return w != null && w.isVisible();
  }

  void toggleMaximize(App app) {
    VisWindow w = windows.get(app);
    if (w == null) return;
    boolean isMax = maximized.getOrDefault(app, false);
    if (!isMax) {
      restored.put(app, new float[] {w.getX(), w.getY(), w.getWidth(), w.getHeight()});
      float h = stage.getHeight(), width = stage.getWidth();
      w.setBounds(24, 102, width - 48, h - 174);
    } else {
      float[] p = restored.get(app);
      if (p != null) w.setBounds(p[0], p[1], p[2], p[3]);
    }
    maximized.put(app, !isMax);
    w.invalidateHierarchy();
  }

  void resize(int width, int height) {
    for (VisWindow w : windows.values()) {
      w.setPosition(
          Math.max(2, Math.min(w.getX(), width - w.getWidth() - 8)),
          Math.max(95, Math.min(w.getY(), height - w.getHeight() - 52)));
    }
  }

  void reset() {
    for (App app : windows.keySet()) {
      VisWindow w = windows.get(app);
      w.clearActions();
      w.setVisible(false);
      w.setColor(1, 1, 1, 1);
    }
  }
}

package life.ctrl.ui;

import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.Pixmap;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.Texture;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.SpriteBatch;
import java.util.Random;

/**
 * A self-contained, deterministic Pinux desktop wallpaper. Native libGDX texture,
 * generated once on the rendering thread; no downloaded art or web view.
 *
 * 960x540 source is deliberately 2x the logical 480x270 game canvas so silhouettes
 * and light gradients survive high-DPI scaling while keeping the image inexpensive.
 */
public final class PinuxWallpaper {
  private static final int W = 960, H = 540;
  private Texture city;
  private final Random random = new Random(0xA57A21);

  public Texture texture() {
    if (city == null) create();
    return city;
  }

  public void draw(SpriteBatch batch, boolean duskTheme) {
    if (city == null) create();
    batch.setColor(1f, duskTheme ? .89f : 1f, duskTheme ? .79f : 1f, 1f);
    batch.draw(city, 0, 0, 480, 270);
    batch.setColor(1f, 1f, 1f, 1f);
  }

  private void create() {
    Pixmap p = new Pixmap(W, H, Pixmap.Format.RGBA8888);
    paintSky(p);
    paintClouds(p);
    paintSun(p);
    // Quiet skyline kept to the lower half: room for icons and application windows.
    // Three intentionally sparse parallax layers instead of a wall of identical towers.
    paintCity(p, 410, 33, 31, 16, 0x67436e, 0x533862, .17f);
    paintCity(p, 469, 47, 63, 22, 0x332f59, 0x252742, .16f);
    paintCity(p, 513, 68, 49, 16, 0x1b2640, 0x171e35, .13f);
    paintForeground(p);
    city = new Texture(p);
    city.setFilter(Texture.TextureFilter.Linear, Texture.TextureFilter.Linear);
    p.dispose();
  }

  private void paintSky(Pixmap p) {
    final float[][] stops = {
      {0f, 12, 19, 49},
      {.26f, 54, 37, 89},
      {.49f, 117, 66, 136},
      {.63f, 215, 105, 146},
      {.76f, 173, 86, 137},
      {1f, 35, 39, 79}
    };
    for (int y = 0; y < H; y++) {
      float t = y / (float)(H - 1);
      int band = 0;
      while (band < stops.length - 2 && t > stops[band + 1][0]) band++;
      float[] a = stops[band], b = stops[band + 1];
      float v = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (t - a[0]) / (b[0] - a[0])));
      v = v * v * (3 - 2 * v);
      for (int x = 0; x < W; x++) {
        float dist = (float) Math.sqrt(
            Math.pow((x - 730) / 320f, 2) + Math.pow((y - 288) / 155f, 2));
        float glow = Math.max(0, 1f - dist);
        glow = glow * glow * .24f;
        float grain = (((x * 17 + y * 29) % 19) - 9) * .095f;
        int r = (int) (mix(a[1], b[1], v) * (1 - glow) + 250 * glow + grain);
        int g = (int) (mix(a[2], b[2], v) * (1 - glow) + 153 * glow + grain);
        int blue = (int) (mix(a[3], b[3], v) * (1 - glow) + 150 * glow + grain);
        p.drawPixel(x, y, rgba(r, g, blue, 255));
      }
    }
  }

  private void paintClouds(Pixmap p) {
    // Painterly wisps (broad, translucent and slightly curved), never random scanlines.
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
      int centerX = 120 + random.nextInt(800);
      int centerY = 48 + random.nextInt(210);
      int span = 65 + random.nextInt(145);
      int tint = centerY > 145 ? 0xdf83ab : 0x66509b;
      int rr = (tint >> 16) & 255, gg = (tint >> 8) & 255, bb = tint & 255;
      for (int dx = -span; dx <= span; dx++) {
        float along = Math.abs(dx) / (float)span;
        float edge = Math.max(0f, 1f - along * along);
        int y0 = centerY + (int)(3 * Math.sin(dx / 42.0 + i));
        int x = centerX + dx;
        if (x < 0 || x >= W) continue;
        for (int dy = -3; dy <= 3; dy++) {
          int y = y0 + dy;
          if (y < 0 || y >= H) continue;
          float falloff = (1f - Math.abs(dy) / 4f) * edge;
          float alpha = falloff * .12f;
          p.drawPixel(x, y, blend(p.getPixel(x, y), rgba(rr, gg, bb, 255), alpha));
        }
      }
    }
  }

  private void paintSun(Pixmap p) {
    int cx = 745, cy = 311, radius = 46;
    for (int y = cy - radius; y <= cy + radius; y++) {
      for (int x = cx - radius; x <= cx + radius; x++) {
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= W || y >= H) continue;
        float dist = (float)Math.hypot(x - cx, y - cy);
        if (dist >= radius) continue;
        float t = (y - (cy - radius)) / (radius * 2f);
        int r = 255, g = (int)mix(200, 132, t), b = (int)mix(182, 171, t);
        // Continuous glowing disk: deliberately avoid dark scanlines that read as bugs.
        float rim = Math.min(1f, (radius - dist) / 3.5f);
        p.drawPixel(x, y, blend(p.getPixel(x, y), rgba(r, g, b, 255), rim * .86f));
      }
    }
  }

  private void paintCity(Pixmap p, int horizon, int minWidth, int maxHeight, int variance,
                         int topColor, int bottomColor, float windows) {
    int x = -24;
    while (x < W) {
      int width = minWidth + 9 + random.nextInt(minWidth + 25);
      int roof = horizon - (10 + random.nextInt(maxHeight) + random.nextInt(variance));
      // Leave a glowing pocket around the setting sun.
      if (x < 810 && x + width > 665 && horizon < 500) roof += 19;
      roof = Math.max(230, Math.min(horizon - 12, roof));
      int r1 = (topColor >>> 16) & 255, g1 = (topColor >>> 8) & 255, b1 = topColor & 255;
      int r2 = (bottomColor >>> 16) & 255, g2 = (bottomColor >>> 8) & 255, b2 = bottomColor & 255;
      int colorRoof = rgba(r1, g1, b1, 255);
      // Small architectural variations: setback roofs, chimneys, narrow towers.
      int type = random.nextInt(5);
      p.setColor(colorRoof);
      if (type == 0) {
        p.fillRectangle(x + 7, roof - 7, Math.max(8, width - 14), 7);
      } else if (type == 1) {
        p.fillRectangle(x + width / 2, roof - 12, 2, 12);
        p.fillRectangle(x + width / 2 - 2, roof - 2, 6, 2);
      } else if (type == 2) {
        p.fillRectangle(x + 5, roof - 4, Math.max(9, width / 3), 4);
      }
      for (int yy = roof; yy <= H - 30; yy++) {
        float t = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, (yy - roof) / (float)(H - roof)));
        // Slightly lighter left façade gives buildings actual depth.
        int col = rgba((int)mix(r1, r2, t), (int)mix(g1, g2, t),
            (int)mix(b1, b2, t), 255);
        p.setColor(col);
        p.fillRectangle(x, yy, width, 1);
      }
      p.setColor(rgba(Math.min(255, r1 + 11), Math.min(255, g1 + 7),
          Math.min(255, b1 + 12), 255));
      p.fillRectangle(x, roof + 2, 2, H - roof - 35);
      int windowStep = minWidth >= 65 ? 14 : minWidth >= 47 ? 12 : 10;
      for (int wy = roof + 13; wy < H - 34; wy += 14 + random.nextInt(4)) {
        for (int wx = x + 9; wx < x + width - 6; wx += windowStep) {
          if (wx < 1 || wx + 2 >= W || random.nextFloat() > windows) continue;
          int col = random.nextBoolean()
              ? rgba(242, 165, 152, 255) : rgba(178, 135, 192, 255);
          p.setColor(col);
          p.fillRectangle(wx, wy, 2, 4);
        }
      }
      x += width + 3 + random.nextInt(10);
    }
  }

  private void paintForeground(Pixmap p) {
    // Dark architectural framing and layered near-field foliage. Visible only in
    // the peripheral wallpaper so it does not compete with application windows.
    p.setColor(rgba(13, 20, 39, 255));
    p.fillRectangle(0, 480, W, 60);
    p.setColor(rgba(79, 62, 102, 255));
    p.fillRectangle(0, 480, W, 3);
    for (int i = 0; i < 18; i++) {
      int x = i * 58;
      int y = 482 + random.nextInt(27);
      p.setColor(rgba(28 + i % 3 * 2, 28, 51, 255));
      p.fillRectangle(x, y, 1 + random.nextInt(3), H - y);
    }
    p.setColor(rgba(17, 21, 42, 255));
    p.fillRectangle(0, 0, 17, H);
    p.fillRectangle(W - 18, 0, 18, H);

    // Keep the sky uncluttered: no cartoon tree blobs in the upper corners.
    p.setColor(rgba(15, 20, 39, 255));
    p.fillRectangle(0, 475, W, 9);
  }

  private void drawBranch(Pixmap p, int x, int y, int length, int direction, int depth) {
    int sign = direction == 0 ? 1 : -1;
    p.setColor(rgba(19, 20, 40, 255));
    for (int i = 0; i < length; i++) {
      int xx = x + sign * i / 3;
      int yy = y - i;
      if (xx < 18 || xx >= W - 18 || yy < 15) break;
      p.fillRectangle(xx, yy, Math.max(1, 6 - i / 33), 3);
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < 11 + depth; i++) {
      int bx = x + sign * (12 + random.nextInt(85));
      int by = y - 20 - random.nextInt(length - 30);
      p.setColor(rgba(22 + i % 3 * 6, 24, 49 + i % 3 * 6, 255));
      p.fillCircle(bx, by, 5 + random.nextInt(12));
    }
  }

  private static float mix(float a, float b, float t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }

  private static int rgba(int r, int g, int b, int a) {
    return (clamp(r) << 24) | (clamp(g) << 16) | (clamp(b) << 8) | clamp(a);
  }

  private static int clamp(int v) { return Math.max(0, Math.min(255, v)); }

  private static int blend(int base, int overlay, float alpha) {
    float o = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, alpha));
    return rgba(
        (int)mix((base >>> 24) & 255, (overlay >>> 24) & 255, o),
        (int)mix((base >>> 16) & 255, (overlay >>> 16) & 255, o),
        (int)mix((base >>> 8) & 255, (overlay >>> 8) & 255, o), 255);
  }

  public void dispose() {
    if (city != null) {
      city.dispose();
      city = null;
    }
  }
}
END FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/ui/PinuxWallpaper.java

FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/ui/PinuxWindowManager.java

package life.ctrl.ui;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.EnumMap;
import java.util.List;
import life.ctrl.render.PixelCanvas;

/**
 * Windowing rules for the fictional Pinux Astra desktop. Coordinates use the
 * 480x270 logical canvas, not the host OS pixel coordinates.
 */
public final class PinuxWindowManager {
  public enum App { CODE, TERMINAL, FILES, MAIL, SETTINGS }
  public static final int TOP = 17, BOTTOM = 250;

  public static final class Window {
    public final App app;
    public final String title;
    public int x, y, width, height;
    public int restoreX, restoreY, restoreWidth, restoreHeight;
    public boolean visible, minimized, maximized;
    public float animation;
    private Window(App app, String title, int x, int y, int width, int height) {
      this.app = app;
      this.title = title;
      this.x = x; this.y = y; this.width = width; this.height = height;
      restoreX = x; restoreY = y; restoreWidth = width; restoreHeight = height;
    }
    public int right() { return x + width; }
    public int bottom() { return y + height; }
    public int contentY() { return y + 20; }
    public int contentHeight() { return height - 23; }
  }

  @FunctionalInterface
  public interface ContentPainter { void paint(Window window, boolean active); }

  private final EnumMap<App, Window> windows = new EnumMap<>(App.class);
  private final ArrayList<Window> zOrder = new ArrayList<>();
  private Window dragging, resizing;
  private int grabX, grabY, dragStartX, dragStartY;
  private int resizeWidth, resizeHeight;
  private float time;
  private long lastClickAt;
  private App lastClickApp;
  private boolean moved;
  private int hoverX = -1, hoverY = -1;
  private static final String EDGE = "4e7087", TEXT = "dceaf4", ACCENT = "8dcce0";

  public PinuxWindowManager() {
    add(App.CODE, "Astra Code  |  main.bs", 28, 27, 420, 202);
    add(App.TERMINAL, "Terminal  |  dev@astra", 99, 40, 353, 181);
    add(App.FILES, "Files  |  /home/dev", 49, 34, 370, 193);
    add(App.MAIL, "TaskMail  |  inbox", 74, 35, 352, 194);
    add(App.SETTINGS, "Settings  |  Appearance", 115, 48, 296, 176);
  }

  private void add(App app, String title, int x, int y, int w, int h) {
    windows.put(app, new Window(app, title, x, y, w, h));
  }

  public Window get(App app) { return windows.get(app); }
  public int openCount() { return (int) zOrder.stream().filter(w -> !w.minimized && w.visible).count(); }

  public Window front() {
    for (int i = zOrder.size() - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      Window w = zOrder.get(i);
      if (w.visible && !w.minimized) return w;
    }
    return null;
  }

  public boolean isFront(App app) { Window w = front(); return w != null && w.app == app; }
  public boolean isMaximizedCode() {
    Window w = front();
    return w != null && w.app == App.CODE && w.maximized;
  }

  public void open(App app) {
    Window w = get(app);
    if (!w.visible) {
      w.animation = 0;
      w.visible = true;
    }
    w.minimized = false;
    focus(app);
  }

  public void focus(App app) {
    Window w = get(app);
    if (w == null || !w.visible) return;
    w.minimized = false;
    zOrder.remove(w);
    zOrder.add(w);
  }

  public void minimize(App app) {
    Window w = get(app);
    if (w == null) return;
    w.minimized = true;
    if (dragging == w) dragging = null;
    if (resizing == w) resizing = null;
  }

  public void close(App app) {
    Window w = get(app);
    if (w == null) return;
    w.visible = false;
    w.minimized = false;
    // Let the window shrink away instead of disappearing in a single frame.
    if (dragging == w) dragging = null;
    if (resizing == w) resizing = null;
  }

  public void closeFront() { Window w = front(); if (w != null) close(w.app); }

  public void toggleMaximize(App app) {
    Window w = get(app);
    if (w == null) return;
    if (!w.maximized) {
      w.restoreX = w.x; w.restoreY = w.y;
      w.restoreWidth = w.width; w.restoreHeight = w.height;
      w.x = 10; w.y = TOP + 1; w.width = 460; w.height = BOTTOM - TOP - 1;
      w.maximized = true;
    } else {
      w.x = w.restoreX; w.y = w.restoreY;
      w.width = w.restoreWidth; w.height = w.restoreHeight;
      w.maximized = false;
      clamp(w);
    }
  }

  /** Handles window caption, system buttons, focus and dragging. */
  public boolean pointerDown(int x, int y) {
    if (x < 0 || x >= PixelCanvas.W || y < TOP || y >= BOTTOM) return false;
    for (int i = zOrder.size() - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      Window w = zOrder.get(i);
      if (!w.visible || w.minimized || !inside(w, x, y)) continue;
      boolean wasFront = front() == w;
      focus(w.app);
      if (!w.maximized && x >= w.right() - 10 && y >= w.bottom() - 10) {
        resizing = w;
        dragStartX = x; dragStartY = y;
        resizeWidth = w.width; resizeHeight = w.height;
        return true;
      }
      if (y < w.y + 19) {
        // Three caption buttons: minimize, maximize and close.
        if (x >= w.right() - 15) { close(w.app); return true; }
        if (x >= w.right() - 30) { toggleMaximize(w.app); return true; }
        if (x >= w.right() - 45) { minimize(w.app); return true; }
        long now = System.nanoTime();
        if (lastClickApp == w.app && now - lastClickAt < 350_000_000L) {
          toggleMaximize(w.app);
          lastClickAt = 0;
          return true;
        }
        lastClickAt = now;
        lastClickApp = w.app;
        dragging = w;
        moved = false;
        grabX = x - w.x; grabY = y - w.y;
        dragStartX = x; dragStartY = y;
        return true;
      }
      // A lower window consumes the first click to focus it, preventing click-through.
      return !wasFront;
    }
    return false;
  }

  public boolean pointerDragged(int x, int y) {
    if (resizing != null) {
      Window w = resizing;
      w.width = Math.max(210, Math.min(PixelCanvas.W - w.x, resizeWidth + x - dragStartX));
      w.height = Math.max(125, Math.min(BOTTOM - w.y, resizeHeight + y - dragStartY));
      w.restoreWidth = w.width; w.restoreHeight = w.height;
      return true;
    }
    if (dragging == null) return false;
    Window w = dragging;
    if (Math.abs(x - dragStartX) + Math.abs(y - dragStartY) > 3) moved = true;
    if (!moved) return true;
    if (w.maximized) {
      toggleMaximize(w.app);
      grabX = Math.min(w.width - 30, Math.max(18, grabX));
      grabY = 9;
    }
    w.x = x - grabX; w.y = y - grabY;
    clamp(w);
    return true;
  }

  public boolean pointerUp(int x, int y) {
    if (resizing != null) {
      resizing = null;
      return true;
    }
    if (dragging == null) return false;
    Window w = dragging;
    dragging = null;
    if (moved && y <= TOP + 4 && !w.maximized) toggleMaximize(w.app);
    else if (moved && x <= 3 && !w.maximized) snap(w, true);
    else if (moved && x >= PixelCanvas.W - 4 && !w.maximized) snap(w, false);
    if (moved) lastClickAt = 0; // Dragging must not count as a double-click.
    moved = false;
    return true;
  }

  public void cancelDrag() { dragging = null; resizing = null; moved = false; }

  private static void snap(Window w, boolean left) {
    w.restoreX = w.x; w.restoreY = w.y;
    w.restoreWidth = w.width; w.restoreHeight = w.height;
    w.x = left ? 2 : 242; w.y = TOP + 1;
    w.width = 236; w.height = BOTTOM - TOP - 2;
  }

  public void pointerMove(int x, int y) { hoverX = x; hoverY = y; }

  private static boolean inside(Window w, int x, int y) {
    return x >= w.x && x < w.right() && y >= w.y && y < w.bottom();
  }

  private static void clamp(Window w) {
    w.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(PixelCanvas.W - w.width, w.x));
    w.y = Math.max(TOP + 1, Math.min(BOTTOM - w.height, w.y));
  }

  public void tick(float delta) {
    time += delta;
    for (Window w : windows.values()) {
      float target = w.visible && !w.minimized ? 1 : 0;
      float speed = Math.min(1, delta * 13);
      w.animation += (target - w.animation) * speed;
      if (Math.abs(w.animation - target) < .008f) w.animation = target;
    }
    zOrder.removeIf(w -> !w.visible && w.animation == 0);
  }

  public void draw(PixelCanvas c, ContentPainter painter) {
    for (Window w : zOrder) {
      if (w.animation < .03f) continue;
      if (isMaximizedCode() && w.app != App.CODE) continue; // completely covered
      boolean active = front() == w;
      if (isMaximizedCode() && w.app == App.CODE) {
        // Legacy Workbench screens are drawn at the native 480x270 layout.
        painter.paint(w, true);
        drawCaption(c, w, true);
        continue;
      }
      float p = w.animation * w.animation * (3 - 2 * w.animation);
      int inset = Math.round((1 - p) * 13);
      int xx = w.x + inset, yy = w.y + inset;
      int ww = w.width - inset * 2, hh = w.height - inset * 2;
      if (ww < 50 || hh < 30) continue;
      c.rect(xx + 3, yy + 4, ww, hh, "020912a0");
      c.rect(xx, yy, ww, hh, active ? EDGE : "344a5e");
      c.rect(xx + 1, yy + 1, ww - 2, hh - 2, "0e1d2d");
      c.rect(xx + 1, yy + 1, ww - 2, 18, active ? "244359" : "1b2f41");
      if (w.animation > .94f) {
        c.clip(w.x + 2, w.y + 20, w.width - 4, w.height - 22);
        painter.paint(w, active && w.visible && !w.minimized);
        c.unclip();
        drawCaption(c, w, active);
      }
    }
  }

  private void drawCaption(PixelCanvas c, Window w, boolean active) {
    int y = w.y;
    c.rect(w.x, y, w.width, 18, active ? "25475e" : "1b3042");
    c.rect(w.x, y + 17, w.width, 1, active ? "5591a9" : "334a5c");
    c.fitted("◆  " + w.title, w.x + 8, y + 4, w.width - 65,
        active ? TEXT : "9eb3c0", true);
    int start = w.right() - 43;
    if (!w.maximized) {
      c.rect(w.right() - 7, w.bottom() - 4, 5, 1, "6695a8");
      c.rect(w.right() - 4, w.bottom() - 7, 1, 5, "6695a8");
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      int x = start + i * 15;
      boolean hovered = hoverY >= y + 2 && hoverY <= y + 16 &&
          hoverX >= x && hoverX <= x + 12;
      c.rect(x, y + 3, 12, 12, hovered ? (i == 2 ? "b6525d" : "4a7c92")
          : i == 2 ? "704850" : "34576a");
      c.small(i == 0 ? "_" : i == 1 ? "□" : "×", x + 3, y + 2,
          i == 2 ? "ffd5d1" : ACCENT);
    }
  }

  public void reset() {
    zOrder.clear();
    dragging = null;
    resizing = null;
    for (Window w : windows.values()) {
      w.visible = false; w.minimized = false; w.maximized = false; w.animation = 0;
      // Preserve each window's restored size/position across visits to the in-game PC.
      w.x = w.restoreX; w.y = w.restoreY;
      w.width = w.restoreWidth; w.height = w.restoreHeight;
    }
  }

  public List<App> taskbar() {
    return zOrder.stream().filter(w -> w.visible).map(w -> w.app).toList();
  }

  public void cycleFocus() {
    if (zOrder.isEmpty()) return;
    List<Window> candidates = zOrder.stream().filter(w -> w.visible).toList();
    if (candidates.size() < 2) return;
    Window next = candidates.get(Math.max(0, candidates.size() - 2));
    focus(next.app);
  }
}

package life.ctrl.ui;

import java.util.*;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Gdx;
import life.ctrl.CtrlLife;
import life.ctrl.CtrlLife.Mode;
import life.ctrl.bytescript.ByteScript;
import life.ctrl.content.LifeContent;
import life.ctrl.model.*;
import life.ctrl.render.PixelCanvas;

/** An in-world workbench: contracts, experiments, revisions, portfolio and owned tools. */
public final class Workbench {
  private final CtrlLife game;
  private final LifeScreens life;
  private final PixelCanvas c;
  private int boardPage;
  private int portfolioPage;
  private boolean rush;
  private double[] inputs = {0};
  private String labResult = "Нажми «Запустить»: увидишь ответ, эталон и число операций.";
  private int baseline = -1, current = -1;
  private double cachedInput = Double.NaN, previewValue;
  private String cachedSource = "";
  private String visualSource = "", visualJob = "";
  private int visualTypedCount;
  private float typewriterCarry;
  private static final String TEXT = "efe4c6",
      SOFT = "a7b5b6",
      GOLD = "edbb71",
      GREEN = "a5c19c",
      RED = "e49a83";

  public Workbench(CtrlLife game, LifeScreens life, PixelCanvas c) {
    this.game = game;
    this.life = life;
    this.c = c;
  }

  private GameState s() {
    return game.world().state;
  }

  private void button(String label, int x, int y, int w, Runnable f) {
    button(label, x, y, w, true, f);
  }

  private void button(String label, int x, int y, int w, boolean enabled, Runnable f) {
    game.uiButton(label, x, y, w, 22, enabled, f);
  }

  private void small(String label, int x, int y, int w, Runnable f) {
    game.uiButton(label, x, y, w, 17, true, f);
  }

  private void page(int p) {
    life.codePage = p;
    game.resetSelection();
  }

  private void save() {
    game.refreshState();
  }

  private void scene(String title, String text) {
    life.scene(title, text, Mode.IDE);
  }

  private void frame(String title) {
    c.rect(0, 0, 480, 270, "111e2bf2");
    c.panel(12, 10, 456, 250);
    c.rect(16, 14, 448, 38, "203441");
    c.small("KEYCODE  /  " + Career.rank(s()).toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT), 26, 22, GREEN);
    c.text(title, 26, 39, TEXT);
    c.small(s().money + " ₽", 379, 23, GOLD);
    c.rect(16, 254, 448, 2, "527b78");
  }

  private void home() {
    button("К заказам", 28, 231, 206, () -> page(0));
    button(
        "Встать из-за ПК",
        246,
        231,
        206,
        () -> {
          game.switchMode(Mode.WORLD);
          save();
        });
  }

  private void choose(LifeContent.Job j) {
    if (!s().activeJob.isEmpty() && s().jobStage >= 2 && s().jobStage < 5) {
      page(2);
      scene(
          "Текущий проект",
          "Исходник сохранён. Закончи проект или отложи его через Git → «Отложить заказ». Денежного"
              + " штрафа нет.");
      return;
    }
    String reason = Career.blocked(s(), j);
    if (!reason.isEmpty()) {
      scene("Пока недоступно", reason);
      return;
    }
    s().activeJob = j.id;
    page(1);
    rush = false;
    life.report = null;
  }

  public void draw() {
    if (life.codePage == 0) {
      board();
      return;
    }
    if (life.codePage == 7) {
      career();
      return;
    }
    if (life.codePage == 8) {
      gear();
      return;
    }
    if (life.codePage == 9) {
      project();
      return;
    }
    if (life.codePage == 11) {
      portfolio();
      return;
    }
    if (s().activeJob.isEmpty()) {
      page(0);
      board();
      return;
    }
    var j = life.data.job(s().activeJob);
    frame(j.title);
    if (life.codePage == 1) {
      brief(j);
      return;
    }
    if (life.codePage == 3) {
      patches(j);
      return;
    }
    if (life.codePage == 4) {
      preview(j);
      return;
    }
    if (life.codePage == 5) {
      lab(j);
      return;
    }
    if (life.codePage == 6) {
      history();
      return;
    }
    if (life.codePage == 10) {
      c.wrap(
          "Отложить текущий заказ? Его исходник и локальные коммиты будут удалены. Денег и"
              + " репутации ты не потеряешь.",
          28,
          83,
          420,
          TEXT);
      button(
          "Отложить",
          28,
          199,
          206,
          () -> {
            Coding.abandon(s());
            page(0);
            save();
          });
      button("Сохранить проект", 246, 199, 206, () -> page(6));
      return;
    }
    editor(j);
  }

  private void board() {
    frame("Биржа заказов · день " + s().day());
    c.small("СДАНО " + Career.paidCountToday(s()) + " / 3  ·  ОПЫТ " + s().codingXp, 28, 65, GOLD);
    c.wrap(
        s().completedOrders == 0
            ? "Начни с кассы для «Ложки». Читай правило → код → тесты → оплата."
            : "Баги, новые функции и быстрый код. Состав предложений меняется каждый день.",
        28,
        82,
        420,
        SOFT);
    var offers = Career.offers(s(), life.data);
    int pages = Math.max(1, (offers.size() + 2) / 3);
    boardPage = Math.floorMod(boardPage, pages);
    for (int row = 0; row < 3; row++) {
      int index = boardPage * 3 + row;
      if (index >= offers.size()) break;
      var j = offers.get(index);
      String lock = Career.blocked(s(), j);
      button(
          j.title + " · " + j.pay + " ₽", 28, 112 + row * 29, 424, lock.isEmpty(), () -> choose(j));
    }
    small(
        "Лист " + (boardPage + 1) + "/" + pages,
        28,
        201,
        90,
        () -> {
          boardPage++;
          game.resetSelection();
        });
    small("Карьера", 125, 201, 100, () -> page(7));
    small("Свой бот", 232, 201, 100, () -> page(9));
    small("Железо", 339, 201, 113, () -> page(8));
    button(
        "Встать из-за ПК",
        28,
        226,
        424,
        () -> {
          game.switchMode(Mode.WORLD);
          save();
        });
  }

  private void brief(LifeContent.Job j) {
    c.small(j.client, 28, 66, GOLD);
    if (!j.once && s().completedOrders > 0)
      small(rush ? "Срочно: +25%" : "Обычный срок", 302, 61, 150, () -> rush = !rush);
    c.wrap(j.brief, 28, 88, 420, TEXT);
    c.small(
        j.cost > 0
            ? "Запуск: " + j.cost + " ₽ · работа " + Career.duration(s(), j) + " мин"
            : rush
                ? "Бонус, если сдашь за "
                    + (Career.duration(s(), j) + 90)
                    + " мин. Оплата: "
                    + j.pay
                    + " ₽ +25%."
                : "Работа: " + Career.duration(s(), j) + " мин · оплата " + j.pay + " ₽",
        28,
        132,
        SOFT);
    if (j.kind.equals("feature"))
      for (int i = 0; i < j.algorithms.length; i++) {
        int choice = i;
        button(j.algorithms[i], 28, 150 + i * 28, 424, () -> start(j, choice));
      }
    else {
      button(
          j.kind.equals("profile") ? "Открыть код и измерить нагрузку" : "Открыть исходник",
          28,
          150,
          424,
          () -> start(j, 0));
      button("Прочитать сообщение клиента", 28, 180, 424, () -> scene(j.client, j.hint));
      button("К заказам", 28, 226, 424, () -> page(0));
    }
  }

  private void start(LifeContent.Job j, int choice) {
    String result = Coding.accept(s(), j, choice, rush, true);
    if (s().jobStage < 2 || !s().activeJob.equals(j.id)) {
      scene("Проект", result);
      return;
    }
    life.report = null;
    page(2);
    save();
  }

  /**
   * The game-native code editor borrows the familiar VS Code layout while remaining
   * a fictional application in Pinux Astra. The current ByteScript engine stays authoritative.
   */
  private void editor(LifeContent.Job j) {
    c.rect(16, 53, 448, 197, "0a1725");
    c.rect(16, 53, 28, 197, "182c3e");
    c.rect(45, 53, 100, 197, "142538");
    c.rect(145, 53, 319, 118, "0c1928");
    c.rect(145, 172, 319, 55, "0b1723");
    c.rect(145, 226, 319, 24, "20384b");
    c.rect(144, 53, 1, 197, "38516a");
    c.rect(145, 171, 319, 1, "38516a");
    c.small("{}", 22, 70, GREEN);
    c.small("[]", 22, 103, SOFT);
    c.small(">", 25, 136, GOLD);
    c.small("EXPLORER", 52, 63, SOFT);
    c.small("▼  PROJECT", 51, 82, TEXT);
    c.small("   ▼ src", 52, 104, SOFT);
    c.small("     main.bs", 52, 121, GREEN);
    c.small("   README.md", 52, 142, SOFT);
    c.rect(51, 161, 88, 1, "355167");
    c.small("ACTIVE JOB", 52, 171, SOFT);
    c.fitted(j.id, 52, 189, 84, GOLD, true);
    c.small("XP " + s().codingXp, 52, 210, SOFT);
    c.rect(146, 54, 138, 23, "152b40");
    c.rect(146, 76, 138, 1, "82d3e8");
    c.small("◇ main.bs   ●", 156, 59, TEXT);
    c.small("ByteScript  /  UTF-8", 334, 59, SOFT);
    // Input commits progress immediately, while the visible text follows with a smooth typewriter trail.
    if (!visualSource.equals(s().codeSource) || !visualJob.equals(s().activeJob)) {
      visualSource = s().codeSource;
      visualJob = s().activeJob;
      visualTypedCount = 0;
      typewriterCarry = 0;
    }
    int target = Math.min(s().typedCount, s().codeSource.length());
    visualTypedCount = Math.min(visualTypedCount, target);
    typewriterCarry += Math.min(.05f, Gdx.graphics.getDeltaTime()) * 95;
    if (visualTypedCount < target) {
      int reveal = Math.max(1, (int) typewriterCarry);
      visualTypedCount = Math.min(target, visualTypedCount + reveal);
      typewriterCarry = Math.max(0, typewriterCarry - reveal);
    } else typewriterCarry = 0;
    String visible = s().codeSource.substring(0, visualTypedCount);
    String[] visibleLines = visible.split("\n", -1);
    int firstLine = Math.max(0, visibleLines.length - 5);
    c.clip(151, 80, 307, 80);
    for (int i = firstLine; i < visibleLines.length; i++) {
      int row = i - firstLine;
      int y = 83 + row * 15;
      String line = visibleLines[i];
      if (i == visibleLines.length - 1) c.rect(153, y - 2, 301, 14, "172d4055");
      c.small(String.valueOf(i + 1), 155, y, "58758b");
      String codeColor = line.stripLeading().startsWith("fn ") ? "86cfe0" :
          line.stripLeading().startsWith("return ") ? "c8a1e6" : GREEN;
      c.fitted(line, 177, y, 275, codeColor, true);
      if (i == visibleLines.length - 1 && (System.nanoTime() / 400_000_000L) % 2 == 0)
        c.rect(177 + Math.min(273, c.smallWidth(line)), y - 1, 1, 11, "82d3e8");
    }
    c.unclip();
    float progress = s().codeSource.isEmpty() ? 0f :
        s().typedCount / (float) s().codeSource.length();
    c.rect(153, 158, 299, 2, "263e53");
    c.rect(153, 158, 299 * Math.min(1f, progress), 2, GREEN);
    c.small("ПРОБЛЕМЫ   ВЫВОД   ТЕСТЫ", 153, 177, SOFT);
    if (life.report == null) {
      c.fitted(s().typedCount < s().codeSource.length()
          ? "ВВОД: нажимай любые клавиши — исходник печатается."
          : "Черновик готов. Запусти тесты.", 154, 191, 299, SOFT, true);
      c.fitted("Символов: " + s().typedCount + "/" + s().codeSource.length()
          + "   |   " + (s().rushJob ? "СРОЧНЫЙ ЗАКАЗ" : "main"), 154, 208, 299, GOLD, true);
    } else {
      c.fitted((life.report.passed() ? "PASS: " : "FAIL: ") + life.report.message(),
          154, 191, 299, life.report.passed() ? GREEN : RED, true);
      if (!life.report.passed() && s().typedCount >= s().codeSource.length())
        c.fitted("Жми любые клавиши: начинается исправление.", 154, 208, 299, GOLD, true);
      else if (!life.report.cases().isEmpty())
        c.fitted(life.report.cases().get(0), 154, 208, 299, SOFT, true);
    }
    small("Тесты", 151, 229, 65, () -> {
      life.report = Coding.test(s(), j);
      save();
    });
    small(life.report != null && life.report.passed() ? "Превью" : "Отладка", 221, 229, 84, () -> {
      if (s().typedCount < s().codeSource.length()) {
        life.report = Coding.test(s(), j);
      } else if (life.report != null && life.report.passed()) {
        page(4);
        life.previewInput = j.id.equals("discount") ? 500 : j.seed[j.seed.length - 1];
      } else page(3);
    });
    small("Lab", 310, 229, 67, () -> openLab(j));
    small("Git", 382, 229, 75, () -> page(6));
  }

  private void patches(LifeContent.Job j) {
    c.small("CODE REVIEW · ВЫБЕРИ ИЗМЕНЕНИЕ", 28, 66, GOLD);
    c.wrap(
        s().codeSource.contains("prce")
            ? "Имя prce не объявлено. В аргументах есть price и count. Что должно участвовать в"
                + " произведении?"
            : j.hint,
        28,
        88,
        420,
        TEXT);
    for (int i = 0; i < j.patches.length; i++) {
      int choice = i;
      button(
          j.patches[i],
          28,
          150 + i * 28,
          424,
          () -> {
            Coding.repair(s(), j, choice, true);
            life.report = null;
            page(2);
            save();
          });
    }
  }

  private void preview(LifeContent.Job j) {
    c.small("ПРЕДПРОСМОТР · РЕЗУЛЬТАТ ТВОЕГО КОДА", 28, 67, GOLD);
    c.wrap(
        j.id.equals("total")
            ? "Цена блюда 30 ₽. Меняй количество: касса считает твоей функцией."
            : "Меняй "
                + j.function
                + " / "
                + ByteScript.compile(j.source).parameters().get(j.seed.length - 1)
                + ". Остальные аргументы — из первого теста клиента.",
        28,
        91,
        420,
        TEXT);
    if (!s().programPreviewed
        || cachedInput != life.previewInput
        || !cachedSource.equals(s().codeSource)) {
      previewValue = Coding.preview(s(), j, life.previewInput);
      cachedSource = s().codeSource;
      cachedInput = life.previewInput;
    }
    double value = previewValue;
    c.text(
        "Ввод " + Coding.format(life.previewInput) + " → " + Coding.format(value), 28, 134, GREEN);
    button("−", 28, 164, 70, () -> life.previewInput = Math.max(-10000, life.previewInput - 1));
    button("+", 110, 164, 70, () -> life.previewInput = Math.min(10000, life.previewInput + 1));
    c.small(
        "Работа " + Career.duration(s(), j) + " мин · бонус " + Career.bonus(s(), j) + " ₽",
        195,
        170,
        SOFT);
    button(
        j.once
            ? "Запустить этап · −" + j.cost + " ₽"
            : "Сдать клиенту · " + (j.pay + Career.bonus(s(), j)) + " ₽",
        28,
        199,
        424,
        s().jobStage != 5,
        () -> {
          String result = Coding.deliver(s(), j);
          game.successCue();
          life.scene(s().jobStage == 5 ? "Работа принята" : "Проект сохранён", result, Mode.WORLD);
        });
    button("Вернуться к коду", 28, 229, 424, () -> page(2));
  }

  private static String clock(int minute) {
    int v = Math.floorMod(minute, 1440);
    return "%02d:%02d".formatted(v / 60, v % 60);
  }

  private void openLab(LifeContent.Job j) {
    inputs = j.seed.clone();
    labResult = "Меняй входы: ноль, граница, отрицательное число. Потом запускай.";
    measure(j);
    page(5);
  }

  private int operations(String source, LifeContent.Job j) {
    try {
      var p = ByteScript.compile(source);
      int max = 0;
      for (var t : j.tests) max = Math.max(max, p.operations(t.args));
      return max;
    } catch (RuntimeException e) {
      return -1;
    }
  }

  private void measure(LifeContent.Job j) {
    baseline =
        operations(s().revisions.isEmpty() ? s().codeSource : s().revisions.get(0).source, j);
    current = operations(s().codeSource, j);
  }

  private void lab(LifeContent.Job j) {
    c.small("ЛАБОРАТОРИЯ / ПРОФИЛИРОВЩИК", 28, 64, GOLD);
    var params = ByteScript.compile(j.source).parameters();
    for (int i = 0; i < inputs.length; i++) {
      int at = i, y = 86 + i * 27;
      c.text(params.get(i) + " = " + Coding.format(inputs[i]), 28, y, TEXT);
      small("−10", 228, y - 2, 50, () -> inputs[at] = Math.max(-10000, inputs[at] - 10));
      small("−1", 284, y - 2, 50, () -> inputs[at] = Math.max(-10000, inputs[at] - 1));
      small("+1", 340, y - 2, 50, () -> inputs[at] = Math.min(10000, inputs[at] + 1));
      small("+10", 396, y - 2, 56, () -> inputs[at] = Math.min(10000, inputs[at] + 10));
    }
    c.small(
        "Операции на тестах: было "
            + (baseline < 0 ? "—" : baseline)
            + " → сейчас "
            + (current < 0 ? "ошибка кода" : current)
            + (j.operationBudget > 0 ? " / лимит " + j.operationBudget : ""),
        28,
        145,
        SOFT);
    c.rect(28, 160, 180, 3, "344652");
    c.rect(
        28,
        160,
        Math.max(2, 180f * Math.max(0, current) / Math.max(1, Math.max(baseline, current))),
        3,
        current >= 0 && (j.operationBudget == 0 || current <= j.operationBudget) ? GREEN : RED);
    c.small(
        j.once
            ? "Свой проект · без премии за тесты"
            : "Свои случаи: " + Math.min(2, s().customCases.size()) + " / 2 · бонус 100 ₽",
        225,
        156,
        GOLD);
    c.wrap(labResult, 28, 176, 424, TEXT);
    button(
        "Запустить",
        28,
        226,
        132,
        () -> {
          labResult = Coding.probe(s(), j, inputs.clone());
          measure(j);
          save();
        });
    button("Подсказка", 168, 226, 132, () -> scene("Заметка по расследованию", j.hint));
    button("К исходнику", 308, 226, 144, () -> page(2));
  }

  private void history() {
    c.small("GIT · ВЫБОР ВЕРСИИ = ОТКАТ", 28, 64, GOLD);
    small(
        "Коммит",
        260,
        59,
        90,
        () -> {
          String msg = Coding.commit(s());
          save();
          scene("Git", msg);
        });
    small("Отложить", 358, 59, 94, () -> page(10));
    for (int i = 0; i < s().revisions.size(); i++) {
      int at = i;
      var rev = s().revisions.get(i);
      String expr = rev.source.replace('\n', ' ').replaceAll(".*return ", "");
      if (expr.length() > 27) expr = expr.substring(0, 27) + "…";
      small(
          rev.label + " · " + expr,
          28,
          83 + i * 18,
          424,
          () -> {
            String msg = Coding.restore(s(), at);
            life.report = null;
            save();
            scene("Git / checkout", msg);
          });
    }
    button("Назад к исходнику", 28, 231, 424, () -> page(2));
  }

  private void career() {
    frame("Портфолио / карьера");
    c.text(Career.rank(s()) + " · " + s().codingXp + " опыта", 28, 69, GOLD);
    c.wrap(
        Career.tier(s()) == 0
            ? "При 3 опыте откроется расследование багов. Выполнено заказов: " + s().completedOrders
            : Career.tier(s()) == 1
                ? "При 8 опыте откроются задачи на производительность."
                : "Доступны все виды задач. Клиентские заказы: максимум три в день.",
        28,
        94,
        420,
        SOFT);
    c.small("ПОСЛЕДНЕЕ СООБЩЕНИЕ", 28, 140, GREEN);
    c.wrap(
        s().lastClientMessage.isEmpty()
            ? "Первый клиент ждёт кассу. После релиза здесь останется его отзыв."
            : s().lastClientMessage,
        28,
        159,
        420,
        TEXT);
    small("Готовые проекты: " + s().shipped.size(), 28, 205, 424, () -> page(11));
    home();
  }

  private void portfolio() {
    frame("Портфолио / выпущенные проекты");
    var jobs = Arrays.stream(life.data.jobs).filter(j -> s().shipped.contains(j.id)).toList();
    int pages = Math.max(1, (jobs.size() + 4) / 5);
    portfolioPage = Math.floorMod(portfolioPage, pages);
    c.small("Выбери проект: прочитаешь отзыв после запуска.", 28, 65, SOFT);
    if (jobs.isEmpty())
      c.wrap("Здесь появится твоя первая работа. Начни с кассы для «Ложки».", 28, 100, 420, TEXT);
    for (int row = 0; row < 5; row++) {
      int at = portfolioPage * 5 + row;
      if (at >= jobs.size()) break;
      var j = jobs.get(at);
      button(j.title, 28, 88 + row * 25, 424, () -> scene(j.client, j.feedback));
    }
    small(
        "Лист " + (portfolioPage + 1) + "/" + pages,
        28,
        222,
        140,
        () -> {
          portfolioPage++;
          game.resetSelection();
        });
    small("К карьере", 182, 222, 270, () -> page(7));
  }

  private void gear() {
    frame("Мастерская / рабочее место");
    int y = 68;
    for (var g : Career.GEAR) {
      c.text(g.title(), 28, y, GOLD);
      c.wrap(g.description(), 28, y + 21, 285, SOFT);
      button(
          s().upgrades.contains(g.id()) ? "Установлено" : g.price() + " ₽",
          335,
          y + 20,
          117,
          !s().upgrades.contains(g.id()),
          () -> scene("Рабочее место", Career.buy(s(), g.id())));
      y += 74;
    }
    home();
  }

  private void project() {
    frame("Свой проект / районный бот");
    int stage = Career.projectStage(s());
    c.small("ОБЪЯВЛЕНИЯ → НАПОМИНАНИЯ → ЗАПУСК", 28, 65, GOLD);
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) c.rect(28 + i * 142, 85, 132, 4, i < stage ? GREEN : "425663");
    if (stage == 3) {
      c.wrap(
          "Бот работает. Жители поддерживают его: +160 ₽ за новый игровой день. Поступления видны в"
              + " бюджете. Все три этапа сохранены в портфолио.",
          28,
          111,
          420,
          TEXT);
      c.small("Твой первый продукт живёт за пределами редактора.", 28, 190, GREEN);
    } else {
      var j = life.data.job(List.of("board", "remind", "support").get(stage));
      c.text(j.title, 28, 108, TEXT);
      c.wrap(
          "Нужны код, тесты и "
              + j.cost
              + " ₽ на запуск этапа. Всего три этапа; после запуска — 160 ₽ в день. Можно делать"
              + " между клиентскими заказами.",
          28,
          134,
          420,
          SOFT);
      button(
          "Взяться за этап · " + Career.duration(s(), j) + " мин", 28, 191, 424, () -> choose(j));
    }
    home();
  }
}
