package life.ctrl.render;

import com.badlogic.gdx.*;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.*;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.*;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.glutils.FrameBuffer;
import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.Disposable;
import java.util.*;

/** Stable 480×270 layout with a native-resolution framebuffer and high-density assets. */
public final class PixelCanvas implements Disposable {
  public static final int W = 480, H = 270;
  public final SpriteBatch batch = new SpriteBatch();
  private final OrthographicCamera camera = new OrthographicCamera();
  private FrameBuffer frame;
  private int density;
  private final ArrayDeque<com.badlogic.gdx.math.Rectangle> clips = new ArrayDeque<>();
  private final Set<String> highDensity = new HashSet<>();
  private final Map<String, Color> colors = new HashMap<>();

  private Color color(String hex) {
    return colors.computeIfAbsent(hex, Color::valueOf);
  }

  public float smallWidth(String text) {
    layout.setText(small, text);
    return layout.width;
  }

  private final Map<String, Texture> textures = new HashMap<>();
  private final Map<String, Texture> roundedMasks = new HashMap<>();
  private final Map<String, TextureRegion[][]> actors = new HashMap<>();
  private final Texture white;
  public final BitmapFont font, small, title;
  private final GlyphLayout layout = new GlyphLayout();
  public int scale = 1, offsetX, offsetY;

  public PixelCanvas() {
    Pixmap p = new Pixmap(1, 1, Pixmap.Format.RGBA8888);
    p.setColor(Color.WHITE);
    p.fill();
    white = new Texture(p);
    p.dispose();
    font = new BitmapFont(Gdx.files.internal("fonts/hd/ui.fnt"));
    small = new BitmapFont(Gdx.files.internal("fonts/hd/small.fnt"));
    title = new BitmapFont(Gdx.files.internal("fonts/hd/title.fnt"));
    for (BitmapFont f : new BitmapFont[] {font, small, title}) {
      f.getData().setScale(.25f);
      f.setUseIntegerPositions(false);
      f.getRegion()
          .getTexture()
          .setFilter(Texture.TextureFilter.Linear, Texture.TextureFilter.Linear);
    }
    camera.setToOrtho(false, W, H);
  }

  public void preload() {
    for (String n :
        new String[] {
          "desk_ssd", "chair_upgrade", "light_amber", "light_monitor", "light_window", "shadow"
        }) texture(n);
    for (String n :
        new String[] {
          "room",
          "stair",
          "street",
          "desk",
          "bed",
          "wardrobe",
          "kitchen",
          "plant",
          "boxes",
          "bench",
          "tree",
          "lamp",
          "mailbox",
          "notice",
          "letter",
          "boxes_open",
          "plant_watered",
          "mailbox_signed",
          "portrait_hero",
          "portrait_mira",
          "portrait_arkady",
          "portrait_lev",
          "portrait_note",
          "glow_warm",
          "glow_cool",
          "hero",
          "hero_1",
          "hero_2",
          "mira",
          "arkady",
          "lev"
        }) texture(n);
    for (String n :
        new String[] {
          "cafe",
          "diner",
          "workshop",
          "park",
          "counter",
          "table",
          "fridge",
          "crt_spare",
          "bookcase",
          "portrait_asya",
          "portrait_nika",
          "portrait_tamara",
          "asya",
          "nika",
          "tamara"
        }) texture(n);
    for (String n :
        new String[] {
          "hero", "hero_1", "hero_2", "mira", "arkady", "lev", "asya", "nika", "tamara"
        }) {
      Texture t = texture(n);
      int d = highDensity.contains(n) ? 2 : 1;
      actors.put(n, TextureRegion.split(t, 16 * d, 24 * d));
    }
  }

  public Texture texture(String name) {
    return textures.computeIfAbsent(
        name,
        n -> {
          var hd = Gdx.files.internal("art/hd/" + n + ".png");
          if (hd.exists()) highDensity.add(n);
          Texture t = new Texture(hd.exists() ? hd : Gdx.files.internal("art/" + n + ".png"));
          var filter =
              n.startsWith("light_") || n.equals("shadow")
                  ? Texture.TextureFilter.Linear
                  : Texture.TextureFilter.Nearest;
          t.setFilter(filter, filter);
          return t;
        });
  }

  public void begin() {
    int target =
        Math.max(
            1,
            Math.min(
                Gdx.graphics.getBackBufferWidth() / W, Gdx.graphics.getBackBufferHeight() / H));
    if (frame == null || target != density) {
      if (frame != null) frame.dispose();
      density = target;
      frame = new FrameBuffer(Pixmap.Format.RGBA8888, W * density, H * density, false);
      frame
          .getColorBufferTexture()
          .setFilter(Texture.TextureFilter.Linear, Texture.TextureFilter.Linear);
    }
    clips.clear();
    frame.begin();
    Gdx.gl.glDisable(GL20.GL_SCISSOR_TEST);
    Gdx.gl.glClearColor(.075f, .1f, .14f, 1);
    Gdx.gl.glClear(GL20.GL_COLOR_BUFFER_BIT);
    batch.setProjectionMatrix(camera.combined);
    batch.begin();
    batch.setColor(Color.WHITE);
  }

  public void end() {
    if (!clips.isEmpty()) throw new IllegalStateException("Unbalanced render clips");
    batch.end();
    frame.end();
    int w = Gdx.graphics.getWidth(), h = Gdx.graphics.getHeight();
    scale = Math.max(1, Math.min(w / W, h / H));
    offsetX = (w - W * scale) / 2;
    offsetY = (h - H * scale) / 2;
    Gdx.gl.glViewport(0, 0, Gdx.graphics.getBackBufferWidth(), Gdx.graphics.getBackBufferHeight());
    Gdx.gl.glClearColor(.045f, .06f, .085f, 1);
    Gdx.gl.glClear(GL20.GL_COLOR_BUFFER_BIT);
    camera.setToOrtho(false, w, h);
    batch.setProjectionMatrix(camera.combined);
    batch.begin();
    batch.setColor(Color.WHITE);
    batch.draw(frame.getColorBufferTexture(), offsetX, offsetY, W * scale, H * scale, 0, 0, 1, 1);
    batch.end();
    camera.setToOrtho(false, W, H);
  }

  public float artWidth(String name) {
    Texture t = texture(name);
    return t.getWidth() / (highDensity.contains(name) ? 2f : 1f);
  }

  public float artHeight(String name) {
    Texture t = texture(name);
    return t.getHeight() / (highDensity.contains(name) ? 2f : 1f);
  }

  /** Nested framebuffer scissor; every layer is intersected with its parent. */
  public void clip(float x, float y, float w, float h) {
    batch.flush();
    float x0 = Math.max(0, x),
        y0 = Math.max(0, y),
        x1 = Math.min(W, x + w),
        y1 = Math.min(H, y + h);
    if (!clips.isEmpty()) {
      var p = clips.peek();
      x0 = Math.max(x0, p.x);
      y0 = Math.max(y0, p.y);
      x1 = Math.min(x1, p.x + p.width);
      y1 = Math.min(y1, p.y + p.height);
    }
    clips.push(
        new com.badlogic.gdx.math.Rectangle(x0, y0, Math.max(0, x1 - x0), Math.max(0, y1 - y0)));
    applyClip();
  }

  private void applyClip() {
    if (clips.isEmpty()) {
      Gdx.gl.glDisable(GL20.GL_SCISSOR_TEST);
      return;
    }
    var p = clips.peek();
    int x0 = (int) Math.ceil(p.x * density), y0 = (int) Math.ceil(p.y * density);
    int x1 = (int) Math.floor((p.x + p.width) * density),
        y1 = (int) Math.floor((p.y + p.height) * density);
    Gdx.gl.glEnable(GL20.GL_SCISSOR_TEST);
    Gdx.gl.glScissor(x0, H * density - y1, Math.max(0, x1 - x0), Math.max(0, y1 - y0));
  }

  public void unclip() {
    batch.flush();
    if (clips.isEmpty()) throw new IllegalStateException("Missing render clip");
    clips.pop();
    applyClip();
  }

  public void fitted(String text, float x, float y, float width, String color, boolean compact) {
    BitmapFont f = compact ? small : font;
    layout.setText(f, text);
    if (layout.width > width) {
      int end = text.length();
      do {
        end--;
        layout.setText(f, text.substring(0, end) + "…");
      } while (end > 0 && layout.width > width);
      text = text.substring(0, end) + "…";
    }
    clip(x, y - 1, width, compact ? 13 : 16);
    if (compact) small(text, x, y, color);
    else text(text, x, y, color);
    unclip();
  }

  public void glow(String name, float x, float y, float strength) {
    batch.setColor(1, 1, 1, Math.max(0, Math.min(1, strength)));
    art(name, x, y);
    batch.setColor(Color.WHITE);
  }

  public void rect(float x, float y, float w, float h, Color color) {
    batch.setColor(color);
    batch.draw(white, x, H - y - h, w, h);
    batch.setColor(Color.WHITE);
  }

  public void rect(float x, float y, float w, float h, String color) {
    rect(x, y, w, h, color(color));
  }

  /**
   * Smooth high-density rounded panels. A cached three-times-resolution signed-
   * distance mask avoids the chunky stepped corners of the low-res prototype.
   * The mask is white and can be tinted to any colour by the SpriteBatch.
   */
  public void roundRect(float x, float y, float w, float h, int radius, String hex) {
    int r = Math.max(0, Math.min(radius, (int)Math.min(w, h) / 2));
    if (r == 0 || w < 1 || h < 1) { rect(x, y, w, h, hex); return; }
    int pw = Math.max(2, Math.round(w * 3)), ph = Math.max(2, Math.round(h * 3));
    String key = pw + ":" + ph + ":" + r;
    Texture mask = roundedMasks.get(key);
    if (mask == null) {
      Pixmap p = new Pixmap(pw, ph, Pixmap.Format.RGBA8888);
      float radiusPx = r * 3f;
      float bx = pw / 2f - radiusPx;
      float by = ph / 2f - radiusPx;
      for (int py = 0; py < ph; py++) {
        float qy = Math.abs(py + .5f - ph / 2f) - by;
        for (int px = 0; px < pw; px++) {
          float qx = Math.abs(px + .5f - pw / 2f) - bx;
          float ax = Math.max(0, qx), ay = Math.max(0, qy);
          float distance = (float)Math.sqrt(ax * ax + ay * ay)
              + Math.min(0, Math.max(qx, qy)) - radiusPx;
          int alpha = Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, .5f - distance)) * 255);
          p.drawPixel(px, py, 0xffffff00 | alpha);
        }
      }
      mask = new Texture(p);
      mask.setFilter(Texture.TextureFilter.Linear, Texture.TextureFilter.Linear);
      p.dispose();
      roundedMasks.put(key, mask);
    }
    batch.setColor(color(hex));
    batch.draw(mask, x, H - y - h, w, h);
    batch.setColor(Color.WHITE);
  }

  public void panel(int x, int y, int w, int h) {
    rect(x + 1, y + 2, w, h, "07141cc0");
    rect(x, y, w, h, "526b70");
    rect(x + .5f, y + .5f, w - 1, h - 1, "172e3bf9");
    rect(x + 1, y + 1, w - 2, .5f, "829b9160");
  }

  public void art(String name, float x, float y) {
    Texture t = texture(name);
    batch.draw(
        t, Math.round(x), H - Math.round(y) - artHeight(name), artWidth(name), artHeight(name));
  }

  public void scaledArt(String name, float x, float y, float width, float height) {
    batch.draw(texture(name), Math.round(x), H - Math.round(y) - height, width, height);
  }

  public void actor(String name, int dir, int animation, float x, float feetY, int outfit) {
    if (name.equals("hero") && outfit > 0) name = "hero_" + outfit;
    batch.draw(actors.get(name)[dir][animation], Math.round(x) - 8, H - Math.round(feetY), 16, 24);
    batch.setColor(Color.WHITE);
  }

  public void text(String text, float x, float y, Color color) {
    font.setColor(color);
    font.draw(batch, text, Math.round(x), H - Math.round(y));
  }

  public void text(String text, float x, float y, String color) {
    text(text, x, y, color(color));
  }

  public void small(String text, float x, float y, String color) {
    small.setColor(color(color));
    small.draw(batch, text, Math.round(x), H - Math.round(y));
  }

  public void title(String text, float x, float y, String color) {
    title.setColor(color(color));
    title.draw(batch, text, Math.round(x), H - Math.round(y));
  }

  public void wrap(String text, float x, float y, float width, String color) {
    wrap(text, x, y, width, H - y - 8, color);
  }

  public void wrap(String text, float x, float y, float width, float height, String color) {
    clip(x, y - 1, Math.min(width, W - x - 12), height);
    font.setColor(color(color));
    font.draw(batch, text, x, H - y, width, com.badlogic.gdx.utils.Align.left, true);
    unclip();
  }

  public float width(String text) {
    layout.setText(font, text);
    return layout.width;
  }

  public void center(String text, float y, String color) {
    text(text, Math.round((W - width(text)) / 2), y, color);
  }

  public float logicalX(int x) {
    return (x - offsetX) / (float) scale;
  }

  public float logicalY(int y) {
    return (y - offsetY) / (float) scale;
  }

  public float mouseX() {
    return logicalX(Gdx.input.getX());
  }

  public float mouseY() {
    return logicalY(Gdx.input.getY());
  }

  public void screenshot(String path) {
    Pixmap pix =
        Pixmap.createFromFrameBuffer(
            0, 0, Gdx.graphics.getBackBufferWidth(), Gdx.graphics.getBackBufferHeight());
    PixmapIO.writePNG(Gdx.files.absolute(path), pix, -1, true);
    pix.dispose();
  }

  public void dispose() {
    font.dispose();
    small.dispose();
    title.dispose();
    textures.values().forEach(Texture::dispose);
    roundedMasks.values().forEach(Texture::dispose);
    white.dispose();
    if (frame != null) frame.dispose();
    batch.dispose();
  }
}
END FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/render/PixelCanvas.java

package life.ctrl.render;

/** Visible cutaway boundaries in gameplay units. Shared by renderer and visual QA. */
public final class SceneLayout {
  private SceneLayout() {}

  public record Bounds(int x, int y, int width, int height) {}

  public static Bounds bounds(String map) {
    return switch (map) {
      case "room" -> new Bounds(137, 57, 206, 206);
      case "stair" -> new Bounds(90, 25, 301, 254);
      case "cafe", "diner", "workshop" -> new Bounds(89, 41, 302, 230);
      case "park" -> new Bounds(24, 56, 432, 213);
      case "street" -> new Bounds(16, 48, 928, 417);
      default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unknown scene: " + map);
    };
  }
}

FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/render/AudioScene.java

package life.ctrl.render;

import com.badlogic.gdx.Gdx;
import com.badlogic.gdx.audio.*;
import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.Disposable;
import life.ctrl.model.Settings;

public final class AudioScene implements Disposable {
  private Music music, rain;
  private Sound click, step, door, success;

  public AudioScene() {
    if (Boolean.getBoolean("ctrl.noAudio")) return;
    try {
      music = Gdx.audio.newMusic(Gdx.files.internal("audio/evening.wav"));
      rain = Gdx.audio.newMusic(Gdx.files.internal("audio/rain.wav"));
      click = Gdx.audio.newSound(Gdx.files.internal("audio/click.wav"));
      step = Gdx.audio.newSound(Gdx.files.internal("audio/step.wav"));
      door = Gdx.audio.newSound(Gdx.files.internal("audio/door.wav"));
      success = Gdx.audio.newSound(Gdx.files.internal("audio/success.wav"));
      music.setLooping(true);
      rain.setLooping(true);
      music.play();
      rain.play();
    } catch (RuntimeException e) {
      Gdx.app.log("Audio", "Аудиоустройство недоступно; игра продолжит работу.");
      dispose();
    }
  }

  public boolean ready() {
    return music != null
        && rain != null
        && click != null
        && step != null
        && door != null
        && success != null;
  }

  public void update(Settings s, boolean outside, boolean focused) {
    if (music != null) music.setVolume(focused ? s.music * .65f : 0);
    if (rain != null) rain.setVolume(focused ? s.sfx * (outside ? .9f : .18f) : 0);
  }

  public void door(Settings s) {
    if (door != null) door.play(s.sfx * .7f);
  }

  public void success(Settings s) {
    if (success != null) success.play(s.sfx * .6f);
  }

  public void click(Settings s) {
    if (click != null) click.play(s.sfx * .5f);
  }

  public void step(Settings s) {
    if (step != null) step.play(s.sfx * .3f);
  }

  public void dispose() {
    if (door != null) {
      door.dispose();
      door = null;
    }
    if (success != null) {
      success.dispose();
      success = null;
    }
    if (music != null) {
      music.dispose();
      music = null;
    }
    if (rain != null) {
      rain.dispose();
      rain = null;
    }
    if (click != null) {
      click.dispose();
      click = null;
    }
    if (step != null) {
      step.dispose();
      step = null;
    }
  }
}
END FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/render/AudioScene.java

package life.ctrl.desktop;

import com.badlogic.gdx.backends.lwjgl3.Lwjgl3Application;
import com.badlogic.gdx.backends.lwjgl3.Lwjgl3ApplicationConfiguration;
import com.badlogic.gdx.backends.lwjgl3.Lwjgl3WindowAdapter;
import life.ctrl.CtrlLife;

public final class DesktopLauncher {
  public static void main(String[] args) {
    var config = new Lwjgl3ApplicationConfiguration();
    config.setTitle("CTRL+LIFE — Тихий район");
    config.setWindowedMode(1440, 810);
    config.setWindowIcon("art/icon32.png", "art/icon64.png", "art/icon128.png");
    config.setWindowSizeLimits(960, 540, -1, -1);
    config.useVsync(true);
    config.setForegroundFPS(60);
    config.setIdleFPS(15);
    if (Boolean.getBoolean("ctrl.noAudio")) config.disableAudio(true);
    var game = new CtrlLife();
    config.setWindowListener(
        new Lwjgl3WindowAdapter() {
          @Override
          public void focusLost() {
            game.focusChanged(false);
          }

          @Override
          public void focusGained() {
            game.focusChanged(true);
          }
        });
    new Lwjgl3Application(game, config);
  }
}

FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/CtrlLife.java

package life.ctrl;

import com.badlogic.gdx.*;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Input.Keys;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.*;
import life.ctrl.content.Content;
import life.ctrl.model.*;
import life.ctrl.render.*;
import life.ctrl.save.SaveStore;
import life.ctrl.ui.LifeScreens;

/** Desktop exploration slice. Menus freeze simulation, input belongs to exactly one mode. */
public class CtrlLife extends ApplicationAdapter {
  public enum Mode {
    MENU,
    CREATE,
    CONFIRM_NEW,
    WORLD,
    DIALOGUE,
    PAUSE,
    SETTINGS,
    CONTROLS,
    JOURNAL,
    MAP,
    BAG,
    INTRO,
    SHOP,
    IDE,
    SOCIAL,
    CONTACTS,
    REST,
    SCENE,
    NIGHT,
    BUDGET
  }

  private PixelCanvas c;
  private LifeScreens life;
  private List<float[]> guidePath = List.of();
  private float guideTimer;
  private AudioScene audio;
  private final com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.Color dusk =
      new com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.Color(.10f, .17f, .27f, 0);
  private Content content;
  private SaveStore saves;
  private Settings settings;
  private World world;
  private Mode mode = Mode.MENU, settingsReturn = Mode.MENU;
  private Content.Dialogue dialogue;
  private final Set<Integer> held = new HashSet<>();
  private final ArrayList<Button> buttons = new ArrayList<>();
  private final ArrayList<DrawItem> drawItems = new ArrayList<>();
  private final World.Actor actor = new World.Actor();
  private final Comparator<DrawItem> depth = Comparator.comparingDouble(d -> d.y);
  private float accumulator,
      visualTime,
      footTimer,
      cameraX,
      cameraY,
      toastTimer,
      arrivalTimer,
      doorFade;
  private final com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.Color transitionColor =
      new com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.Color(.07f, .11f, .15f, 0);
  private String toast = "", name = "Саша";
  private int selection, bindIndex = -1, newOutfit = 0, windowScale = 3;
  private boolean confirm, clicked, mouseMoved, focused = true, showDetails, hasSession;
  private float clickX, clickY;
  private static final String CREAM = "ecdbb6", MUTED = "a1b2b0", GOLD = "ecc48a", MINT = "a8c3a4";

  private record Button(
      String label, int x, int y, int width, int height, boolean enabled, Runnable action) {}

  private static final class DrawItem {
    String art;
    float x, y, artX, artY;
    int dir, frame, outfit;
    boolean person;

    DrawItem(String art, float x, float y, float ax, float ay) {
      this.art = art;
      this.x = x;
      this.y = y;
      artX = ax;
      artY = ay;
    }
  }

  @Override
  public void create() {
    content = Content.load();
    saves = new SaveStore(SaveStore.defaultDirectory(), content);
    settings = Settings.load(saves.directory());
    c = new PixelCanvas();
    c.preload();
    life = new LifeScreens(this, c);
    audio = new AudioScene();
    world = new World(content, new GameState());
    rebuildDrawItems();
    snapCamera();
    windowScale = settings.scale;
    selection = saves.exists() ? 0 : 1;
    if (!Boolean.getBoolean("ctrl.test")) applyDisplay();
    Gdx.input.setInputProcessor(
        new InputMultiplexer(life.pcNativeStage(), new InputAdapter() {
          @Override
          public boolean keyDown(int key) {
            return press(key);
          }

          @Override
          public boolean keyUp(int key) {
            held.remove(key);
            if (key == Keys.TAB) showDetails = false;
            return true;
          }

          @Override
          public boolean keyTyped(char ch) {
            if (mode == Mode.CREATE) {
              if (ch == '\b' && !name.isEmpty()) name = name.substring(0, name.length() - 1);
              else if (!Character.isISOControl(ch)
                  && (Character.isLetterOrDigit(ch) || ch == ' ' || ch == '-')
                  && name.length() < 18) name += ch;
            } else if (mode == Mode.IDE) {
              life.pcKeyTyped(ch);
            }
            return true;
          }

          @Override
          public boolean touchDown(int x, int y, int pointer, int button) {
            if (button == Input.Buttons.LEFT) {
              int px = Math.round(c.logicalX(x)), py = Math.round(c.logicalY(y));
              if (mode == Mode.IDE && life.pcPointerDown(px, py)) {
                clicked = false;
                return true;
              }
              clicked = true;
              clickX = c.logicalX(x);
              clickY = c.logicalY(y);
            }
            return true;
          }

          @Override
          public boolean touchDragged(int x, int y, int pointer) {
            if (mode == Mode.IDE)
              return life.pcPointerDragged(Math.round(c.logicalX(x)), Math.round(c.logicalY(y)));
            return false;
          }

          @Override
          public boolean touchUp(int x, int y, int pointer, int button) {
            if (mode == Mode.IDE && button == Input.Buttons.LEFT)
              return life.pcPointerUp(Math.round(c.logicalX(x)), Math.round(c.logicalY(y)));
            return false;
          }

          @Override
          public boolean mouseMoved(int x, int y) {
            mouseMoved = true;
            if (mode == Mode.IDE) life.pcPointerMove(Math.round(c.logicalX(x)), Math.round(c.logicalY(y)));
            return true;
          }
        }));
  }

  public void switchMode(Mode next) {
    change(next);
  }

  public void resetSelection() {
    selection = 0;
    confirm = false;
    clicked = false;
  }

  public void uiButton(String text, int x, int y, int w, int h, boolean enabled, Runnable run) {
    button(text, x, y, w, h, enabled, run);
  }

  /** Register a native game UI hit-target without painting a legacy rectangular button. */
  public void uiHotspot(String label, int x, int y, int w, int h, Runnable action) {
    int index = buttons.size();
    Button b = new Button(label, x, y, w, h, true, action);
    buttons.add(b);
    if (mouseMoved && contains(b, c.mouseX(), c.mouseY())) selection = index;
  }


  public void refreshState() {
    Social.expire(world.state);
    rebuildDrawItems();
    saveQuiet();
    guideTimer = 0;
  }

  public void successCue() {
    audio.success(settings);
  }

  public LifeScreens lifeScreens() {
    return life;
  }

  public World world() {
    return world;
  }

  public boolean audioReady() {
    return audio != null && audio.ready();
  }

  public Mode mode() {
    return mode;
  }

  public PixelCanvas canvas() {
    return c;
  }

  private void change(Mode next) {
    if (mode == Mode.IDE && next != Mode.IDE && life != null) life.pcCancelPointer();
    mode = next;
    held.clear();
    accumulator = 0;
    selection = next == Mode.MENU && !saves.exists() ? 1 : 0;
    confirm = false;
    clicked = false;
    showDetails = false;
    buttons.clear();
    world.moving = false;
  }

  private boolean press(int key) {
    if (bindIndex >= 0) {
      if (key == Keys.ESCAPE) {
        bindIndex = -1;
        return true;
      }
      if (settings.bind(bindIndex, key)) {
        bindIndex = -1;
        persistSettings();
      } else notify("Эта клавиша занята. Выбери другую.");
      return true;
    }
    if (key == Keys.F11) {
      settings.fullscreen = !settings.fullscreen;
      applyDisplay();
      persistSettings();
      held.clear();
      return true;
    }
    if (mode == Mode.IDE && life.pcKeyDown(key)) return true;
    if (mode == Mode.WORLD) {
      held.add(key);
      if (key == settings.interact || key == Keys.ENTER) {
        interact();
      } else if (key == Keys.ESCAPE) {
        change(Mode.PAUSE);
        saveQuiet();
      } else if (key == settings.journal) {
        change(Mode.JOURNAL);
      } else if (key == Keys.R) {
        change(Mode.CONTACTS);
      } else if (key == Keys.B) {
        change(Mode.BUDGET);
      } else if (key == Keys.M) {
        change(Mode.MAP);
      } else if (key == Keys.I) {
        change(Mode.BAG);
      } else if (key == Keys.F5) {
        save();
      } else if (key == Keys.TAB) {
        showDetails = true;
      }
      return true;
    }
    if (key == Keys.ESCAPE) {
      back();
      return true;
    }
    if (key == Keys.UP || key == Keys.DOWN) {
      int direction = key == Keys.UP ? -1 : 1;
      int next = selection + direction;
      while (next >= 0 && next < buttons.size() && !buttons.get(next).enabled) next += direction;
      if (next >= 0 && next < buttons.size()) selection = next;
      return true;
    }
    if (key == Keys.ENTER) {
      confirm = true;
      return true;
    }
    if (mode == Mode.DIALOGUE && key >= Keys.NUM_1 && key <= Keys.NUM_3) {
      selection = key - Keys.NUM_1;
      confirm = true;
    }
    return true;
  }

  private void back() {
    switch (mode) {
      case MENU -> {
        /* Exit is an explicit menu action. */
      }
      case CREATE, CONFIRM_NEW -> change(Mode.MENU);
      case SETTINGS -> {
        persistSettings();
        change(settingsReturn);
      }
      case CONTROLS -> change(Mode.SETTINGS);
      case INTRO -> {
        world.state.introSeen = true;
        change(Mode.WORLD);
        saveQuiet();
      }
      case IDE, SHOP, SOCIAL, CONTACTS, REST, SCENE, NIGHT, BUDGET, PAUSE, JOURNAL, MAP, BAG -> {
        change(Mode.WORLD);
        saveQuiet();
      }
      case DIALOGUE -> {
        change(Mode.WORLD);
        saveQuiet();
      }
      default -> change(Mode.PAUSE);
    }
  }

  private void openSettings(Mode from) {
    settingsReturn = from;
    change(Mode.SETTINGS);
  }

  private void newGame() {
    GameState state = new GameState();
    state.name = name.strip().isEmpty() ? "Саша" : name.strip();
    state.outfit = newOutfit;
    world = new World(content, state);
    hasSession = true;
    rebuildDrawItems();
    snapCamera();
    life.introPage = 0;
    change(Mode.INTRO);
    arrivalTimer = 4;
    saveQuiet();
    notify("WASD — идти · E — действие · J — журнал");
  }

  private void continueGame() {
    try {
      var loaded = saves.load();
      world = new World(content, loaded.state());
      hasSession = true;
      rebuildDrawItems();
      snapCamera();
      life.introPage = 0;
      change(world.state.introSeen ? Mode.WORLD : Mode.INTRO);
      arrivalTimer = 3;
      if (loaded.fromBackup()) notify("Восстановлена предыдущая резервная копия.");
    } catch (IOException e) {
      notify("Сохранение не прочитано. Можно начать новую игру.");
    }
  }

  private void save() {
    try {
      saves.save(world.state);
      notify("Игра сохранена.");
    } catch (IOException | RuntimeException e) {
      notify("Не удалось сохранить. Проверь доступ к папке данных.");
      Gdx.app.error("Save", "Не удалось сохранить игру", e);
    }
  }

  private void saveQuiet() {
    if (!hasSession) return;
    try {
      saves.save(world.state);
    } catch (IOException | RuntimeException e) {
      notify("Автосохранение не удалось. Нажми F5 для повтора.");
      Gdx.app.error("Save", "Автосохранение", e);
    }
  }

  private void persistSettings() {
    try {
      settings.save(saves.directory());
    } catch (IOException e) {
      notify("Не удалось сохранить настройки.");
    }
  }

  private void applyDisplay() {
    if (settings.fullscreen) Gdx.graphics.setFullscreenMode(Gdx.graphics.getDisplayMode());
    else {
      var display = Gdx.graphics.getDisplayMode();
      int max = Math.max(2, Math.min(display.width / 480, display.height / 270));
      windowScale = Math.min(settings.scale, max);
      Gdx.graphics.setWindowedMode(480 * windowScale, 270 * windowScale);
    }
  }

  public void focusChanged(boolean focused) {
    this.focused = focused;
    held.clear();
    accumulator = 0;
    showDetails = false;
    if (!focused && mode == Mode.WORLD) {
      change(Mode.PAUSE);
      saveQuiet();
    }
  }

  @Override
  public void pause() {
    focusChanged(false);
  }

  @Override
  public void resume() {
    focused = true;
    held.clear();
    accumulator = 0;
  }

  private void notify(String text) {
    toast = text;
    toastTimer = 4.5f;
  }

  private void interact() {
    var npc = world.interactionNpc();
    var prop = world.nearestProp();
    if (npc != null) {
      if (npc.id.equals("mira") || npc.id.equals("asya") || npc.id.equals("nika")) {
        if (npc.id.equals(world.state.plannedGirl)
            && world.state.map.equals(world.state.plannedVenue)) life.openDate();
        else life.openSocial(npc.id, false);
        return;
      }
      String reaction = Career.community(world.state, world.state.map);
      if (npc.id.equals("tamara") && !reaction.isEmpty()) {
        life.scene(
            "Тамара", reaction + "\n\nЕда — у стойки. Рада, что ты заглянул(а).", Mode.WORLD);
        return;
      }
      openDialogue(world.npcDialogue(npc));
      return;
    }
    if (prop == null) return;
    audio.click(settings);
    switch (prop.action) {
      case "mentor" -> {
        life.openCode();
        life.codePage = 8;
        return;
      }
      case "computer" -> {
        life.openCode();
        return;
      }
      case "shop" -> {
        life.shopPage = 0;
        change(Mode.SHOP);
        return;
      }
      case "rest" -> {
        change(Mode.REST);
        return;
      }
      case "date" -> {
        life.openDate();
        return;
      }
      case "courier" -> {
        life.scene("Подработка без ПК", Life.courier(world.state), Mode.WORLD);
        return;
      }
      case "wait" -> {
        Life.advance(world.state, 30);
        world.state.energy = Math.min(100, world.state.energy + 8);
        life.scene("Пауза в сквере", "Прошло 30 минут. Ты немного отдохнул(а).", Mode.WORLD);
        return;
      }
    }
    if (prop.action.equals("door")) {
      audio.door(settings);
      doorFade = settings.reducedEffects ? 0 : .18f;
      world.enter(prop);
      rebuildDrawItems();
      snapCamera();
      held.clear();
      accumulator = 0;
      arrivalTimer = 3;
      saveQuiet();
    } else if (prop.action.equals("wardrobe")) {
      world.state.outfit = (world.state.outfit + 1) % 3;
      notify("Куртка: " + new String[] {"медовая", "дождливая", "кирпичная"}[world.state.outfit]);
      saveQuiet();
    } else openDialogue(world.propDialogue(prop));
  }

  private void openDialogue(String id) {
    dialogue = content.dialogue(id);
    change(Mode.DIALOGUE);
    audio.click(settings);
  }

  private void choose(Content.Choice choice) {
    boolean delivered = world.state.deliveredLetter;
    world.effect(choice.effect);
    rebuildDrawItems();
    saveQuiet();
    if (!delivered && world.state.deliveredLetter) {
      audio.success(settings);
      notify("Письмо у Миры. В журнале новая запись.");
    }
    if (choice.next.isEmpty()) change(Mode.WORLD);
    else {
      dialogue = content.dialogue(choice.next);
      selection = 0;
    }
  }

  private float targetCameraX() {
    return Math.max(0, Math.min(world.map().width - 480, world.state.x - 240));
  }

  private float targetCameraY() {
    return Math.max(0, Math.min(world.map().height - 270, world.state.y - 172));
  }

  private void snapCamera() {
    cameraX = targetCameraX();
    cameraY = targetCameraY();
  }

  private void rebuildDrawItems() {
    drawItems.clear();
    for (var p : world.map().objects)
      if (p.art != null && world.visible(p)) {
        String art = p.art;
        if (p.id.equals("boxes") && world.state.unpacked) art = "boxes_open";
        if (p.id.equals("plant") && world.state.wateredPlant) art = "plant_watered";
        if (p.id.equals("post") && world.state.signedMailbox) art = "mailbox_signed";
        drawItems.add(
            new DrawItem(
                art,
                p.x,
                p.solid != null ? p.solid[1] + p.solid[3] : p.artY + c.artHeight(art),
                p.artX,
                p.artY));
      }
    for (var n : world.npcs()) {
      DrawItem d = new DrawItem(n.art, 0, 0, 0, 0);
      d.person = true;
      drawItems.add(d);
    }
    DrawItem hero = new DrawItem("hero", world.state.x, world.state.y, 0, 0);
    hero.person = true;
    drawItems.add(hero);
  }

  @Override
  public void render() {
    float delta = Math.min(Gdx.graphics.getDeltaTime(), .1f);
    visualTime += delta;
    toastTimer = Math.max(0, toastTimer - delta);
    arrivalTimer = Math.max(0, arrivalTimer - delta);
    doorFade = Math.max(0, doorFade - delta);
    if (mode == Mode.WORLD && focused) {
      accumulator += delta;
      float dx =
          (held.contains(settings.right) || held.contains(Keys.RIGHT) ? 1 : 0)
              - (held.contains(settings.left) || held.contains(Keys.LEFT) ? 1 : 0);
      float dy =
          (held.contains(settings.down) || held.contains(Keys.DOWN) ? 1 : 0)
              - (held.contains(settings.up) || held.contains(Keys.UP) ? 1 : 0);
      while (accumulator >= World.STEP) {
        world.tick(dx, dy);
        accumulator -= World.STEP;
      }
      if (world.moving) {
        footTimer += delta;
        if (footTimer > .30f) {
          audio.step(settings);
          footTimer = 0;
        }
      }
      float blend = 1 - (float) Math.exp(-delta * 9);
      cameraX += (targetCameraX() - cameraX) * blend;
      cameraY += (targetCameraY() - cameraY) * blend;
    } else accumulator = 0;
    audio.update(
        settings,
        (world.state.map.equals("street") || world.state.map.equals("park"))
            && Atmosphere.rain(world.state),
        focused);
    buttons.clear();
    c.begin();
    drawWorld();
    if (mode == Mode.WORLD) drawGuide();
    if (doorFade > 0) {
      transitionColor.a = doorFade / .18f;
      c.rect(0, 0, 480, 270, transitionColor);
    }
    switch (mode) {
      case MENU -> drawMenu();
      case CREATE -> drawCreate();
      case CONFIRM_NEW -> drawConfirm();
      case WORLD -> drawHud();
      case DIALOGUE -> {
        drawHud();
        drawDialogue();
      }
      case PAUSE -> drawPause();
      case SETTINGS -> drawSettings();
      case CONTROLS -> drawControls();
      case JOURNAL, MAP, BAG, INTRO, SHOP, IDE, SOCIAL, CONTACTS, REST, SCENE, NIGHT, BUDGET ->
          life.draw(mode);
    }
    if (toastTimer > 0 && mode == Mode.WORLD) {
      int w = Math.min(460, (int) Math.ceil(c.width(toast)) + 18);
      int y = 57;
      c.rect((480 - w) / 2, y, w, 20, "182a35f5");
      c.fitted(toast, (480 - w) / 2f + 9, y + 4, w - 18, MINT, false);
    }
    c.end();
    if (mode == Mode.IDE) life.renderPcOverlay(delta);
    activateButtons();
    mouseMoved = false;
  }

  private void drawWorld() {
    float ox = Math.round(cameraX), oy = Math.round(cameraY);
    c.art(world.state.map, -ox, -oy);
    var boundary = SceneLayout.bounds(world.state.map);
    c.clip(boundary.x() - ox, boundary.y() - oy, boundary.width(), boundary.height());
    if (world.state.map.equals("street")) {
      String[] names = {"ОБЩЕЖИТИЕ № 7", "ПЛЁНКА", "МАСТЕРСКАЯ", "ЛОЖКА"};
      int[] left = {76, 300, 411, 628}, widths = {200, 86, 185, 217};
      for (int i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
        c.rect(left[i] - ox, 145 - oy, widths[i], 12, "203c46");
        c.rect(left[i] - ox, 145 - oy, widths[i], .5f, "b7b397");
        c.fitted(
            names[i],
            left[i] + (widths[i] - c.smallWidth(names[i])) / 2 - ox,
            146 - oy,
            widths[i] - 4,
            "ead7ae",
            true);
      }
      c.rect(850 - ox, 415 - oy, 85, 14, "203c46");
      c.fitted("СКВЕР →", 862 - ox, 417 - oy, 69, "d6d9b5", true);
    }
    for (var item : drawItems)
      if (item.person) {
        if (item.art.equals("hero")) {
          item.x = world.state.x;
          item.y = world.state.y;
          item.dir = world.state.direction;
          item.frame = world.moving ? (int) (world.state.elapsed * 8) % 4 : 0;
          item.outfit = world.state.outfit;
        } else
          for (var npc : world.npcs())
            if (npc.art.equals(item.art)) {
              world.position(npc, actor);
              item.x = actor.x;
              item.y = actor.y;
              item.dir = actor.direction;
              item.frame = actor.moving ? (int) (world.state.elapsed * 5) % 4 : 0;
            }
      }
    drawItems.sort(depth);
    for (var item : drawItems) {
      if (item.person) {
        c.scaledArt("shadow", item.x - 9 - ox, item.y - 4 - oy, 18, 7);
        c.actor(item.art, item.dir, item.frame, item.x - ox, item.y - oy, item.outfit);
      } else {
        String drawnArt =
            item.art.equals("desk")
                    && world.state.map.equals("room")
                    && world.state.upgrades.contains("ssd")
                ? "desk_ssd"
                : item.art;
        if (item.art.equals("desk")
            && world.state.map.equals("room")
            && world.state.upgrades.contains("chair")) c.art("chair_upgrade", 204 - ox, 121 - oy);
        c.scaledArt("shadow", item.artX - 2 - ox, item.y - 7 - oy, c.artWidth(drawnArt) + 4, 13);
        if (item.artX + c.artWidth(drawnArt) > ox
            && item.artX < ox + 480
            && item.artY + c.artHeight(drawnArt) > oy
            && item.artY < oy + 270) c.art(drawnArt, item.artX - ox, item.artY - oy);
      }
    }
    float night = Atmosphere.night(world.state);
    boolean outside = world.state.map.equals("street") || world.state.map.equals("park");
    dusk.a = night * (outside ? .32f : .18f);
    c.rect(0, 0, 480, 270, dusk);
    if (world.state.map.equals("room")) {
      c.art("light_monitor", 107 - ox, 64 - oy);
      c.glow("light_window", 170 - ox, 55 - oy, .3f + night * .7f);
      c.rect(211 - ox, 121 - oy, 2, 2, ((int) (visualTime * 2) % 2 == 0) ? "c4c992" : "68756e");
      if (!settings.reducedEffects) {
        int lift = (int) (visualTime * 4) % 4;
        c.rect(163 - ox, 110 - oy - lift, 1, 2, "d8d3b14a");
      }
    }
    if (world.state.map.equals("stair"))
      c.glow("light_amber", 152 - ox, 40 - oy, .4f + night * .6f);
    if (world.state.map.equals("cafe") || world.state.map.equals("diner"))
      c.art("light_amber", 90 - ox, 68 - oy);
    if (world.state.map.equals("street")) {
      for (float[] light : new float[][] {{391, 282}, {862, 405}, {649, 192}, {713, 192}})
        c.glow("light_amber", light[0] - 80 - ox, light[1] - 56 - oy, night);
      for (int x : new int[] {130, 241, 445, 556, 685, 796})
        c.glow("light_window", x - 80 - ox, 118 - oy, night);
    }
    if (outside && Atmosphere.rain(world.state) && !settings.reducedEffects) {
      for (int i = 0; i < 42; i++) {
        float x = (i * 83 + visualTime * 19) % 500 - 10, y = (i * 47 + visualTime * 125) % 290 - 10;
        c.rect(x, y, 1, 4, "b8c9cc42");
        if (i < 10) {
          c.clip(
              -ox,
              (world.state.map.equals("street") ? 221 : 56) - oy,
              world.map().width,
              world.state.map.equals("street") ? 244 : 208);
          float t = (visualTime * 1.7f + i * .31f) % 1;
          if (t < .45f) {
            float px = (i * 67 + 43 - cameraX * .2f) % 480, py = 155 + i % 4 * 12;
            c.rect(px - 2 - t * 3, py, 4 + t * 6, 1, "bcc8bb45");
          }
          c.unclip();
        }
      }
    }
    c.unclip();
  }

  private void meter(String label, double value, int x, String color) {
    c.small(label + " " + (int) value, x, 8, CREAM);
    c.rect(x, 21, 70, 3, "344652");
    c.rect(x, 21, Math.max(1, (float) value * .7f), 3, value < 25 ? "df8d72" : color);
  }

  private void drawHud() {
    c.rect(0, 0, 480, 41, "15232ef8");
    c.rect(12, 40, 456, .5f, "607a7560");
    meter("ЗДОРОВЬЕ", world.state.health, 12, "b99d89");
    meter("СЫТОСТЬ", world.state.hunger, 100, "b8bd82");
    meter("ЭНЕРГИЯ", world.state.energy, 188, "8caaa5");
    c.small(world.state.money + " ₽", 285, 8, GOLD);
    c.small("День " + world.state.day() + " · " + world.state.clock(), 365, 8, CREAM);
    c.fitted(world.map().name + " · " + Atmosphere.period(world.state), 12, 28, 223, MUTED, true);
    c.small("J план · M карта · R люди · B деньги", 242, 28, MUTED);
    if (mode != Mode.WORLD) return;
    var target = Navigation.goal(world, life.data);
    c.rect(0, 231, 480, 39, "15232efb");
    c.rect(12, 231, 456, .5f, "607a7560");
    c.small("ТЕКУЩАЯ ЦЕЛЬ", 12, 233, GOLD);
    c.fitted(target.title(), 12, 244, 456, CREAM, false);
    c.fitted(target.hint(), 12, 259, 456, MUTED, true);
    var n = world.interactionNpc();
    var p = world.nearestProp();
    String prompt = n != null ? "Поговорить: " + n.name.split(" · ")[0] : p != null ? p.label : "";
    if (!prompt.isEmpty()) {
      String text = Keys.toString(settings.interact) + " — " + prompt;
      int w = Math.min(464, (int) Math.ceil(c.width(text)) + 20);
      c.panel((480 - w) / 2, 201, w, 23);
      c.fitted(text, (480 - w) / 2f + 10, 206, w - 20, CREAM, false);
    }
  }

  private void drawGuide() {
    var goal = Navigation.localGoal(world, Navigation.goal(world, life.data));
    guideTimer -= Gdx.graphics.getDeltaTime();
    if (guideTimer <= 0) {
      guidePath = Navigation.route(world, goal.x(), goal.y());
      guideTimer = .45f;
    }
    int phase = (int) (visualTime * 3) % 3;
    for (int i = phase; i < guidePath.size(); i += 5) {
      var p = guidePath.get(i);
      float x = p[0] - cameraX, y = p[1] - cameraY;
      if (x > 5 && x < 475 && y > 45 && y < 200) c.rect(x - 1, y - 1, 3, 2, "d8b875a0");
    }
    float x = goal.x() - cameraX, y = goal.y() - cameraY - 29;
    if (x > 12 && x < 468 && y > 48 && y < 198) {
      c.rect(x - 7, y - 3, 15, 17, "192f3dde");
      c.text("!", x - 2, y - 2, GOLD);
    } else {
      float px = Math.max(18, Math.min(462, x)), py = Math.max(55, Math.min(198, y));
      c.rect(px - 8, py - 6, 17, 17, "192f3dde");
      c.text(x < 18 ? "←" : x > 462 ? "→" : y < 55 ? "↑" : "↓", px - 5, py - 5, GOLD);
    }
  }

  private void shade() {
    c.rect(0, 0, 480, 270, "142332cb");
  }

    shade();
    c.small("ТИХИЙ ГОРОД. БОЛЬШОЕ НАЧАЛО.", 32, 31, MINT);
    c.title("CTRL+LIFE", 30, 57, CREAM);
    c.text("РАЙОН, В КОТОРОМ ГОРИТ СВЕТ", 33, 93, GOLD);
    c.wrap(
        "Последняя маршрутка.\nКомната в общежитии.\nПервый заказ — и новые люди.",
        33,
        122,
        236,
        MUTED);
    button("Продолжить", 32, 182, 176, 23, saves.exists(), this::continueGame);
    button(
        "Новая игра",
        32,
        209,
        176,
        23,
        true,
        () -> {
          name = "Саша";
          newOutfit = 0;
          change(saves.exists() ? Mode.CONFIRM_NEW : Mode.CREATE);
        });
    button("Настройки", 245, 182, 135, 23, true, () -> openSettings(Mode.MENU));
    button("Выйти", 245, 209, 135, 23, true, () -> Gdx.app.exit());
    c.small("ПЕРВЫЕ ДЕНЬГИ. ПЕРВЫЕ ЧУВСТВА.", 32, 248, MUTED);
    c.small("0.4.0", 419, 248, MUTED);
    // An illuminated architectural vignette cut out of the room.
    c.panel(314, 44, 120, 112);
    c.art("desk", 342, 66);
    c.actor("hero", 0, (int) (visualTime * .7f) % 4, 372, 139, 0);
    c.small("КОМНАТА 04", 339, 145, GOLD);
  }

  private void drawCreate() {
    shade();
    c.panel(65, 24, 350, 222);
    c.small("НОВЫЙ АДРЕС", 84, 38, MINT);
    c.text("Кто зажигает свет в комнате 04?", 84, 59, CREAM);
    c.small("Имя · 21 год или старше", 84, 89, MUTED);
    c.rect(84, 105, 220, 25, "192b39");
    c.text(name + ((int) (visualTime * 2) % 2 == 0 ? "_" : ""), 91, 109, GOLD);
    c.small("Печатай имя. Backspace — стереть.", 84, 133, MUTED);
    c.actor("hero", 0, 0, 359, 125, newOutfit);
    button(
        "Куртка: " + new String[] {"медовая", "дождливая", "кирпичная"}[newOutfit],
        84,
        159,
        210,
        23,
        true,
        () -> newOutfit = (newOutfit + 1) % 3);
    button("Начать вечер", 84, 199, 180, 25, true, this::newGame);
    button("Назад", 292, 199, 103, 25, true, () -> change(Mode.MENU));
  }

  private void drawConfirm() {
    shade();
    c.panel(70, 65, 340, 141);
    c.text("Начать новую историю?", 89, 81, CREAM);
    c.wrap(
        "Текущее сохранение будет заменено. Предыдущая копия останется в папке данных.",
        89,
        108,
        300,
        MUTED);
    button("Новая история", 89, 163, 149, 25, true, () -> change(Mode.CREATE));
    button("Отмена", 253, 163, 137, 25, true, () -> change(Mode.MENU));
  }

  private void drawPause() {
    shade();
    c.panel(133, 37, 214, 200);
    c.center("МОЖНО НЕ ТОРОПИТЬСЯ", 52, CREAM);
    c.center("Время остановлено", 73, MUTED);
    button("Вернуться в игру", 151, 101, 178, 24, true, () -> change(Mode.WORLD));
    button("Сохранить", 151, 130, 178, 24, true, this::save);
    button("Настройки", 151, 159, 178, 24, true, () -> openSettings(Mode.PAUSE));
    button(
        "В главное меню",
        151,
        188,
        178,
        24,
        true,
        () -> {
          saveQuiet();
          change(Mode.MENU);
        });
  }

  private void drawDialogue() {
    c.panel(14, 142, 452, 121);
    c.small(dialogue.speaker.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT), 27, 150, GOLD);
    String portrait =
        switch (dialogue.speaker) {
          case "Мира" -> "mira";
          case "Аркадий" -> "arkady";
          case "Лев" -> "lev";
          default -> "note";
        };
    c.rect(26, 165, 34, 35, "1c303e");
    c.art("portrait_" + portrait, 27, 166);
    c.wrap(dialogue.text, 72, 166, 378, CREAM);
    int count = dialogue.choices.length;
    for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      var choice = dialogue.choices[i];
      button(
          (i + 1) + ". " + choice.label.replace("Саша", world.state.name),
          27,
          207 + i * 16,
          426,
          15,
          true,
          () -> choose(choice));
    }
    c.small("↑↓ / Enter · Esc — закончить разговор", 232, 133, MUTED);
  }

  private void drawSettings() {
    shade();
    c.panel(70, 16, 340, 239);
    c.text("Настройки вечера", 89, 30, CREAM);
    button(
        "Музыка    " + Math.round(settings.music * 100) + "%",
        89,
        57,
        302,
        23,
        true,
        () -> {
          settings.music = settings.music >= .99f ? 0 : Math.min(1, settings.music + .25f);
          persistSettings();
        });
    button(
        "Эффекты   " + Math.round(settings.sfx * 100) + "%",
        89,
        84,
        302,
        23,
        true,
        () -> {
          settings.sfx = settings.sfx >= .99f ? 0 : Math.min(1, settings.sfx + .25f);
          persistSettings();
        });
    button(
        "Окно "
            + settings.scale
            + "×  /  "
            + (settings.fullscreen ? "полный экран" : "оконный режим"),
        89,
        111,
        302,
        23,
        true,
        () -> {
          if (settings.fullscreen) {
            settings.fullscreen = false;
            settings.scale = 2;
          } else if (settings.scale == 4) settings.fullscreen = true;
          else settings.scale++;
          applyDisplay();
          persistSettings();
        });
    button(
        "Дождь и эффекты: " + (settings.reducedEffects ? "уменьшены" : "полные"),
        89,
        138,
        302,
        23,
        true,
        () -> {
          settings.reducedEffects = !settings.reducedEffects;
          persistSettings();
        });
    button("Переназначить клавиши", 89, 165, 302, 23, true, () -> change(Mode.CONTROLS));
    button("Готово", 89, 208, 302, 25, true, this::back);
    c.small("F11 — полный экран. Звук: нажми для смены уровня.", 89, 193, MUTED);
  }

  private void drawControls() {
    shade();
    c.panel(70, 12, 340, 247);
    c.text("Управление", 89, 25, CREAM);
    String[] labels = {"Вверх", "Вниз", "Влево", "Вправо", "Взаимодействие", "Журнал"};
    for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
      int index = i;
      button(
          labels[i] + "    [ " + Keys.toString(settings.key(i)) + " ]",
          89,
          50 + i * 24,
          302,
          21,
          true,
          () -> {
            bindIndex = index;
            held.clear();
          });
    }
    c.small(
        bindIndex >= 0
            ? "Нажми новую клавишу. Esc — отмена."
            : "Стрелки, Enter, Esc, I, M, Tab, F5, F11 закреплены.",
        89,
        198,
        bindIndex >= 0 ? GOLD : MUTED);
    button("Готово", 89, 222, 302, 23, bindIndex < 0, () -> change(Mode.SETTINGS));
  }

  private void button(
      String label, int x, int y, int width, int height, boolean enabled, Runnable action) {
    int index = buttons.size();
    Button b = new Button(label, x, y, width, height, enabled, action);
    buttons.add(b);
    if (mouseMoved && contains(b, c.mouseX(), c.mouseY()) && enabled) selection = index;
    boolean selected = selection == index && enabled;
    boolean pressed =
        enabled && ((clicked && contains(b, clickX, clickY)) || (confirm && selected));
    c.rect(x, y, width, height, selected ? "c8b184" : "48616b");
    c.rect(
        x + .5f,
        y + .5f,
        width - 1,
        height - 1,
        pressed ? "112c38" : selected ? "354f58" : "203946");
    if (selected) c.rect(x + 3, y + 3, 2, height - 6, GOLD);
    c.fitted(
        label,
        x + 10,
        y + (height < 20 ? 2 : (height - 15) / 2),
        width - 19,
        enabled ? (selected ? GOLD : CREAM) : "809597",
        height < 20);
  }

  private boolean contains(Button b, float x, float y) {
    return x >= b.x && x < b.x + b.width && y >= b.y && y < b.y + b.height;
  }

  private void activateButtons() {
    Button target = null;
    if (clicked) {
      // Late-drawn window/dock buttons appear on top; give them click priority.
      for (int i = buttons.size() - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        var b = buttons.get(i);
        if (b.enabled && contains(b, clickX, clickY)) {
          target = b;
          break;
        }
      }
    } else if (confirm
        && selection >= 0
        && selection < buttons.size()
        && buttons.get(selection).enabled) target = buttons.get(selection);
    clicked = false;
    confirm = false;
    if (target != null) {
      audio.click(settings);
      target.action.run();
    }
  }

  @Override
  public void resize(int width, int height) {
    held.clear();
    if (life != null) life.resizePc(width, height);
  }

  @Override
  public void dispose() {
    if (mode != Mode.MENU && mode != Mode.CREATE && mode != Mode.CONFIRM_NEW && world != null)
      saveQuiet();
    if (settings != null) persistSettings();
    if (audio != null) audio.dispose();
    if (life != null) life.dispose();
    if (c != null) c.dispose();
  }
}

package life.ctrl.bytescript;

import java.util.*;

/** A bounded expression language, never evaluated by Java, a shell or an external service. */
public final class ByteScript {
  public enum Kind {
    SYNTAX,
    SEMANTIC
  }

  public static final class Error extends RuntimeException {
    public final Kind kind;
    public final int line, column;

    public Error(Kind kind, String message, int line, int column) {
      super(message);
      this.kind = kind;
      this.line = line;
      this.column = column;
    }
  }

  public record Program(String name, List<String> parameters, Expr expression) {
    public int operations(double... args) {
      run(args);
      Map<String, Double> values = new HashMap<>();
      for (int i = 0; i < args.length; i++) values.put(parameters.get(i), args[i]);
      return count(expression, values);
    }

    private static int count(Expr e, Map<String, Double> v) {
      if (e instanceof Binary b) return 1 + count(b.left, v) + count(b.right, v);
      if (e instanceof Select t)
        return 1 + count(t.condition, v) + count(t.condition.eval(v) != 0 ? t.yes : t.no, v);
      return 0;
    }

    public double run(double... args) {
      if (args.length != parameters.size()) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Число аргументов");
      Map<String, Double> values = new HashMap<>();
      for (int i = 0; i < args.length; i++) values.put(parameters.get(i), args[i]);
      double result = expression.eval(values);
      if (!Double.isFinite(result))
        throw new ArithmeticException("Деление на ноль или слишком большое число");
      return result;
    }
  }

  public sealed interface Expr permits Number, Variable, Binary, Select {
    double eval(Map<String, Double> values);
  }

  public record Number(double value) implements Expr {
    public double eval(Map<String, Double> v) {
      return value;
    }
  }

  public record Variable(String name) implements Expr {
    public double eval(Map<String, Double> v) {
      return v.get(name);
    }
  }

  public record Binary(String op, Expr left, Expr right) implements Expr {
    public double eval(Map<String, Double> v) {
      double a = left.eval(v), b = right.eval(v);
      return switch (op) {
        case "+" -> a + b;
        case "-" -> a - b;
        case "*" -> a * b;
        case "/" -> a / b;
        case ">=" -> a >= b ? 1 : 0;
        case "<=" -> a <= b ? 1 : 0;
        case ">" -> a > b ? 1 : 0;
        case "<" -> a < b ? 1 : 0;
        case "==" -> a == b ? 1 : 0;
        default -> throw new IllegalStateException(op);
      };
    }
  }

  public record Select(Expr condition, Expr yes, Expr no) implements Expr {
    public double eval(Map<String, Double> v) {
      return condition.eval(v) != 0 ? yes.eval(v) : no.eval(v);
    }
  }

  private record Token(String text, int line, int column) {}

  private final List<Token> tokens = new ArrayList<>();
  private final Set<String> names = new HashSet<>();
  private int at;

  private ByteScript(String source) {
    if (source.length() > 4096) throw new Error(Kind.SYNTAX, "Программа слишком длинная", 1, 1);
    int line = 1, col = 1;
    for (int i = 0; i < source.length(); ) {
      char c = source.charAt(i);
      if (Character.isWhitespace(c)) {
        if (c == '\n') {
          line++;
          col = 1;
        } else col++;
        i++;
        continue;
      }
      int begin = i, startCol = col;
      if (Character.isLetter(c) || c == '_') {
        while (i < source.length()
            && (Character.isLetterOrDigit(source.charAt(i)) || source.charAt(i) == '_')) i++;
      } else if (Character.isDigit(c)) {
        while (i < source.length()
            && (Character.isDigit(source.charAt(i)) || source.charAt(i) == '.')) i++;
      } else if ("(){};,?:+-*/<>=".indexOf(c) >= 0) {
        i++;
        if (i < source.length() && source.charAt(i) == '=' && "<>= ".indexOf(c) >= 0) i++;
      } else throw new Error(Kind.SYNTAX, "Неожиданный символ «" + c + "»", line, col);
      String text = source.substring(begin, i);
      tokens.add(new Token(text, line, startCol));
      col += text.length();
    }
    if (tokens.size() > 512) throw new Error(Kind.SYNTAX, "Слишком много операций", line, col);
    tokens.add(new Token("<end>", line, col));
  }

  public static Program compile(String source) {
    return new ByteScript(source).parse();
  }

  private Program parse() {
    expect("fn");
    String name = identifier();
    expect("(");
    List<String> params = new ArrayList<>();
    if (!peek(")")) {
      do {
        String p = identifier();
        if (!names.add(p)) fail(Kind.SEMANTIC, "Повтор аргумента: " + p);
        params.add(p);
      } while (take(","));
    }
    expect(")");
    expect("{");
    expect("return");
    Expr expr = select(0);
    expect(";");
    expect("}");
    expect("<end>");
    return new Program(name, List.copyOf(params), expr);
  }

  private Expr select(int depth) {
    if (depth > 48) fail(Kind.SYNTAX, "Слишком глубокое выражение");
    Expr c = compare(depth + 1);
    if (take("?")) {
      Expr y = select(depth + 1);
      expect(":");
      return new Select(c, y, select(depth + 1));
    }
    return c;
  }

  private Expr compare(int d) {
    Expr a = add(d);
    while (Set.of(">=", "<=", ">", "<", "==").contains(current().text)) {
      String op = next().text;
      a = new Binary(op, a, add(d));
    }
    return a;
  }

  private Expr add(int d) {
    Expr a = multiply(d);
    while (peek("+") || peek("-")) {
      String op = next().text;
      a = new Binary(op, a, multiply(d));
    }
    return a;
  }

  private Expr multiply(int d) {
    Expr a = atom(d);
    while (peek("*") || peek("/")) {
      String op = next().text;
      a = new Binary(op, a, atom(d));
    }
    return a;
  }

  private Expr atom(int d) {
    if (d > 48) fail(Kind.SYNTAX, "Слишком глубокое выражение");
    if (take("(")) {
      Expr e = select(d + 1);
      expect(")");
      return e;
    }
    if (take("-")) return new Binary("-", new Number(0), atom(d + 1));
    Token token = next();
    try {
      if (Character.isDigit(token.text.charAt(0)))
        return new Number(Double.parseDouble(token.text));
    } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
      fail(Kind.SYNTAX, "Неверное число");
    }
    if (!token.text.matches("[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*"))
      throw new Error(Kind.SYNTAX, "Ожидалось число или аргумент", token.line, token.column);
    if (!names.contains(token.text))
      throw new Error(
          Kind.SEMANTIC,
          "Неизвестное имя «" + token.text + "». Сверь его с аргументами функции.",
          token.line,
          token.column);
    return new Variable(token.text);
  }

  private String identifier() {
    Token t = next();
    if (!t.text.matches("[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*"))
      throw new Error(Kind.SYNTAX, "Ожидалось имя", t.line, t.column);
    return t.text;
  }

  private Token current() {
    return tokens.get(Math.min(at, tokens.size() - 1));
  }

  private Token next() {
    Token t = current();
    at++;
    return t;
  }

  private boolean peek(String s) {
    return current().text.equals(s);
  }

  private boolean take(String s) {
    if (peek(s)) {
      at++;
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  private void expect(String s) {
    if (!take(s)) fail(Kind.SYNTAX, "Ожидалось «" + s + "», получено «" + current().text + "»");
  }

  private void fail(Kind k, String message) {
    Token t = current();
    throw new Error(k, message, t.line, t.column);
  }
}

package life.ctrl.content;

import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.Json;
import java.io.*;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.*;

/** Content contains no rendering objects and can be validated in headless tests. */
public final class Content {
  public MapDef[] maps;
  public Dialogue[] dialogues;

  public static final class MapDef {
    public String id, name, subtitle;
    public int width, height;
    public float spawnX, spawnY;
    public float[][] solids;
    public Prop[] objects;
    public Npc[] npcs;
    public Sign[] signs;
  }

  public static final class Sign {
    public String text, color;
    public float x, y;
  }

  public static final class Prop {
    public String id, label, action, art, target;
    public float x, y, artX, artY, targetX, targetY;
    public float[] solid;
  }

  public static final class Npc {
    public String id, name, art, dialogue;
    public float[][] route;
  }

  public static final class Dialogue {
    public String id, speaker, text;
    public Choice[] choices;
  }

  public static final class Choice {
    public String label, next, effect;
  }

  public static Content load() {
    try (var in = Content.class.getResourceAsStream("/data/world.json")) {
      if (in == null) throw new IllegalStateException("Не найден world.json");
      Content c =
          new Json().fromJson(Content.class, new String(in.readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
      c.validate();
      return c;
    } catch (IOException e) {
      throw new UncheckedIOException(e);
    }
  }

  public MapDef map(String id) {
    for (var m : maps) if (m.id.equals(id)) return m;
    throw new IllegalArgumentException("Карта: " + id);
  }

  public Dialogue dialogue(String id) {
    for (var d : dialogues) if (d.id.equals(id)) return d;
    throw new IllegalArgumentException("Диалог: " + id);
  }

  public void validate() {
    Set<String> mapIds = new HashSet<>(), dialogueIds = new HashSet<>(), npcIds = new HashSet<>();
    for (var d : dialogues)
      if (!dialogueIds.add(d.id)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Повтор диалога: " + d.id);
    for (var m : maps)
      if (!mapIds.add(m.id)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Повтор карты: " + m.id);
    for (var m : maps) {
      Set<String> props = new HashSet<>();
      for (var p : m.objects) {
        if (!props.add(p.id)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Повтор объекта: " + p.id);
        if (p.target != null && !mapIds.contains(p.target))
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("Сломанный переход: " + p.id);
      }
      for (var n : m.npcs) {
        if (!npcIds.add(n.id) || !dialogueIds.contains(n.dialogue) || n.route.length < 2)
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("NPC: " + n.id);
      }
    }
    for (var d : dialogues)
      for (var c : d.choices)
        if (!c.next.isEmpty() && !dialogueIds.contains(c.next))
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("Ссылка диалога: " + c.next);
  }
}

package life.ctrl.content;

import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.Json;
import java.io.*;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.*;

public final class LifeContent {
  public Girl[] girls;
  public Item[] items;
  public Job[] jobs;

  public static final class Girl {
    public String id,
        name,
        about,
        values,
        likes,
        portrait,
        homeMap,
        introduction,
        commitLine,
        notReady,
        nightLine,
        story;
    public int age;
    public String[] favoriteGifts, venues;
    public Topic[] topics;
    public DateChoice[] dateChoices;
  }

  public static final class Topic {
    public String id, label, response;
    public int affinity, trust;
  }

  public static final class DateChoice {
    public String label, response;
    public int affinity, trust;
  }

  public static final class Item {
    public String id, name, kind, description;
    public int price, hunger, energy;
  }

  public static final class Job {
    public String id,
        title,
        brief,
        function,
        source,
        kind = "feature",
        client = "Клиент",
        feedback = "",
        hint = "";
    public String[] solutions, initialSources, patches;
    public double[] seed;
    public int tier, xp = 1, cost, operationBudget;
    public boolean once;
    public String[] algorithms;
    public int pay, minutes;
    public Case[] tests;
  }

  public static final class Case {
    public double[] args;
    public double expected;
  }

  public static LifeContent load() {
    try (var in = LifeContent.class.getResourceAsStream("/data/life.json")) {
      if (in == null) throw new IOException("Нет life.json");
      var c =
          new Json()
              .fromJson(LifeContent.class, new String(in.readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
      Set<String> ids = new HashSet<>();
      for (var g : c.girls)
        if (g.age < 21 || !ids.add(g.id) || g.topics.length < 3 || g.dateChoices.length < 2)
          throw new IOException("Некорректная героиня");
      ids.clear();
      for (var j : c.jobs) {
        if (!ids.add(j.id)
            || j.pay < 0
            || j.cost < 0
            || j.minutes <= 0
            || j.minutes > 720
            || j.solutions == null
            || j.solutions.length != 3
            || j.initialSources.length != 3
            || j.patches.length != 3
            || j.tests.length == 0
            || j.seed.length == 0) throw new IOException("Некорректный заказ: " + j.id);
      }
      return c;
    } catch (IOException e) {
      throw new UncheckedIOException(e);
    }
  }

  public Girl girl(String id) {
    for (var g : girls) if (g.id.equals(id)) return g;
    throw new IllegalArgumentException("Героиня: " + id);
  }

  public Item item(String id) {
    for (var i : items) if (i.id.equals(id)) return i;
    throw new IllegalArgumentException("Предмет: " + id);
  }

  public Job job(String id) {
    for (var j : jobs) if (j.id.equals(id)) return j;
    throw new IllegalArgumentException("Заказ: " + id);
  }
}

package life.ctrl.model;

/** Continuous dusk, dawn and deterministic weather; rendering never changes saved simulation. */
public final class Atmosphere {
  private Atmosphere() {}

  public static float night(GameState s) {
    double hour = (s.gameMinutes % 1440) / 60;
    if (hour < 5 || hour >= 22) return 1;
    if (hour < 8) return (float) ((8 - hour) / 3);
    if (hour < 17) return 0;
    return (float) ((hour - 17) / 5);
  }

  public static boolean rain(GameState s) {
    return Math.floorMod(s.day(), 3) != 2;
  }

  public static String period(GameState s) {
    double h = (s.gameMinutes % 1440) / 60;
    return h < 5 ? "ночь" : h < 11 ? "утро" : h < 17 ? "день" : h < 22 ? "вечер" : "ночь";
  }
}

package life.ctrl.model;

import java.util.*;
import life.ctrl.content.LifeContent;

/** Career rules are deterministic, inspectable, and saved independently of UI. */
public final class Career {
  private Career() {}

  public record Upgrade(String id, String title, int price, String description) {}

  public static final List<Upgrade> GEAR =
      List.of(
          new Upgrade(
              "ssd",
              "SSD из мастерской",
              4200,
              "Работа занимает на 15% меньше времени. Установка включена."),
          new Upgrade(
              "chair",
              "Кресло с поддержкой спины",
              2800,
              "Дополнительная усталость за заказ: 2 вместо 6. В комнате появится кресло."));

  public static int tier(GameState s) {
    return s.codingXp >= 8 ? 2 : s.codingXp >= 3 ? 1 : 0;
  }

  public static String rank(GameState s) {
    return switch (tier(s)) {
      case 2 -> "Разработчик";
      case 1 -> "Фрилансер";
      default -> "Первый коммит";
    };
  }

  public static int paidCountToday(GameState s) {
    return (int) s.paidJobs.stream().filter(x -> x.startsWith(s.day() + ":")).count();
  }

  public static int projectStage(GameState s) {
    int n = 0;
    for (String id : List.of("board", "remind", "support")) {
      if (!s.shipped.contains(id)) break;
      n++;
    }
    return n;
  }

  public static String blocked(GameState s, LifeContent.Job j) {
    if (j.once) {
      int stage = projectStage(s);
      int required = List.of("board", "remind", "support").indexOf(j.id);
      if (s.shipped.contains(j.id)) return "Этот этап уже запущен.";
      if (required != stage) return "Сначала заверши предыдущий этап своего бота.";
      if (s.completedOrders < 1) return "Сначала выполни первый заказ для клиента.";
    } else {
      if (paidCountToday(s) >= 3)
        return "Три клиентских заказа на сегодня выполнены. Время для своего проекта или прогулки.";
      if (Coding.paidToday(s, j.id)) return "Этот заказ уже оплачен сегодня.";
    }
    if (tier(s) < j.tier)
      return "Нужно опыта: " + (j.tier == 1 ? 3 : 8) + ". Сейчас " + s.codingXp + ".";
    return "";
  }

  public static List<LifeContent.Job> offers(GameState s, LifeContent data) {
    List<LifeContent.Job> result = new ArrayList<>();
    for (var j : data.jobs) if (!j.once && j.tier == 0) result.add(j);
    List<LifeContent.Job> advanced =
        Arrays.stream(data.jobs).filter(j -> !j.once && j.tier > 0 && j.tier <= tier(s)).toList();
    if (!advanced.isEmpty())
      for (int i = 0; i < Math.min(3, advanced.size()); i++)
        result.add(advanced.get(Math.floorMod(s.day() + i - 1, advanced.size())));
    return result;
  }

  public static int duration(GameState s, LifeContent.Job j) {
    return (int) Math.ceil(j.minutes * (s.upgrades.contains("ssd") ? .85 : 1));
  }

  public static int bonus(GameState s, LifeContent.Job j) {
    if (j.once) return 0;
    return (s.customCases.size() >= 2 ? 100 : 0)
        + (s.rushJob && s.minutes() + duration(s, j) <= s.rushDeadline ? j.pay / 4 : 0);
  }

  public static String buy(GameState s, String id) {
    var gear = GEAR.stream().filter(g -> g.id.equals(id)).findFirst().orElseThrow();
    if (s.upgrades.contains(id)) return "Уже установлено.";
    if (!Economy.spend(s, gear.price, gear.title))
      return "Не хватает денег. Улучшение подождёт; на работу можно брать заказы и со старым ПК.";
    s.upgrades.add(id);
    return gear.title + " установлено. " + gear.description;
  }

  public static void settleProject(GameState s) {
    if (projectStage(s) < 3) return;
    if (s.projectIncomeDay < 0) {
      s.projectIncomeDay = s.day();
      return;
    }
    int days = s.day() - s.projectIncomeDay;
    if (days <= 0) return;
    Economy.earn(s, Math.min(days, 365) * 160, "Поддержка районного бота: " + days + " дн.");
    s.projectIncomeDay = s.day();
  }

  public static String community(GameState s, String map) {
    if (map.equals("cafe") && s.shipped.contains("queue"))
      return "Ася: «С твоей очередью я теперь успеваю выйти поснимать».";
    if (map.equals("diner") && s.shipped.contains("refund"))
      return "Тамара: «Возвраты больше не съедают выручку. Спасибо за патч».";
    if (map.equals("diner") && s.shipped.contains("total"))
      return "Тамара узнаёт тебя: «Наш программист! Касса работает».";
    if (map.equals("park") && s.shipped.contains("booking"))
      return "Ника: «В репетиционной теперь порядок с бронированиями».";
    if (projectStage(s) == 3)
      return "В районном чате благодарят за бота. Кто-то уже нашёл потерянные ключи.";
    return "";
  }
}
END FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/model/Career.java

FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/model/Coding.java
