package life.ctrl.model;

import java.util.*;
import life.ctrl.bytescript.ByteScript;
import life.ctrl.content.LifeContent;

public final class Coding {
  private Coding() {}

  public record Report(boolean compiled, boolean passed, String message, List<String> cases) {}

  public static final class Revision {
    public String source, label;

    public Revision() {}

    public Revision(String source, String label) {
      this.source = source;
      this.label = label;
    }
  }

  public static String ledgerId(GameState s, String job) {
    return s.day() + ":" + job;
  }

  public static boolean paidToday(GameState s, String job) {
    return s.paidJobs.contains(ledgerId(s, job));
  }

  public static String accept(GameState s, LifeContent.Job j, int algorithm) {
    return accept(s, j, algorithm, false);
  }

  public static String accept(GameState s, LifeContent.Job j, int algorithm, boolean rush) {
    return accept(s, j, algorithm, rush, false);
  }

  /** Use tactile typing in the interactive IDE; direct model callers retain older semantics. */
  public static String accept(GameState s, LifeContent.Job j, int algorithm, boolean rush, boolean tactile) {
    if (!s.activeJob.isEmpty() && s.jobStage > 0 && s.jobStage < 5)
      return "Текущий проект сохранён. Продолжи его или отложи через историю версий.";
    String blocked = Career.blocked(s, j);
    if (!blocked.isEmpty()) return blocked;
    if (algorithm < 0 || algorithm >= j.initialSources.length) return "Выбери вариант из задания.";
    s.activeJob = j.id;
    s.activeLedger = ledgerId(s, j.id);
    s.algorithm = algorithm;
    s.codeSource = j.initialSources[algorithm];
    s.typedCount = tactile ? 0 : j.kind.equals("feature") ? 0 : s.codeSource.length();
    s.jobStage = 2;
    s.programPreviewed = false;
    s.rushJob = rush && !j.once;
    s.rushDeadline =
        s.rushJob
            ? s.rushPromises.computeIfAbsent(
                ledgerId(s, j.id), key -> s.minutes() + Career.duration(s, j) + 90)
            : -1;
    s.revisions.clear();
    s.customCases.clear();
    s.revisions.add(new Revision(s.codeSource, "Исходная версия · " + s.clock()));
    return "Проект открыт. Прочитай требование и проверь граничные случаи.";
  }

  public static void type(GameState s) {
    if (s.jobStage == 2) s.typedCount = Math.min(s.codeSource.length(), s.typedCount + 5);
  }

  public static void completeTyping(GameState s) {
    s.typedCount = s.codeSource.length();
  }

  public static Report test(GameState s, LifeContent.Job j) {
    if (!s.activeJob.equals(j.id))
      return new Report(false, false, "Этот заказ не открыт в редакторе.", List.of());
    if (s.typedCount < s.codeSource.length())
      return new Report(
          false, false, "Сначала допечатай исходник. Быстрые реакции не нужны.", List.of());
    try {
      var p = ByteScript.compile(s.codeSource);
      if (!p.name().equals(j.function))
        return new Report(true, false, "Клиент ожидает функцию " + j.function, List.of());
      List<String> results = new ArrayList<>();
      boolean passed = true;
      int maxOps = 0;
      for (var t : j.tests) {
        double actual = p.run(t.args);
        boolean ok = Math.abs(actual - t.expected) < .001;
        passed &= ok;
        maxOps = Math.max(maxOps, p.operations(t.args));
        results.add(
            Arrays.toString(t.args)
                + " → "
                + format(actual)
                + " / нужно "
                + format(t.expected)
                + (ok ? "  OK" : "  ОШИБКА"));
      }
      boolean fast = j.operationBudget == 0 || maxOps <= j.operationBudget;
      passed &= fast;
      s.jobStage = passed ? 3 : 2;
      return new Report(
          true,
          passed,
          !fast
              ? "Ответ верный, но операций "
                  + maxOps
                  + ". Бюджет: "
                  + j.operationBudget
                  + ". Открой профилировщик."
              : passed
                  ? "Все тесты пройдены. Проверь предпросмотр и отправляй."
                  : "Результат не совпал. Сравни вход, ответ и требование.",
          List.copyOf(results));
    } catch (ByteScript.Error e) {
      s.jobStage = 2;
      return new Report(
          false,
          false,
          (e.kind == ByteScript.Kind.SYNTAX ? "Синтаксис" : "Неизвестное имя")
              + ", строка "
              + e.line
              + ": "
              + e.getMessage(),
          List.of());
    } catch (ArithmeticException | IllegalArgumentException e) {
      s.jobStage = 2;
      return new Report(true, false, e.getMessage(), List.of());
    }
  }

  public static String format(double x) {
    return x == Math.rint(x)
        ? String.valueOf((long) x)
        : String.valueOf(Math.round(x * 100.0) / 100.0);
  }

  public static void repair(GameState s, LifeContent.Job j, int choice) {
    repair(s, j, choice, false);
  }

  public static void repair(GameState s, LifeContent.Job j, int choice, boolean tactile) {
    if (!s.activeJob.equals(j.id) || s.jobStage == 5 || choice < 0 || choice >= j.solutions.length)
      return;
    s.codeSource = j.solutions[choice];
    // The IDE requires a second typing phase after a patch is chosen.
    s.typedCount = tactile ? 0 : s.codeSource.length();
    s.jobStage = 2;
    s.programPreviewed = false;
  }

  public static String commit(GameState s) {
    if (s.jobStage < 2 || s.jobStage == 5) return "Сначала открой проект.";
    if (s.revisions.stream().anyMatch(r -> r.source.equals(s.codeSource)))
      return "Эта версия уже сохранена. Коммит фиксирует изменение, а не повторный клик.";
    s.revisions.add(new Revision(s.codeSource, "Коммит " + s.revisions.size() + " · " + s.clock()));
    if (s.revisions.size() > 8) s.revisions.remove(1);
    return "Коммит сохранён. Теперь можно экспериментировать и вернуться к нему.";
  }

  public static String restore(GameState s, int index) {
    if (index < 0 || index >= s.revisions.size() || s.jobStage == 5) return "Версия недоступна.";
    s.codeSource = s.revisions.get(index).source;
    s.typedCount = s.codeSource.length();
    s.jobStage = 2;
    s.programPreviewed = false;
    return "Версия восстановлена. Прогони тесты заново.";
  }

  public static String abandon(GameState s) {
    s.activeJob = "";
    s.activeLedger = "";
    s.codeSource = "";
    s.typedCount = 0;
    s.jobStage = 0;
    s.programPreviewed = false;
    s.revisions.clear();
    s.customCases.clear();
    s.rushJob = false;
    return "Заказ отложен без штрафа. Можно выбрать другой.";
  }

  public static String probe(GameState s, LifeContent.Job j, double[] inputs) {
    if (!s.activeJob.equals(j.id) || s.typedCount < s.codeSource.length())
      return "Сначала допечатай исходник.";
    if (inputs.length != j.seed.length
        || Arrays.stream(inputs).anyMatch(v -> !Double.isFinite(v) || Math.abs(v) > 10000))
      return "Ввод: конечные числа от −10000 до 10000.";
    try {
      var p = ByteScript.compile(s.codeSource);
      double actual = p.run(inputs), expected = ByteScript.compile(j.source).run(inputs);
      String key = Arrays.toString(inputs);
      boolean novel = Arrays.stream(j.tests).noneMatch(t -> Arrays.equals(t.args, inputs));
      if (novel && !s.customCases.contains(key) && s.customCases.size() < 20)
        s.customCases.add(key);
      return "Ответ "
          + format(actual)
          + " · нужно "
          + format(expected)
          + " · операций "
          + p.operations(inputs)
          + (Math.abs(actual - expected) < .001 ? " · OK" : " · ОШИБКА");
    } catch (RuntimeException e) {
      return "Запуск не удался: " + e.getMessage();
    }
  }

  public static double preview(GameState s, LifeContent.Job j, double input) {
    if (!test(s, j).passed) throw new IllegalStateException("Сначала исправь программу");
    double[] args = j.id.equals("total") ? new double[] {30, input} : j.seed.clone();
    args[args.length - 1] = input;
    s.programPreviewed = true;
    return ByteScript.compile(s.codeSource).run(args);
  }

  public static String deliver(GameState s, LifeContent.Job j) {
    if (!s.activeJob.equals(j.id) || !s.activeLedger.endsWith(":" + j.id))
      return "Сначала открой нужный заказ.";
    if (s.jobStage == 5
        || s.paidJobs.contains(s.activeLedger)
        || (j.once && s.shipped.contains(j.id))) return "Эта работа уже сдана.";
    if (!s.programPreviewed || !test(s, j).passed) return "Нужны пройденные тесты и предпросмотр.";
    if (!j.once && Career.paidCountToday(s) >= 3)
      return "Сегодня уже оплачены три заказа. Продолжи завтра; исходник сохранён.";
    if (s.money < j.cost)
      return "Для запуска нужно " + j.cost + " ₽. Код сохранён, можно вернуться после подработки.";
    int time = Career.duration(s, j), bonus = Career.bonus(s, j);
    boolean late = s.rushJob && s.minutes() + time > s.rushDeadline;
    Economy.spend(s, j.cost, "Свой бот: " + j.title);
    if (!j.once) {
      s.paidJobs.add(ledgerId(s, j.id));
      if (!s.activeLedger.equals(ledgerId(s, j.id))) s.paidJobs.add(s.activeLedger);
      Economy.earn(s, j.pay + bonus, "Заказ: " + j.title);
      s.completedOrders++;
    }
    if (!s.shipped.contains(j.id)) s.shipped.add(j.id);
    s.codingXp += j.xp;
    s.jobStage = 5;
    Life.advance(s, time);
    s.energy = Math.max(0, s.energy - (s.upgrades.contains("chair") ? 2 : 6));
    s.mood = Math.min(100, s.mood + 8);
    s.lastClientMessage = j.feedback;
    return (j.once
            ? "Этап запущен. Расход: " + j.cost + " ₽."
            : "Клиент принял работу: +"
                + (j.pay + bonus)
                + " ₽"
                + (bonus > 0 ? " (бонус " + bonus + " ₽)" : "")
                + ".")
        + " Прошло "
        + time
        + " мин. Опыт +"
        + j.xp
        + ".\n\n"
        + j.feedback
        + (late ? "\n\nСрочный бонус истёк; базовая оплата сохранена." : "");
  }
}

FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/model/Economy.java

package life.ctrl.model;

public final class Economy {
  private Economy() {}

  public static final int WEEKLY_RENT = 3000;

  public static final class Entry {
    public int day, amount, balance;
    public String reason;

    public Entry() {}

    public Entry(GameState s, int amount, String reason) {
      day = s.day();
      this.amount = amount;
      balance = s.money;
      this.reason = reason;
    }
  }

  public static boolean spend(GameState s, int amount, String reason) {
    if (amount < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Отрицательная цена");
    if (s.money < amount) return false;
    if (amount == 0) return true;
    s.money -= amount;
    record(s, -amount, reason);
    return true;
  }

  public static void earn(GameState s, int amount, String reason) {
    if (amount < 0 || amount > 1000000 || s.money > Integer.MAX_VALUE - amount)
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("Сумма");
    s.money += amount;
    record(s, amount, reason);
  }

  private static void record(GameState s, int amount, String reason) {
    s.transactions.add(new Entry(s, amount, reason));
    if (s.transactions.size() > 100) s.transactions.remove(0);
  }

  public static void rent(GameState s) {
    while (s.day() > s.rentThroughDay) {
      int paid = Math.min(WEEKLY_RENT, s.money);
      spend(s, paid, "Комната: неделя аренды");
      s.rentDebt += WEEKLY_RENT - paid;
      s.rentThroughDay += 7;
    }
  }

  public static String repay(GameState s) {
    int amount = Math.min(s.rentDebt, s.money);
    if (amount == 0) return "Сейчас нечего погашать.";
    spend(s, amount, "Погашение долга за комнату");
    s.rentDebt -= amount;
    return "Долг уменьшен на " + amount + " ₽. Осталось: " + s.rentDebt + " ₽.";
  }
}
END FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/model/Economy.java

package life.ctrl.model;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;

/** Only durable values: no graphics, input state, or transient UI. */
public final class GameState {
  public String name = "Саша", map = "room";
  public float x = 240, y = 190;
  public int direction = 0, outfit = 0;
  public int money = 10000, rentDebt = 0, rentThroughDay = 3;
  public double health = 85, hunger = 42, energy = 62, mood = 45, gameMinutes = 17 * 60 + 30;
  public boolean introSeen, ateFirstMeal, firstDateFinished;
  public String trackedGirl = "mira",
      partnerId = "",
      plannedGirl = "",
      plannedVenue = "",
      activeJob = "",
      codeSource = "",
      activeLedger = "";
  public int plannedDay = -1, typedCount = 0, jobStage = 0, algorithm = 0, goalChoice = 0;
  public boolean programPreviewed;
  public int lastCourierMinute = -10000, lastFreeMealDay = -1;
  public ArrayList<String> paidJobs = new ArrayList<>();
  public ArrayList<Economy.Entry> transactions = new ArrayList<>();
  public HashMap<String, Integer> items = new HashMap<>();
  public HashMap<String, Relationship> relationships = new HashMap<>();
  public int codingXp, completedOrders, rushDeadline = -1, projectIncomeDay = -1;
  public boolean rushJob;
  public ArrayList<String> shipped = new ArrayList<>(),
      upgrades = new ArrayList<>(),
      customCases = new ArrayList<>();
  public ArrayList<Coding.Revision> revisions = new ArrayList<>();
  public HashMap<String, Integer> rushPromises = new HashMap<>();
  public String lastClientMessage = "", workNotice = "";
  public double elapsed = 0;
  public boolean inspectedPc,
      metMira,
      metArkady,
      metLev,
      askedLetter,
      hasLetter,
      deliveredLetter,
      wateredPlant,
      unpacked,
      signedMailbox,
      readBoard;
  public ArrayList<String> visited = new ArrayList<>();

  public GameState() {
    visited.add("room");
  }

  public int minutes() {
    return (int) gameMinutes;
  }

  public int day() {
    return 1 + minutes() / 1440;
  }

  public int count(String id) {
    return items.getOrDefault(id, 0);
  }

  public Relationship relation(String id) {
    return relationships.computeIfAbsent(id, k -> new Relationship());
  }

  public String clock() {
    int m = minutes() % 1440;
    return "%02d:%02d".formatted(m / 60, m % 60);
  }

  public String objective() {
    if (paidJobs.isEmpty())
      return inspectedPc
          ? "Закончи первый заказ в KeyCode. Оплата: 650 ₽."
          : "Подойди к столу и включи компьютер [E].";
    if (!ateFirstMeal) return "Зайди в «Ложку» и поешь у стойки [E].";
    if (!plannedGirl.isEmpty()) return "Свидание назначено. Доберись до места встречи.";
    if (!firstDateFinished) return "Познакомься с Мирой: разговор → приглашение на свидание.";
    if (energy < 30) return "Вернись в комнату и выспись [E у кровати].";
    return switch (goalChoice) {
      case 1 -> "Заработай кодингом: новые заказы на домашнем ПК.";
      case 2 -> "Проведи время с девушкой: контакты [R].";
      default -> "Твоя жизнь: заказы, знакомства, еда и новые дни.";
    };
  }
}

package life.ctrl.model;

import life.ctrl.content.LifeContent;

/** Economy and needs have a single mutation point, including explicit time costs. */
public final class Life {
  private Life() {}

  public static void advance(GameState s, double minutes) {
    if (!Double.isFinite(minutes) || minutes < 0 || minutes > 1440)
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("Время");
    s.gameMinutes += minutes;
    s.hunger = Math.max(0, s.hunger - minutes * .065);
    s.energy = Math.max(0, s.energy - minutes * .045);
    if (s.hunger < 10) s.health = Math.max(15, s.health - minutes * .012);
    Career.settleProject(s);
    Economy.rent(s);
  }

  public static String buy(GameState s, LifeContent.Item item) {
    if (s.money < item.price)
      return "Не хватает денег. Подработка у доски во дворе доступна всегда.";
    Economy.spend(s, item.price, "Покупка: " + item.name);
    s.items.merge(item.id, 1, Integer::sum);
    return "Куплено: " + item.name + ". [I] — съесть или посмотреть подарки.";
  }

  public static String eat(GameState s, LifeContent.Item item) {
    if (!item.kind.equals("food") || s.count(item.id) < 1) return "Сначала купи еду у стойки.";
    consume(s, item.id);
    s.hunger = Math.min(100, s.hunger + item.hunger);
    s.energy = Math.min(100, s.energy + item.energy);
    s.health = Math.min(100, s.health + 3);
    s.ateFirstMeal = true;
    advance(s, 10);
    return "Ты поел(а). Сытость +" + item.hunger + ", энергия +" + item.energy + ".";
  }

  public static void consume(GameState s, String item) {
    int n = s.count(item);
    if (n < 1) throw new IllegalStateException("Нет предмета");
    if (n == 1) s.items.remove(item);
    else s.items.put(item, n - 1);
  }

  public static void sleep(GameState s) {
    int wait = (480 - s.minutes() % 1440 + 1440) % 1440;
    if (wait == 0) wait = 1440;
    advance(s, wait);
    s.energy = 96;
    s.health = Math.min(100, s.health + 12);
    s.mood = Math.min(100, s.mood + 8);
  }

  public static String courier(GameState s) {
    if (s.minutes() - s.lastCourierMinute < 120)
      return "Следующая доставка будет через "
          + (120 - (s.minutes() - s.lastCourierMinute))
          + " мин. Можно отдохнуть на лавке.";
    s.lastCourierMinute = s.minutes();
    advance(s, 35);
    Economy.earn(s, 240, "Доставка посылки");
    s.energy = Math.max(0, s.energy - 6);
    s.hunger = Math.max(0, s.hunger - 3);
    return "Ты доставил(а) посылку в соседний дом: +240 ₽, прошло 35 минут.";
  }

  public static String freeMeal(GameState s) {
    if (s.lastFreeMealDay == s.day())
      return "Сегодня ты уже получил(а) бесплатный суп. Завтра будет новая порция.";
    if (s.money >= 250) return "Бесплатный суп оставляют тем, у кого меньше 250 ₽.";
    s.lastFreeMealDay = s.day();
    s.hunger = Math.min(100, s.hunger + 45);
    s.ateFirstMeal = true;
    return "Горячий суп за счёт заведения. Здесь помогают в трудные дни.";
  }
}
END FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/model/Life.java

FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/model/Navigation.java

package life.ctrl.model;

import java.util.*;
import life.ctrl.content.*;

/** One explicit destination, connected by real doors and a walkable path. */
public final class Navigation {
  public record Goal(String map, float x, float y, String title, String hint) {}

  private Navigation() {}

  private static Goal prop(Content c, String map, String id, String title, String hint) {
    for (var p : c.map(map).objects)
      if (p.id.equals(id)) return new Goal(map, p.x, p.y, title, hint);
    throw new IllegalArgumentException(id);
  }

  public static Goal goal(World w, LifeContent life) {
    var s = w.state;
    var c = w.content;
    if (!s.plannedGirl.isEmpty())
      return prop(
          c,
          s.plannedVenue,
          "date",
          "Встреча: " + life.girl(s.plannedGirl).name,
          "Иди к золотой метке. [E] у столика начнёт свидание.");
    if (s.paidJobs.isEmpty())
      return prop(
          c,
          "room",
          "pc",
          "Первый заработок: касса для «Ложки»",
          "[E] у ПК → выбрать правило → набрать код → тесты → оплата.");
    if ((s.hunger < 25 || !s.ateFirstMeal)
        && Arrays.stream(life.items).anyMatch(i -> i.kind.equals("food") && s.count(i.id) > 0))
      return new Goal(
          s.map,
          s.x,
          s.y,
          "Еда куплена — открой карманы [I]",
          "Нажми I и выбери «съесть». Купленная еда пока лежит в сумке.");
    if (s.hunger < 25 || !s.ateFirstMeal)
      return prop(
          c,
          "diner",
          "counter",
          "Поесть в «Ложке»",
          "[E] у стойки. Суп: 220 ₽. Съесть можно в карманах [I].");
    if (s.energy < 25 || (s.firstDateFinished && s.day() == 1 && s.goalChoice == 0))
      return prop(
          c,
          "room",
          "bed",
          "Вернуться в комнату и поспать",
          "[E] у кровати. Сон восстановит силы и начнёт новый день.");
    if (!s.firstDateFinished || s.goalChoice == 2) {
      String id = s.trackedGirl.isEmpty() ? "mira" : s.trackedGirl;
      var g = life.girl(id);
      var r = s.relation(id);
      if (r.lastTalkDay == s.day() && (r.affinity < 12 || r.trust < 8))
        return prop(
            c,
            "room",
            "bed",
            "Дайте друг другу время. Встретьтесь завтра.",
            "[E] у кровати: начать новый день. Неудачная реплика не закрывает отношения.");
      for (var map : c.maps)
        for (var n : map.npcs)
          if (n.id.equals(id)) {
            var a = new World.Actor();
            w.position(n, a);
            return new Goal(
                map.id,
                a.x,
                a.y,
                r.met ? "Пообщаться: " + g.name : "Познакомиться: " + g.name,
                r.met
                    ? "Разговор по душам → приглашение. Сквер бесплатный."
                    : "Подойди к девушке и нажми [E]. Никто не ждёт идеальной реплики.");
          }
    }
    if (Career.paidCountToday(s) >= 3)
      return prop(
          c,
          "room",
          "bed",
          "Работа на сегодня закончена",
          "Три заказа сданы. Можно сделать свой проект, встретиться с девушкой или поспать.");
    return prop(
        c,
        "room",
        "pc",
        "Новые заказы: заработай на свою жизнь",
        "На ПК три заказа в день. [B] — деньги и ближайшая аренда.");
  }

  public static Goal localGoal(World w, Goal finalGoal) {
    if (w.state.map.equals(finalGoal.map)) return finalGoal;
    record Hop(String map, Content.Prop first) {}
    ArrayDeque<Hop> q = new ArrayDeque<>();
    Set<String> seen = new HashSet<>();
    q.add(new Hop(w.state.map, null));
    while (!q.isEmpty()) {
      var h = q.remove();
      if (!seen.add(h.map)) continue;
      for (var p : w.content.map(h.map).objects)
        if (p.action.equals("door")) {
          var first = h.first == null ? p : h.first;
          if (p.target.equals(finalGoal.map))
            return new Goal(
                w.state.map,
                first.x,
                first.y,
                finalGoal.title,
                "Следующий проход: " + w.content.map(first.target).name);
          q.add(new Hop(p.target, first));
        }
    }
    return finalGoal;
  }

  public static List<float[]> route(World w, float targetX, float targetY) {
    int grid = 4, cols = w.map().width / grid, rows = w.map().height / grid;
    int start = Math.round(w.state.y / grid) * cols + Math.round(w.state.x / grid);
    int[] previous = new int[cols * rows];
    Arrays.fill(previous, -1);
    if (start < 0 || start >= previous.length) return List.of();
    previous[start] = start;
    ArrayDeque<Integer> todo = new ArrayDeque<>();
    todo.add(start);
    int goal = -1;
    while (!todo.isEmpty()) {
      int at = todo.remove(), x = at % cols, y = at / cols;
      if (Math.hypot(x * grid - targetX, y * grid - targetY) < 17) {
        goal = at;
        break;
      }
      for (int[] d : DIRS) {
        int nx = x + d[0], ny = y + d[1];
        if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= cols || ny >= rows) continue;
        int next = ny * cols + nx;
        if (previous[next] < 0
            && !w.blocked(nx * grid - 2, ny * grid - 2)
            && !w.blocked(nx * grid + 2, ny * grid - 2)
            && !w.blocked(nx * grid - 2, ny * grid + 2)
            && !w.blocked(nx * grid + 2, ny * grid + 2)) {
          previous[next] = at;
          todo.add(next);
        }
      }
    }
    if (goal < 0) return List.of();
    ArrayList<float[]> path = new ArrayList<>();
    for (int p = goal; p != start; p = previous[p])
      path.add(new float[] {p % cols * grid, p / cols * grid});
    Collections.reverse(path);
    return path;
  }

  private static final int[][] DIRS = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};
}

package life.ctrl.model;

/** Durable relationship history. Affection cannot substitute for trust or consent. */
public final class Relationship {
  public boolean met;
  public int affinity, trust, dates, storyChapter, sharedNights;
  public int lastTalkDay = -1,
      lastGiftDay = -1,
      lastDateDay = -1,
      lastNightDay = -1,
      committedDay = -1;
  public String lastTopic = "", memory = "Вы ещё не знакомы.";
}

package life.ctrl.model;

import com.badlogic.gdx.Input.Keys;
import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.Json;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.*;
import java.util.HashSet;
import life.ctrl.save.SaveStore;

public final class Settings {
  public float music = .35f, sfx = .6f;
  public boolean reducedEffects = false, fullscreen = false;
  public int scale = 3;
  public int up = Keys.W,
      down = Keys.S,
      left = Keys.A,
      right = Keys.D,
      interact = Keys.E,
      journal = Keys.J;

  public int key(int index) {
    return switch (index) {
      case 0 -> up;
      case 1 -> down;
      case 2 -> left;
      case 3 -> right;
      case 4 -> interact;
      default -> journal;
    };
  }

  public boolean bind(int index, int key) {
    if (key == Keys.ESCAPE
        || key == Keys.ENTER
        || key == Keys.F11
        || key == Keys.F5
        || key == Keys.R
        || key == Keys.B
        || key == Keys.I
        || key == Keys.M
        || key == Keys.TAB
        || key == Keys.UNKNOWN
        || key == Keys.UP
        || key == Keys.DOWN
        || key == Keys.LEFT
        || key == Keys.RIGHT) return false;
    for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) if (i != index && key(i) == key) return false;
    switch (index) {
      case 0 -> up = key;
      case 1 -> down = key;
      case 2 -> left = key;
      case 3 -> right = key;
      case 4 -> interact = key;
      case 5 -> journal = key;
      default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException();
    }
    return true;
  }

  public static Settings load(Path dir) {
    try {
      Settings s =
          new Json().fromJson(Settings.class, Files.readString(dir.resolve("settings.json")));
      if (s == null
          || !Float.isFinite(s.music)
          || !Float.isFinite(s.sfx)
          || s.music < 0
          || s.music > 1
          || s.sfx < 0
          || s.sfx > 1
          || s.scale < 2
          || s.scale > 4) throw new IOException();
      HashSet<Integer> keys = new HashSet<>();
      for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++)
        if (s.key(i) <= 0 || s.key(i) > 255 || !keys.add(s.key(i)) || !s.bind(i, s.key(i)))
          throw new IOException();
      return s;
    } catch (Exception e) {
      return new Settings();
    }
  }

  public void save(Path dir) throws IOException {
    Files.createDirectories(dir);
    Path tmp = dir.resolve("settings.tmp.json");
    Files.writeString(tmp, new Json().prettyPrint(this));
    SaveStore.replace(tmp, dir.resolve("settings.json"));
  }
}
END FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/model/Settings.java

package life.ctrl.model;

import life.ctrl.content.LifeContent;

/** Every scene is initiated by the player and subject to the other adult's own preferences. */
public final class Social {
  private Social() {}

  private static void change(Relationship r, int affection, int trust) {
    r.affinity = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, r.affinity + affection));
    r.trust = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, r.trust + trust));
  }

  public static String meet(GameState s, LifeContent.Girl g) {
    var r = s.relation(g.id);
    r.met = true;
    if (g.id.equals("mira")) s.metMira = true;
    r.memory = g.introduction;
    return g.introduction;
  }

  public static String talk(GameState s, LifeContent.Girl g, int topic) {
    var r = s.relation(g.id);
    if (!r.met) meet(s, g);
    if (r.lastTalkDay == s.day())
      return "На сегодня вы уже поговорили по душам. Новая беседа — завтра; сейчас можно погулять"
          + " или выбрать подарок.";
    var t = g.topics[topic];
    r.lastTalkDay = s.day();
    r.lastTopic = t.id;
    change(r, t.affinity, t.trust);
    Life.advance(s, 15);
    r.memory = t.response;
    return t.response;
  }

  public static String gift(GameState s, LifeContent.Girl g, LifeContent.Item item) {
    var r = s.relation(g.id);
    if (!r.met) return "Сначала познакомьтесь.";
    if (r.lastGiftDay == s.day())
      return "Она благодарит, но просит оставить следующий подарок на другой день.";
    if (s.count(item.id) < 1) return "У тебя нет этого подарка. Купить можно в кофейне.";
    if (!item.kind.equals("gift"))
      return "Это еда для тебя. Для подарка выбери предмет с пометкой «подарок».";
    Life.consume(s, item.id);
    r.lastGiftDay = s.day();
    boolean likes = java.util.Arrays.asList(g.favoriteGifts).contains(item.id);
    change(r, likes ? 7 : 1, likes ? 3 : 0);
    r.memory =
        likes
            ? "Ты запомнил(а), что мне нравится. Спасибо — это очень приятно."
            : "Спасибо. Я не очень увлекаюсь такими вещами, но ценю внимание.";
    return r.memory;
  }

  public static String invite(GameState s, LifeContent.Girl g, String venue) {
    var r = s.relation(g.id);
    if (!s.plannedGirl.isEmpty()) return "У тебя уже назначена встреча. Посмотри цель на экране.";
    if (!r.met || r.affinity < 12 || r.trust < 8)
      return g.notReady + " Поговорите по душам и узнайте её интересы.";
    if (r.lastDateDay == s.day())
      return "Сегодня вы уже виделись. Она предлагает встретиться завтра.";
    if (!java.util.Arrays.asList(g.venues).contains(venue))
      return "Это место ей не подходит. Выбери другой вариант.";
    if (!s.partnerId.isEmpty() && !s.partnerId.equals(g.id))
      return "Сначала обсуди завершение нынешних отношений. Здесь знакомства не превращаются в"
          + " тайные параллельные романы.";
    s.plannedGirl = g.id;
    s.plannedVenue = venue;
    s.plannedDay = s.day();
    return "Она согласилась. Место: "
        + venueName(venue)
        + ". Иди к отмеченному столику; она дождётся до конца дня.";
  }

  public static String venueName(String id) {
    return switch (id) {
      case "park" -> "сквер, столик у дерева";
      case "cafe" -> "кофейня «Плёнка»";
      default -> "забегаловка «Ложка»";
    };
  }

  public static boolean canStartDate(GameState s, String venue) {
    return !s.plannedGirl.isEmpty() && s.plannedVenue.equals(venue) && s.plannedDay == s.day();
  }

  public static String date(GameState s, LifeContent.Girl g, int choice) {
    var r = s.relation(g.id);
    if (!s.plannedGirl.equals(g.id) || s.plannedDay != s.day())
      return "Сначала договоритесь о встрече.";
    if (!s.map.equals(s.plannedVenue))
      return "Сначала доберись до места встречи. Маршрут отмечен золотыми точками.";
    int cost = s.plannedVenue.equals("park") ? 0 : 850;
    if (s.money < cost)
      return "Для этой встречи нужно 850 ₽. Можно перенести её в сквер — прогулка бесплатна.";
    var c = g.dateChoices[choice];
    Economy.spend(s, cost, "Свидание: " + g.name);
    boolean food = !s.plannedVenue.equals("park");
    s.plannedGirl = "";
    s.plannedVenue = "";
    r.lastDateDay = s.day();
    r.dates++;
    change(r, c.affinity, c.trust);
    Life.advance(s, 90);
    if (food) {
      s.hunger = Math.min(100, s.hunger + 35);
      s.ateFirstMeal = true;
    }
    s.firstDateFinished = true;
    s.mood = Math.min(100, s.mood + 8);
    if (r.dates >= 2 && r.trust >= 28 && r.storyChapter == 0) {
      r.storyChapter = 1;
      r.memory = g.story;
      return c.response + "\n\n" + g.story;
    }
    r.memory = c.response;
    return c.response;
  }

  public static String commit(GameState s, LifeContent.Girl g) {
    var r = s.relation(g.id);
    if (!s.partnerId.isEmpty() && !s.partnerId.equals(g.id))
      return "Сначала заверши нынешние отношения.";
    if (r.dates < 3 || r.trust < 40 || r.affinity < 40)
      return "Ей нужно больше времени: хотя бы три свидания и достаточно доверия. Подарки не"
          + " заменяют общения.";
    if (s.partnerId.equals(g.id)) return "Вы уже вместе.";
    s.partnerId = g.id;
    r.committedDay = s.day();
    r.memory = g.commitLine;
    return g.commitLine;
  }

  public static String breakup(GameState s, LifeContent.Girl g) {
    if (!s.partnerId.equals(g.id)) return "Вы не состоите в отношениях.";
    s.partnerId = "";
    var r = s.relation(g.id);
    change(r, -12, -8);
    r.committedDay = -1;
    r.memory = "Вы честно обсудили расставание. Ей нужно время.";
    return r.memory;
  }

  public static boolean canShareNight(GameState s, LifeContent.Girl g) {
    var r = s.relation(g.id);
    return s.partnerId.equals(g.id)
        && r.dates >= 4
        && r.trust >= 60
        && r.affinity >= 60
        && s.day() > r.committedDay
        && r.lastNightDay != s.day()
        && (s.minutes() % 1440 >= 18 * 60 || s.minutes() % 1440 < 2 * 60);
  }

  public static String shareNight(GameState s, LifeContent.Girl g, boolean mutualYes) {
    var r = s.relation(g.id);
    if (!mutualYes) return "Вы решаете не спешить. Можно просто побыть рядом.";
    if (!s.map.equals("room")) return "Приглашение можно обсудить дома, у кровати.";
    if (!canShareNight(s, g)) return g.notReady;
    r.lastNightDay = s.day();
    r.sharedNights++;
    change(r, 3, 3);
    Life.sleep(s);
    s.mood = Math.min(100, s.mood + 10);
    r.memory = "Утром вы проснулись рядом. Это осталось вашим личным воспоминанием.";
    return r.memory;
  }

  public static void expire(GameState s) {
    if (!s.plannedGirl.isEmpty() && s.day() > s.plannedDay) {
      var r = s.relation(s.plannedGirl);
      change(r, 0, -2);
      r.memory = "Встреча не состоялась. Она предлагает договориться в другой день.";
      s.plannedGirl = "";
      s.plannedVenue = "";
    }
  }
}
END FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/model/Social.java

FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/model/World.java

package life.ctrl.model;

import java.util.*;
import life.ctrl.content.Content;

public final class World {
  public static final float STEP = 1f / 60f, SPEED = 64f, RADIUS = 4f;
  public final Content content;
  public final GameState state;
  public boolean moving;
  private String cacheMap = "", cacheGirl = "", cacheVenue = "";
  private final ArrayList<Content.Npc> activeNpcs = new ArrayList<>();

  public List<Content.Npc> npcs() {
    if (!cacheMap.equals(state.map)
        || !cacheGirl.equals(state.plannedGirl)
        || !cacheVenue.equals(state.plannedVenue)) {
      cacheMap = state.map;
      cacheGirl = state.plannedGirl;
      cacheVenue = state.plannedVenue;
      activeNpcs.clear();
      for (var m : content.maps)
        for (var n : m.npcs) {
          boolean meeting = n.id.equals(state.plannedGirl);
          if (meeting ? state.map.equals(state.plannedVenue) : m.id.equals(state.map))
            activeNpcs.add(n);
        }
    }
    return activeNpcs;
  }

  public World(Content content, GameState state) {
    this.content = content;
    this.state = state;
    validateState();
  }

  public Content.MapDef map() {
    return content.map(state.map);
  }

  public void validateState() {
    if (state.name == null
        || state.name.isBlank()
        || state.name.length() > 18
        || !Float.isFinite(state.x)
        || !Float.isFinite(state.y)
        || !Double.isFinite(state.elapsed)
        || state.elapsed < 0
        || state.elapsed > 1e9
        || state.direction < 0
        || state.direction > 3
        || state.outfit < 0
        || state.outfit > 2
        || state.visited == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Некорректное сохранение");
    if (state.money < 0
        || state.rentDebt < 0
        || state.rentThroughDay < 3
        || !validNeed(state.health)
        || !validNeed(state.hunger)
        || !validNeed(state.energy)
        || !validNeed(state.mood)
        || !Double.isFinite(state.gameMinutes)
        || state.gameMinutes < 0
        || state.gameMinutes > 1e7
        || state.items == null
        || state.relationships == null
        || state.paidJobs == null
        || state.transactions == null
        || state.activeJob == null
        || state.activeLedger == null
        || state.codeSource == null
        || state.codeSource.length() > 4096
        || state.typedCount < 0
        || state.typedCount > state.codeSource.length()
        || state.partnerId == null
        || state.plannedGirl == null
        || state.plannedVenue == null
        || state.trackedGirl == null
        || state.jobStage < 0
        || state.jobStage > 5) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Некорректные показатели");
    if (state.codingXp < 0
        || state.completedOrders < 0
        || state.shipped == null
        || state.upgrades == null
        || state.customCases == null
        || state.revisions == null
        || state.revisions.size() > 8
        || state.customCases.size() > 20
        || state.rushPromises == null
        || state.lastClientMessage == null
        || state.workNotice == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Некорректная карьера");
    for (var rev : state.revisions)
      if (rev == null || rev.source == null || rev.source.length() > 4096 || rev.label == null)
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Некорректная версия проекта");
    for (var item : state.items.entrySet())
      if (item.getKey() == null || item.getValue() == null || item.getValue() <= 0)
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Некорректный инвентарь");
    for (var relation : state.relationships.values())
      if (relation == null
          || relation.affinity < 0
          || relation.affinity > 100
          || relation.trust < 0
          || relation.trust > 100
          || relation.dates < 0
          || relation.sharedNights < 0
          || relation.memory == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Некорректные отношения");
    if (blocked(state.x, state.y))
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("Персонаж внутри препятствия");
    if (state.hasLetter && state.deliveredLetter)
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("Противоречивое состояние письма");
  }

  private static boolean validNeed(double value) {
    return Double.isFinite(value) && value >= 0 && value <= 100;
  }

  public void tick(float dx, float dy) {
    state.elapsed += STEP;
    Life.advance(state, STEP * .5);
    Social.expire(state);
    float length = (float) Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    moving = length > 0;
    if (!moving) return;
    dx = dx / length * SPEED * STEP;
    dy = dy / length * SPEED * STEP;
    state.direction = Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy) ? (dx < 0 ? 1 : 2) : (dy < 0 ? 3 : 0);
    if (!blocked(state.x + dx, state.y)) state.x += dx;
    if (!blocked(state.x, state.y + dy)) state.y += dy;
  }

  public boolean blocked(float x, float y) {
    var m = map();
    if (x < RADIUS || y < RADIUS || x > m.width - RADIUS || y > m.height - RADIUS) return true;
    for (var r : m.solids) if (intersects(x, y, r)) return true;
    for (var p : m.objects) if (p.solid != null && intersects(x, y, p.solid)) return true;
    return false;
  }

  public static boolean intersects(float x, float y, float[] r) {
    return x + RADIUS > r[0] && x - RADIUS < r[0] + r[2] && y + 2 > r[1] && y - 4 < r[1] + r[3];
  }

  public boolean visible(Content.Prop p) {
    return !p.id.equals("letter") || (!state.hasLetter && !state.deliveredLetter);
  }

  public Content.Prop nearestProp() {
    Content.Prop found = null;
    double best = 29;
    for (var p : map().objects) {
      if (p.action.equals("decoration") || !visible(p)) continue;
      double dist = Math.hypot(state.x - p.x, state.y - p.y);
      if (dist < best) {
        best = dist;
        found = p;
      }
    }
    return found;
  }

  public static final class Actor {
    public float x, y;
    public int direction;
    public boolean moving;
  }

  public void position(Content.Npc n, Actor out) {
    if (n.id.equals(state.plannedGirl) && state.map.equals(state.plannedVenue)) {
      out.x = state.map.equals("park") ? 264 : 328;
      out.y = state.map.equals("park") ? 155 : 216;
      out.direction = 1;
      out.moving = false;
      return;
    }
    // A loop with a four-second pause at every waypoint; derived from saved world time.
    double duration = 0;
    int count = n.route.length;
    for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
      duration +=
          4
              + Math.hypot(
                      n.route[(i + 1) % count][0] - n.route[i][0],
                      n.route[(i + 1) % count][1] - n.route[i][1])
                  / 18;
    double time = state.elapsed % duration;
    for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      float[] a = n.route[i], b = n.route[(i + 1) % count];
      double travel = Math.hypot(b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]) / 18;
      if (time < 4 + travel) {
        float t = (float) Math.max(0, (time - 4) / travel);
        out.x = a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * t;
        out.y = a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * t;
        out.direction =
            time < 4
                ? 0
                : Math.abs(b[0] - a[0]) > Math.abs(b[1] - a[1])
                    ? (b[0] < a[0] ? 1 : 2)
                    : (b[1] < a[1] ? 3 : 0);
        out.moving = time >= 4;
        return;
      }
      time -= 4 + travel;
    }
  }

  public Content.Npc nearestNpc() {
    Content.Npc found = null;
    double best = 28;
    Actor a = new Actor();
    for (var n : npcs()) {
      position(n, a);
      double dist = Math.hypot(state.x - a.x, state.y - a.y);
      if (dist < best) {
        best = dist;
        found = n;
      }
    }
    return found;
  }

  /** Nearby fixtures win over a passing NPC, consistently in the prompt and input handler. */
  public Content.Npc interactionNpc() {
    var prop = nearestProp();
    if (prop != null
        && Math.hypot(state.x - prop.x, state.y - prop.y) < 21
        && Set.of("door", "shop", "computer", "rest", "date").contains(prop.action)) return null;
    return nearestNpc();
  }

  public void enter(Content.Prop p) {
    if (!p.action.equals("door")) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Это не дверь");
    String old = state.map;
    float x = state.x, y = state.y;
    state.map = p.target;
    state.x = p.targetX;
    state.y = p.targetY;
    if (blocked(state.x, state.y)) {
      state.map = old;
      state.x = x;
      state.y = y;
      throw new IllegalStateException("Выход двери заблокирован");
    }
    if (!state.visited.contains(state.map)) state.visited.add(state.map);
    state.direction = p.target.equals("room") ? 3 : 0;
    moving = false;
  }

  public String npcDialogue(Content.Npc npc) {
    return switch (npc.id) {
      case "mira" -> state.hasLetter ? "miraFound" : state.deliveredLetter ? "miraAgain" : "mira";
      case "arkady" -> state.metArkady ? "arkadyAgain" : "arkady";
      default -> npc.dialogue;
    };
  }

  public String propDialogue(Content.Prop p) {
    return switch (p.action) {
      case "computer" -> "pc";
      case "plant" -> state.wateredPlant ? "plantAgain" : "plant";
      case "boxes" -> state.unpacked ? "boxesAgain" : "boxes";
      case "post" -> state.signedMailbox ? "postAgain" : "post";
      default -> p.action;
    };
  }

  public void effect(String effect) {
    switch (effect) {
      case "meetMira" -> state.metMira = true;
      case "meetArkady" -> state.metArkady = true;
      case "meetLev" -> state.metLev = true;
      case "inspectPc" -> state.inspectedPc = true;
      case "askLetter" -> {
        state.metMira = true;
        state.askedLetter = true;
      }
      case "takeLetter" -> {
        if (!state.deliveredLetter) state.hasLetter = true;
      }
      case "deliverLetter" -> {
        if (state.hasLetter) {
          state.hasLetter = false;
          state.deliveredLetter = true;
          state.metMira = true;
        }
      }
      case "waterPlant" -> state.wateredPlant = true;
      case "unpack" -> state.unpacked = true;
      case "signMailbox" -> state.signedMailbox = true;
      case "readBoard" -> state.readBoard = true;
      case "rest" -> state.elapsed += 30;
      case "water", "" -> {}
      default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException("Неизвестный эффект: " + effect);
    }
  }
}
END FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/model/World.java

FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/save/SaveStore.java

package life.ctrl.save;

import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.Json;
import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.JsonWriter;
import java.io.*;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.*;
import life.ctrl.content.Content;
import life.ctrl.model.*;

/** Validated, atomic single-slot saves with a previous-good backup. */
public final class SaveStore {
  public static final int VERSION = 4;

  public static final class Envelope {
    public int version = VERSION;
    public GameState state;

    public Envelope() {}

    public Envelope(GameState s) {
      state = s;
    }
  }

  public record Loaded(GameState state, boolean fromBackup) {}

  private final Path directory;
  private final Content content;

  public SaveStore(Path directory, Content content) {
    this.directory = directory;
    this.content = content;
  }

  public static Path defaultDirectory() {
    String override = System.getProperty("ctrl.dataDir");
    if (override != null) return Path.of(override);
    String base =
        System.getProperty("os.name").startsWith("Windows")
            ? System.getenv("LOCALAPPDATA")
            : System.getenv("XDG_DATA_HOME");
    if (base == null || base.isBlank())
      base = Path.of(System.getProperty("user.home"), ".local", "share").toString();
    return Path.of(base, "CtrlLife");
  }

  private Json json() {
    Json j = new Json();
    j.setOutputType(JsonWriter.OutputType.json);
    j.setUsePrototypes(false);
    return j;
  }

  public boolean exists() {
    return Files.isRegularFile(directory.resolve("save.json"))
        || Files.isRegularFile(directory.resolve("save.backup.json"));
  }

  private GameState read(Path file) throws IOException {
    if (Files.size(file) > 65536) throw new IOException("Файл сохранения слишком большой");
    try {
      Envelope e = json().fromJson(Envelope.class, Files.readString(file, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
      if (e == null || (e.version < 1 || e.version > VERSION) || e.state == null)
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Версия сохранения не поддерживается");
      if (e.version == 1) {
        e.state.gameMinutes = 17 * 60 + 30 + e.state.elapsed / 2;
        var m = content.map(e.state.map);
        // The cutaway room and furniture changed. Old positions get a safe entrance.
        if (e.state.map.equals("room")) {
          e.state.x = m.spawnX;
          e.state.y = m.spawnY;
        }
        try {
          new World(content, e.state);
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
          e.state.x = m.spawnX;
          e.state.y = m.spawnY;
        }
      }
      if (e.version < 3) {
        e.state.completedOrders = e.state.paidJobs.size();
        e.state.codingXp = e.state.completedOrders;
        for (var ledger : e.state.paidJobs) {
          String id = ledger.substring(ledger.indexOf(':') + 1);
          if (!e.state.shipped.contains(id)) e.state.shipped.add(id);
        }
      }
      if (e.version < 4) {
        var map = content.map(e.state.map);
        for (var prop : map.objects) {
          if (prop.id.startsWith("v4_")
              && prop.solid != null
              && World.intersects(e.state.x, e.state.y, prop.solid)) {
            e.state.x = map.spawnX;
            e.state.y = map.spawnY;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
      new World(content, e.state);
      return e.state;
    } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
      throw new IOException("Повреждённое или несовместимое сохранение", ex);
    }
  }

  public Loaded load() throws IOException {
    try {
      return new Loaded(read(directory.resolve("save.json")), false);
    } catch (IOException main) {
      try {
        return new Loaded(read(directory.resolve("save.backup.json")), true);
      } catch (IOException backup) {
        throw new IOException("Не удалось прочитать сохранение и резервную копию", main);
      }
    }
  }

  public void save(GameState state) throws IOException {
    new World(content, state);
    Files.createDirectories(directory);
    Path main = directory.resolve("save.json"),
        temp = directory.resolve("save.tmp.json"),
        backup = directory.resolve("save.backup.json");
    Files.writeString(temp, json().prettyPrint(new Envelope(state)), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    read(temp);
    if (Files.isRegularFile(main)) {
      boolean valid = true;
      try {
        read(main);
      } catch (IOException ignored) {
        valid = false;
      }
      if (valid) Files.copy(main, backup, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
    }
    replace(temp, main);
  }

  public static void replace(Path temp, Path file) throws IOException {
    try {
      Files.move(temp, file, StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
    } catch (AtomicMoveNotSupportedException ex) {
      Files.move(temp, file, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
    }
  }

  public Path directory() {
    return directory;
  }
}
END FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/save/SaveStore.java

package life.ctrl;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import java.io.*;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.regex.*;
import javax.imageio.ImageIO;
import life.ctrl.content.Content;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

/** Asset and text contracts catch failures that Java compilation cannot see. */
class AssetContractTest {
  private java.awt.image.BufferedImage image(String id) throws IOException {
    try (var in = getClass().getResourceAsStream("/art/" + id + ".png")) {
      assertNotNull(in, "Missing PNG: " + id);
      var png = ImageIO.read(in);
      assertNotNull(png, "Invalid PNG: " + id);
      return png;
    }
  }

  @Test
  void everyMapAndObjectHasARealReadableTexture() throws Exception {
    var content = Content.load();
    for (var m : content.maps) {
      var png = image(m.id);
      assertEquals(m.width, png.getWidth());
      assertEquals(m.height, png.getHeight());
      for (var p : m.objects) if (p.art != null) image(p.art);
      for (var n : m.npcs) image(n.art);
    }
  }

  @Test
  void animationAndStateVariantsMatchRuntimeGeometry() throws Exception {
    for (String id : List.of("hero", "hero_1", "hero_2", "mira", "arkady", "lev")) {
      var png = image(id);
      assertEquals(64, png.getWidth(), id);
      assertEquals(96, png.getHeight(), id);
      assertTrue(png.getColorModel().hasAlpha(), id);
    }
    for (String[] pair :
        new String[][] {
          {"boxes", "boxes_open"}, {"plant", "plant_watered"}, {"mailbox", "mailbox_signed"}
        }) {
      var base = image(pair[0]);
      var variant = image(pair[1]);
      assertEquals(base.getWidth(), variant.getWidth());
      assertEquals(base.getHeight(), variant.getHeight());
    }
    for (String id :
        List.of(
            "portrait_hero", "portrait_mira", "portrait_arkady", "portrait_lev", "portrait_note")) {
      var png = image(id);
      assertEquals(32, png.getWidth());
      assertEquals(32, png.getHeight());
    }
  }

  private Map<Integer, Integer> advances() throws IOException {
    try (var in = getClass().getResourceAsStream("/fonts/ui.fnt")) {
      assertNotNull(in);
      String fnt = new String(in.readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
      assertTrue(fnt.contains("padding=0,0,0,0"));
      Map<Integer, Integer> a = new HashMap<>();
      var matcher = Pattern.compile("char id=(\\d+).*?xadvance=(\\d+)").matcher(fnt);
      while (matcher.find())
        a.put(Integer.parseInt(matcher.group(1)), Integer.parseInt(matcher.group(2)));
      return a;
    }
  }

  @Test
  void cyrillicDialogueFitsBesidePortraitWithoutMissingGlyphs() throws Exception {
    var widths = advances();
    for (char c : "ЁёЙй×·№→←↑↓".toCharArray()) assertTrue(widths.containsKey((int) c));
    for (var node : Content.load().dialogues) {
      int lines = 1, used = 0;
      for (String word : node.text.split(" ")) {
        int width = widths.get(32);
        for (char c : word.toCharArray()) {
          assertTrue(widths.containsKey((int) c), "Missing glyph: " + c);
          width += widths.get((int) c);
        }
        if (used + width > 378) {
          lines++;
          used = width;
        } else used += width;
      }
      assertTrue(lines <= 3, node.id + " overflows into response buttons: " + lines + " lines");
    }
  }
}
END FILE: core/src/test/java/life/ctrl/AssetContractTest.java

package life.ctrl;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import java.nio.file.*;
import java.util.*;
import life.ctrl.bytescript.ByteScript;
import life.ctrl.content.*;
import life.ctrl.model.*;
import life.ctrl.save.SaveStore;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;

class CareerTest {
  final LifeContent data = LifeContent.load();
  @TempDir Path folder;

  void finish(GameState s, String id) {
    var j = data.job(id);
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0);
    Coding.completeTyping(s);
    Coding.repair(s, j, Arrays.asList(j.solutions).indexOf(j.source));
    assertTrue(Coding.test(s, j).passed(), id);
    Coding.preview(s, j, j.seed[j.seed.length - 1]);
    Coding.deliver(s, j);
    assertEquals(5, s.jobStage, id);
  }

  @Test
  void tactileDraftAndDebugRequireSeparateTypingPhases() {
    var state = new GameState();
    var job = data.job("total");
    Coding.accept(state, job, 0, false, true);
    assertEquals(0, state.typedCount);
    assertFalse(Coding.test(state, job).compiled());
    while (state.typedCount < state.codeSource.length()) Coding.type(state);
    assertFalse(Coding.test(state, job).passed());

    Coding.repair(state, job, 0, true);
    assertEquals(0, state.typedCount);
    assertFalse(Coding.test(state, job).compiled());
    while (state.typedCount < state.codeSource.length()) Coding.type(state);
    assertTrue(Coding.test(state, job).passed());
  }

  @Test
  void profileCountsExecutedOperationsAndRejectsSlowCorrectProgram() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.codingXp = 8;
    var j = data.job("batch");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0);
    var r = Coding.test(s, j);
    assertTrue(r.compiled());
    assertFalse(r.passed());
    assertTrue(r.cases().stream().allMatch(x -> x.endsWith("OK")));
    assertEquals(7, ByteScript.compile(s.codeSource).operations(3));
    Coding.repair(s, j, 1);
    assertTrue(Coding.test(s, j).passed());
    assertEquals(1, ByteScript.compile(s.codeSource).operations(3));
  }

  @Test
  void profilerOnlyVisitsTakenBranch() {
    var p = ByteScript.compile("fn f(x) { return x > 0 ? x + 1 : x * x + x; }");
    assertEquals(3, p.operations(2));
    assertEquals(4, p.operations(-2));
  }

  @Test
  void revisionsRestoreExactSourceAndInvalidateRelease() throws Exception {
    var s = new GameState();
    var j = data.job("total");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0);
    Coding.completeTyping(s);
    Coding.repair(s, j, 0);
    Coding.commit(s);
    Coding.preview(s, j, 2);
    var store = new SaveStore(folder, Content.load());
    store.save(s);
    s = store.load().state();
    assertEquals(2, s.revisions.size());
    Coding.restore(s, 0);
    assertFalse(s.programPreviewed);
    assertFalse(Coding.test(s, j).compiled());
    Coding.restore(s, 1);
    assertTrue(Coding.test(s, j).passed());
    assertFalse(s.programPreviewed);
  }

  @Test
  void repeatedCommitsAndStockTestsCannotFarmQualityBonus() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var j = data.job("total");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0);
    Coding.repair(s, j, 0);
    Coding.commit(s);
    Coding.commit(s);
    assertEquals(2, s.revisions.size());
    Coding.probe(s, j, new double[] {70, 2});
    assertTrue(s.customCases.isEmpty());
    Coding.probe(s, j, new double[] {12, 8});
    Coding.probe(s, j, new double[] {12, 8});
    assertEquals(1, s.customCases.size());
    assertEquals(0, Career.bonus(s, j));
    Coding.probe(s, j, new double[] {12, 0});
    assertEquals(100, Career.bonus(s, j));
  }

  @Test
  void probeErrorDoesNotGrantQualityCredit() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var j = data.job("total");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0);
    Coding.completeTyping(s);
    Coding.probe(s, j, new double[] {12, 8});
    assertTrue(s.customCases.isEmpty());
    Coding.repair(s, j, 0);
    Coding.probe(s, j, new double[] {Double.NaN, 8});
    assertTrue(s.customCases.isEmpty());
  }

  @Test
  void rushDeadlinePaysBonusOnlyWhenWorkFits() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var j = data.job("total");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0, true);
    Coding.repair(s, j, 0);
    Coding.preview(s, j, 2);
    assertEquals(j.pay / 4, Career.bonus(s, j));
    Life.advance(s, 91);
    assertEquals(0, Career.bonus(s, j));
    Coding.deliver(s, j);
    assertEquals(10650, s.money);
    assertTrue(s.lastClientMessage.contains("Тамара"));
  }

  @Test
  void unfinishedProjectCannotBeSilentlyReplaced() {
    var s = new GameState();
    Coding.accept(s, data.job("total"), 0);
    String before = s.codeSource;
    Coding.accept(s, data.job("discount"), 0);
    assertEquals("total", s.activeJob);
    assertEquals(before, s.codeSource);
    Coding.abandon(s);
    Coding.accept(s, data.job("discount"), 0);
    assertEquals("discount", s.activeJob);
    assertEquals(10000, s.money);
  }

  @Test
  void progressionOpensNewWorkButCapsDailyIncome() {
    var s = new GameState();
    assertEquals(3, Career.offers(s, data).size());
    finish(s, "total");
    finish(s, "discount");
    finish(s, "delivery");
    assertEquals(1, Career.tier(s));
    assertEquals(6, Career.offers(s, data).size());
    Coding.accept(s, data.job("refund"), 0);
    assertNotEquals("refund", s.activeJob);
    Life.sleep(s);
    Coding.accept(s, data.job("refund"), 0);
    assertEquals("refund", s.activeJob);
    assertEquals(2, s.jobStage);
  }

  @Test
  void offerRotationIsStableAndChangesNextDay() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.codingXp = 8;
    var first = Career.offers(s, data).stream().map(j -> j.id).toList();
    assertEquals(first, Career.offers(s, data).stream().map(j -> j.id).toList());
    Life.sleep(s);
    assertNotEquals(first, Career.offers(s, data).stream().map(j -> j.id).toList());
  }

  @Test
  void upgradesHaveRealEffectAndAreOneTimePurchases() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var j = data.job("total");
    int before = Career.duration(s, j);
    Career.buy(s, "ssd");
    assertEquals(5800, s.money);
    assertTrue(Career.duration(s, j) < before);
    Career.buy(s, "ssd");
    assertEquals(5800, s.money);
    Career.buy(s, "chair");
    assertEquals(3000, s.money);
    var poor = new GameState();
    poor.money = 0;
    Career.buy(poor, "ssd");
    assertTrue(poor.upgrades.isEmpty());
  }

  @Test
  void projectRequiresOrderedShippedCodeAndPaysOnlyAfterLaunch() throws Exception {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.completedOrders = 1;
    Coding.accept(s, data.job("support"), 0);
    assertTrue(s.activeJob.isEmpty());
    finish(s, "board");
    Life.sleep(s);
    assertEquals(9700, s.money);
    finish(s, "remind");
    finish(s, "support");
    assertEquals(9100, s.money);
    int day = s.day();
    Life.sleep(s);
    assertEquals(day + 1, s.day());
    assertEquals(9260, s.money);
    Career.settleProject(s);
    assertEquals(9260, s.money);
    var store = new SaveStore(folder, Content.load());
    store.save(s);
    s = store.load().state();
    Career.settleProject(s);
    assertEquals(9260, s.money);
    Coding.deliver(s, data.job("support"));
    assertEquals(9260, s.money);
  }

  @Test
  void unaffordableLaunchKeepsWorkingCodeUntilPlayerCanPay() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.completedOrders = 1;
    s.money = 0;
    var j = data.job("board");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0);
    Coding.repair(s, j, 0);
    Coding.preview(s, j, 2);
    Coding.deliver(s, j);
    assertEquals(0, Career.projectStage(s));
    assertEquals(3, s.jobStage);
    Economy.earn(s, 300, "Подработка");
    Coding.deliver(s, j);
    assertEquals(1, Career.projectStage(s));
    assertEquals(0, s.money);
  }

  @Test
  void versionTwoMigrationPreservesLifeAndRestoresCareer() throws Exception {
    Files.writeString(
        folder.resolve("save.json"),
        "{\"version\":2,\"state\":{\"name\":\"Саша\",\"money\":4321,\"paidJobs\":[\"1:total\",\"2:discount\"]}}");
    var s = new SaveStore(folder, Content.load()).load().state();
    assertEquals(4321, s.money);
    assertEquals(2, s.codingXp);
    assertEquals(2, s.completedOrders);
    assertTrue(s.shipped.contains("discount"));
  }

  @Test
  void duskAndWeatherStayDeterministicAndReadable() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.gameMinutes = 12 * 60;
    assertEquals(0, Atmosphere.night(s));
    s.gameMinutes = 19 * 60;
    assertTrue(Atmosphere.night(s) > .3 && Atmosphere.night(s) < .5);
    s.gameMinutes = 23 * 60;
    assertEquals(1, Atmosphere.night(s));
    assertTrue(Atmosphere.rain(s));
    s.gameMinutes = 1440 + 12 * 60;
    assertFalse(Atmosphere.rain(s));
  }

  @Test
  void overnightLaunchOnlyPaysOnFollowingDay() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.completedOrders = 1;
    s.shipped.addAll(List.of("board", "remind"));
    s.gameMinutes = 23 * 60;
    finish(s, "support");
    assertEquals(2, s.day());
    assertEquals(9700, s.money);
    assertEquals(2, s.projectIncomeDay);
    Life.sleep(s);
    assertEquals(9700, s.money);
    Life.sleep(s);
    assertEquals(9860, s.money);
  }

  @Test
  void cancellingDoesNotResetPromisedRushDeadline() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var j = data.job("total");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0, true);
    int promised = s.rushDeadline;
    Life.advance(s, 100);
    Coding.abandon(s);
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0, true);
    assertEquals(promised, s.rushDeadline);
    assertEquals(0, Career.bonus(s, j));
  }
}
END FILE: core/src/test/java/life/ctrl/CareerTest.java

package life.ctrl;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import java.nio.file.*;
import life.ctrl.bytescript.ByteScript;
import life.ctrl.content.*;
import life.ctrl.model.*;
import life.ctrl.save.SaveStore;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;

class LifeSystemsTest {
  final LifeContent data = LifeContent.load();
  final Content content = Content.load();
  @TempDir Path folder;

  @Test
  void startsWithTenThousandAndAllPricesAreAffordableButMeaningful() {
    var s = new GameState();
    assertEquals(10000, s.money);
    assertEquals(3000, Economy.WEEKLY_RENT);
    for (var j : data.jobs) if (j.tier == 0 && !j.once) assertTrue(j.pay >= 650 && j.pay <= 950);
    assertTrue(data.item("soup").price < 650);
  }

  @Test
  void purchaseAndFoodAreSingleInventoryTransactions() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var meal = data.item("soup");
    Life.buy(s, meal);
    assertEquals(10000 - meal.price, s.money);
    assertEquals(1, s.count("soup"));
    double h = s.hunger;
    Life.eat(s, meal);
    assertEquals(0, s.count("soup"));
    assertTrue(s.hunger > h);
    int money = s.money;
    Life.eat(s, meal);
    assertEquals(money, s.money);
  }

  @Test
  void purchaseRejectsOverspending() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.money = 1;
    Life.buy(s, data.item("book"));
    assertEquals(1, s.money);
    assertEquals(0, s.count("book"));
  }

  @Test
  void weeklyRentOccursOnceAcrossSaveAndLoad() throws Exception {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.gameMinutes = 3 * 1440 - 1;
    Life.advance(s, 2);
    assertEquals(7000, s.money);
    assertEquals(10, s.rentThroughDay);
    var saves = new SaveStore(folder, content);
    saves.save(s);
    s = saves.load().state();
    Life.advance(s, 1);
    assertEquals(7000, s.money);
    assertEquals(1, s.transactions.size());
  }

  @Test
  void povertyHasFoodAndIncomeWithoutAPc() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.money = 0;
    s.hunger = 1;
    Life.freeMeal(s);
    assertTrue(s.hunger >= 40);
    Life.courier(s);
    assertEquals(240, s.money);
    assertTrue(s.items.isEmpty());
    Life.courier(s);
    assertEquals(240, s.money);
  }

  @Test
  void everyTemplateReallyCompilesAndPassesItsClientCases() {
    for (var j : data.jobs) {
      var p = ByteScript.compile(j.source);
      assertEquals(j.function, p.name());
      for (var c : j.tests) assertEquals(c.expected, p.run(c.args), .001);
    }
  }

  @Test
  void syntaxAndSemanticAndTestFailuresAreDifferent() {
    assertEquals(
        ByteScript.Kind.SYNTAX,
        assertThrows(ByteScript.Error.class, () -> ByteScript.compile("fn f(x) { return ; }"))
            .kind);
    assertEquals(
        ByteScript.Kind.SEMANTIC,
        assertThrows(ByteScript.Error.class, () -> ByteScript.compile("fn f(x) { return y; }"))
            .kind);
    var s = new GameState();
    var j = data.job("total");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 1);
    Coding.completeTyping(s);
    var r = Coding.test(s, j);
    assertTrue(r.compiled());
    assertFalse(r.passed());
  }

  @Test
  void realRepairChangesProgramAndPaymentCannotBeDuplicatedEvenAcrossMidnight() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.gameMinutes = 23 * 60 + 50;
    var j = data.job("total");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0);
    Coding.completeTyping(s);
    assertFalse(Coding.test(s, j).compiled());
    String before = s.codeSource;
    Coding.repair(s, j, 0);
    assertNotEquals(before, s.codeSource);
    assertTrue(Coding.test(s, j).passed());
    assertEquals(90, Coding.preview(s, j, 3));
    Coding.deliver(s, j);
    int paid = s.money;
    assertEquals(10650, paid);
    assertEquals(2, s.day());
    Coding.deliver(s, j);
    assertEquals(paid, s.money);
  }

  @Test
  void projectNeedsPreviewAndSurvivesSavingInTheMiddle() throws Exception {
    var s = new GameState();
    var j = data.job("total");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0);
    Coding.type(s);
    var saves = new SaveStore(folder, content);
    saves.save(s);
    var copy = saves.load().state();
    assertEquals(s.typedCount, copy.typedCount);
    assertEquals(s.codeSource, copy.codeSource);
    Coding.deliver(copy, j);
    assertEquals(10000, copy.money);
  }

  @Test
  void favouriteGiftsDoNotBuyTrustAndCannotBeSpammed() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var g = data.girl("mira");
    Social.meet(s, g);
    s.items.put("seeds", 2);
    Social.gift(s, g, data.item("seeds"));
    var r = s.relation("mira");
    int a = r.affinity;
    Social.gift(s, g, data.item("seeds"));
    assertEquals(a, r.affinity);
    assertEquals(1, s.count("seeds"));
    Social.invite(s, g, "park");
    assertTrue(s.plannedGirl.isEmpty());
  }

  @Test
  void topicsAreUniqueAndRepeatedConversationsDoNotFarmAffection() {
    var s = new GameState();
    for (var g : data.girls) {
      Social.meet(s, g);
      Social.talk(s, g, 0);
      var r = s.relation(g.id);
      int a = r.affinity;
      Social.talk(s, g, 0);
      assertEquals(a, r.affinity);
      assertTrue(r.trust >= 8);
    }
    assertNotEquals(data.girl("mira").topics[0].response, data.girl("asya").topics[0].response);
  }

  @Test
  void datesRequireInvitationsAndMoneyAndFreeParkIsPossible() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var g = data.girl("asya");
    Social.meet(s, g);
    Social.talk(s, g, 0);
    Social.date(s, g, 0);
    assertEquals(0, s.relation(g.id).dates);
    Social.invite(s, g, "cafe");
    s.map = "cafe";
    s.money = 0;
    Social.date(s, g, 0);
    assertEquals(0, s.relation(g.id).dates);
    s.plannedVenue = "park";
    s.map = "park";
    Social.date(s, g, 0);
    assertEquals(1, s.relation(g.id).dates);
    assertEquals(0, s.money);
  }

  @Test
  void everyGirlHasAReachableRelationshipAndConsentPath() {
    for (var g : data.girls) {
      var s = new GameState();
      for (int day = 1; day <= 4; day++) {
        s.gameMinutes = (day - 1) * 1440 + 18 * 60;
        Social.meet(s, g);
        Social.talk(s, g, 0);
        Social.invite(s, g, "park");
        s.map = "park";
        Social.date(s, g, 0);
        if (day == 3) Social.commit(s, g);
      }
      assertEquals(g.id, s.partnerId);
      assertTrue(Social.canShareNight(s, g));
      s.map = "room";
      int nights = s.relation(g.id).sharedNights;
      Social.shareNight(s, g, false);
      assertEquals(nights, s.relation(g.id).sharedNights);
      Social.shareNight(s, g, true);
      assertEquals(nights + 1, s.relation(g.id).sharedNights);
      assertTrue(s.energy > 90);
    }
  }

  @Test
  void friendshipNeverImplicitlyConsentsToIntimacy() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var g = data.girl("mira");
    Social.meet(s, g);
    var r = s.relation(g.id);
    r.affinity = 100;
    r.trust = 100;
    r.dates = 10;
    assertFalse(Social.canShareNight(s, g));
    Social.shareNight(s, g, true);
    assertEquals(0, r.sharedNights);
  }

  @Test
  void socialStateAndLedgerRoundTrip() throws Exception {
    var s = new GameState();
    var g = data.girl("nika");
    Social.meet(s, g);
    Social.talk(s, g, 0);
    Life.buy(s, data.item("book"));
    Social.invite(s, g, "park");
    var store = new SaveStore(folder, content);
    store.save(s);
    var copy = store.load().state();
    assertEquals(s.relation(g.id).trust, copy.relation(g.id).trust);
    assertEquals(g.id, copy.plannedGirl);
    assertEquals(s.transactions.get(0).amount, copy.transactions.get(0).amount);
    assertEquals(1, copy.count("book"));
  }

  @Test
  void oneNpcExistsInOnePlaceDuringTheDate() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var g = data.girl("mira");
    Social.meet(s, g);
    Social.talk(s, g, 0);
    Social.invite(s, g, "park");
    var w = new World(content, s);
    int count = 0;
    for (var m : content.maps) {
      s.map = m.id;
      count += w.npcs().stream().filter(n -> n.id.equals("mira")).count();
    }
    assertEquals(1, count);
  }

  @Test
  void legacyRoomSaveMigratesToSafeSmallerRoom() throws Exception {
    String old =
        "{\"version\":1,\"state\":{\"name\":\"Саша\",\"map\":\"room\",\"x\":380,\"y\":210}}";
    Files.writeString(folder.resolve("save.json"), old);
    var state = new SaveStore(folder, content).load().state();
    assertEquals(240, state.x);
    assertEquals(190, state.y);
    assertEquals(10000, state.money);
  }

  @Test
  void allAlgorithmChoicesCanBeRepairedAndPaid() {
    for (var j : data.jobs)
      for (int choice = 0; choice < j.algorithms.length; choice++) {
        var s = new GameState();
        s.codingXp = 8;
        s.completedOrders = 1;
        if (j.id.equals("remind") || j.id.equals("support")) s.shipped.add("board");
        if (j.id.equals("support")) s.shipped.add("remind");
        Coding.accept(s, j, choice);
        Coding.completeTyping(s);
        Coding.repair(s, j, java.util.Arrays.asList(j.solutions).indexOf(j.source));
        assertTrue(Coding.test(s, j).passed(), j.id + ":" + choice);
        Coding.preview(s, j, 2);
        Coding.deliver(s, j);
        assertEquals(10000 + j.pay - j.cost, s.money);
      }
  }

  @Test
  void cannotCollectADifferentJobsFee() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var j = data.job("total");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0);
    Coding.completeTyping(s);
    Coding.repair(s, j, 0);
    Coding.preview(s, j, 2);
    Coding.deliver(s, data.job("discount"));
    assertEquals(10000, s.money);
    assertFalse(Coding.test(s, data.job("discount")).passed());
  }

  @Test
  void datesCannotHappenRemotely() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var g = data.girl("mira");
    Social.meet(s, g);
    Social.talk(s, g, 0);
    Social.invite(s, g, "park");
    Social.date(s, g, 0);
    assertEquals(0, s.relation(g.id).dates);
    assertEquals("mira", s.plannedGirl);
    s.map = "park";
    Social.date(s, g, 0);
    assertEquals(1, s.relation(g.id).dates);
  }

  @Test
  void rejectsNonFiniteNeedsAndBrokenInventory() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.health = Double.NaN;
    assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new World(content, s));
    s.health = 85;
    s.items.put("soup", -1);
    assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new World(content, s));
  }

  @Test
  void twoDailyOrdersCoverTwoMealsAndRentForTwoWeeks() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.gameMinutes = 480;
    for (int day = 1; day <= 14; day++) {
      for (String id : new String[] {"total", "discount"}) {
        var j = data.job(id);
        Coding.accept(s, j, 0);
        Coding.completeTyping(s);
        Coding.repair(s, j, 0);
        Coding.preview(s, j, 2);
        Coding.deliver(s, j);
      }
      Life.buy(s, data.item("soup"));
      Life.eat(s, data.item("soup"));
      Life.advance(s, 360);
      Life.buy(s, data.item("soup"));
      Life.eat(s, data.item("soup"));
      Life.sleep(s);
      assertTrue(s.hunger > 20);
      assertEquals(0, s.rentDebt);
    }
    assertEquals(10000 + 14 * (650 + 950 - 440) - 6000, s.money);
    assertEquals("08:00", s.clock());
  }

  @Test
  void passingNpcCannotStealDoorInteraction() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.map = "stair";
    s.x = 172.4f;
    s.y = 89.07f;
    var w = new World(content, s);
    assertNotNull(w.nearestNpc());
    assertEquals("door", w.nearestProp().action);
    assertNull(w.interactionNpc());
  }
}

package life.ctrl;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import java.nio.file.*;
import life.ctrl.content.Content;
import life.ctrl.model.*;
import life.ctrl.save.SaveStore;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;

class SaveTest {
  @TempDir Path dir;
  final Content content = Content.load();

  @Test
  void roundTripRetainsMapFlagsAppearanceAndClock() throws Exception {
    var saves = new SaveStore(dir, content);
    var s = new GameState();
    s.name = "Ёжик";
    s.outfit = 2;
    s.elapsed = 81.25;
    s.map = "street";
    s.x = 675;
    s.y = 310;
    s.hasLetter = true;
    s.inspectedPc = true;
    s.metMira = true;
    s.visited.add("street");
    s.direction = 2;
    saves.save(s);
    var copy = saves.load().state();
    assertEquals(s.name, copy.name);
    assertEquals(s.map, copy.map);
    assertEquals(s.x, copy.x);
    assertEquals(s.y, copy.y);
    assertEquals(s.elapsed, copy.elapsed);
    assertEquals(s.direction, copy.direction);
    assertEquals(s.outfit, copy.outfit);
    assertTrue(copy.hasLetter);
    assertTrue(copy.inspectedPc);
    assertEquals(s.visited, copy.visited);
  }

  @Test
  void backupRecoversCorruptPrimary() throws Exception {
    var saves = new SaveStore(dir, content);
    var s = new GameState();
    s.name = "Первый";
    saves.save(s);
    s.name = "Второй";
    saves.save(s);
    Files.writeString(dir.resolve("save.json"), "broken");
    var loaded = saves.load();
    assertTrue(loaded.fromBackup());
    assertEquals("Первый", loaded.state().name);
  }

  @Test
  void corruptPrimaryDoesNotOverwriteGoodBackup() throws Exception {
    var saves = new SaveStore(dir, content);
    var s = new GameState();
    saves.save(s);
    s.name = "Мира";
    saves.save(s);
    Files.writeString(dir.resolve("save.json"), "broken");
    s.name = "Лев";
    saves.save(s);
    Files.writeString(dir.resolve("save.json"), "broken again");
    assertEquals("Саша", saves.load().state().name);
  }

  @Test
  void corruptOrFutureSaveDoesNotCrashCaller() throws Exception {
    var saves = new SaveStore(dir, content);
    Files.writeString(dir.resolve("save.json"), "{\"version\":999,\"state\":{}}");
    assertThrows(java.io.IOException.class, saves::load);
  }

  @Test
  void savingInEveryMapWorks() throws Exception {
    var saves = new SaveStore(dir, content);
    for (var m : content.maps) {
      var s = new GameState();
      s.map = m.id;
      s.x = m.spawnX;
      s.y = m.spawnY;
      saves.save(s);
      assertEquals(m.id, saves.load().state().map);
    }
  }

  @Test
  void invalidPositionDoesNotReplaceSave() throws Exception {
    var saves = new SaveStore(dir, content);
    var s = new GameState();
    saves.save(s);
    s.x = -100;
    assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> saves.save(s));
    assertEquals(240, saves.load().state().x);
  }

  @Test
  void settingsRoundTripAndBadFileFallback() throws Exception {
    var s = new Settings();
    s.music = .25f;
    s.reducedEffects = true;
    s.scale = 2;
    s.bind(0, com.badlogic.gdx.Input.Keys.T);
    s.save(dir);
    var copy = Settings.load(dir);
    assertEquals(s.up, copy.up);
    assertEquals(s.music, copy.music);
    assertTrue(copy.reducedEffects);
    Files.writeString(dir.resolve("settings.json"), "bad");
    assertEquals(3, Settings.load(dir).scale);
  }

  @Test
  void reservedAndDuplicateBindingsRejected() {
    var s = new Settings();
    assertFalse(s.bind(0, com.badlogic.gdx.Input.Keys.ESCAPE));
    assertFalse(s.bind(0, s.down));
    assertTrue(s.bind(0, com.badlogic.gdx.Input.Keys.T));
  }
}

FILE: core/src/test/java/life/ctrl/VisualAssetTest.java
