package life.ctrl;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.util.*;
import javax.imageio.ImageIO;
import life.ctrl.content.Content;
import life.ctrl.render.SceneLayout;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class VisualAssetTest {
  BufferedImage image(String path) throws Exception {
    try (var in = getClass().getResourceAsStream("/" + path + ".png")) {
      assertNotNull(in, path);
      return ImageIO.read(in);
    }
  }

  @Test
  void highDensityGeometryMatchesEveryMapAndProp() throws Exception {
    for (var map : Content.load().maps) {
      var hd = image("art/hd/" + map.id);
      assertEquals(map.width * 2, hd.getWidth());
      assertEquals(map.height * 2, hd.getHeight());
      var b = SceneLayout.bounds(map.id);
      assertTrue(
          b.x() >= 0
              && b.y() >= 0
              && b.x() + b.width() <= map.width
              && b.y() + b.height() <= map.height);
      for (var prop : map.objects)
        if (prop.art != null) {
          var old = image("art/" + prop.art);
          var p = image("art/hd/" + prop.art);
          assertEquals(old.getWidth() * 2, p.getWidth(), prop.art);
          assertEquals(old.getHeight() * 2, p.getHeight(), prop.art);
        }
    }
  }

  @Test
  void animationFramesCannotBleedIntoAdjacentCells() throws Exception {
    for (String id :
        List.of("hero", "hero_1", "hero_2", "mira", "asya", "nika", "tamara", "arkady", "lev")) {
      var p = image("art/hd/" + id);
      assertEquals(128, p.getWidth());
      assertEquals(192, p.getHeight());
      for (int row = 0; row < 4; row++)
        for (int col = 0; col < 4; col++) {
          for (int x = 0; x < 32; x++) {
            assertEquals(0, p.getRGB(col * 32 + x, row * 48) >>> 24, id);
            assertEquals(0, p.getRGB(col * 32 + x, row * 48 + 47) >>> 24, id);
          }
          for (int y = 0; y < 48; y++) {
            assertEquals(0, p.getRGB(col * 32, row * 48 + y) >>> 24, id);
            assertEquals(0, p.getRGB(col * 32 + 31, row * 48 + y) >>> 24, id);
          }
        }
    }
  }

  @Test
  void LightAndShadowTexturesFadeToTransparentAtAllEdges() throws Exception {
    for (String id : List.of("light_amber", "light_monitor", "light_window", "shadow")) {
      var p = image("art/hd/" + id);
      for (int x = 0; x < p.getWidth(); x++) {
        assertEquals(0, p.getRGB(x, 0) >>> 24, id);
        assertEquals(0, p.getRGB(x, p.getHeight() - 1) >>> 24, id);
      }
      for (int y = 0; y < p.getHeight(); y++) {
        assertEquals(0, p.getRGB(0, y) >>> 24, id);
        assertEquals(0, p.getRGB(p.getWidth() - 1, y) >>> 24, id);
      }
    }
  }

  @Test
  void cutawayVoidIsOpaqueAndUnpainted() throws Exception {
    for (String id : List.of("room", "stair", "cafe", "diner", "workshop", "park")) {
      var p = image("art/hd/" + id);
      var b = SceneLayout.bounds(id);
      for (int y = 0; y < p.getHeight(); y++)
        for (int x = 0; x < p.getWidth(); x++)
          if (x < b.x() * 2
              || y < b.y() * 2
              || x >= (b.x() + b.width()) * 2
              || y >= (b.y() + b.height()) * 2)
            assertEquals(0xff0e1a24, p.getRGB(x, y), id + " scene escaped its boundary");
    }
  }

  @Test
  void highDensityFontAtlasesFitBaselineGpuAndGlyphsStayInsideAtlas() throws Exception {
    for (String id : List.of("ui", "small", "title")) {
      var p = image("fonts/hd/" + id);
      assertTrue(p.getWidth() <= 2048 && p.getHeight() <= 2048, id);
      try (var in = getClass().getResourceAsStream("/fonts/hd/" + id + ".fnt")) {
        String text = new String(in.readAllBytes(), java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        var match =
            java.util.regex.Pattern.compile(
                    "char id=([0-9]+) x=([0-9]+) y=([0-9]+) width=([0-9]+) height=([0-9]+)")
                .matcher(text);
        int count = 0;
        while (match.find()) {
          int x = Integer.parseInt(match.group(2)), y = Integer.parseInt(match.group(3));
          assertTrue(
              x + Integer.parseInt(match.group(4)) <= p.getWidth(),
              id + " glyph " + match.group(1));
          assertTrue(
              y + Integer.parseInt(match.group(5)) <= p.getHeight(),
              id + " glyph " + match.group(1));
          count++;
        }
        assertTrue(count >= 200, id + " Cyrillic glyphs missing");
      }
    }
  }

  @Test
  void oldSaveInsideNewFurnitureMovesToEntranceWithoutLosingProgress() throws Exception {
    java.nio.file.Path folder = java.nio.file.Files.createTempDirectory("ctrl-visual-migration");
    try {
      java.nio.file.Files.writeString(
          folder.resolve("save.json"),
          "{\"version\":3,\"state\":{\"map\":\"diner\",\"x\":350,\"y\":145,\"money\":4321,\"codingXp\":8,\"completedOrders\":6,\"shipped\":[\"total\"],\"upgrades\":[\"ssd\"]}}");
      var store = new life.ctrl.save.SaveStore(folder, Content.load());
      var state = store.load().state();
      assertEquals(240, state.x);
      assertEquals(248, state.y);
      assertEquals(4321, state.money);
      assertEquals(8, state.codingXp);
      assertEquals(6, state.completedOrders);
      assertTrue(state.shipped.contains("total"));
      assertTrue(state.upgrades.contains("ssd"));
      store.save(state);
      assertEquals(4321, store.load().state().money);
    } finally {
      try (var files = java.nio.file.Files.list(folder)) {
        for (var file : files.toList()) java.nio.file.Files.delete(file);
      }
      java.nio.file.Files.delete(folder);
    }
  }
}
END FILE: core/src/test/java/life/ctrl/VisualAssetTest.java

FILE: core/src/test/java/life/ctrl/WorldTest.java

package life.ctrl;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import java.util.*;
import life.ctrl.content.Content;
import life.ctrl.model.*;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class WorldTest {
  final Content content = Content.load();

  World fresh() {
    return new World(content, new GameState());
  }

  @Test
  void contentReferencesAndEffectsAreValid() {
    content.validate();
    World world = fresh();
    for (var d : content.dialogues)
      for (var c : d.choices) assertDoesNotThrow(() -> world.effect(c.effect));
    for (var m : content.maps)
      for (var p : m.objects)
        if (!Set.of("door", "decoration", "wardrobe", "rest", "shop", "date", "courier", "wait")
            .contains(p.action)) assertDoesNotThrow(() -> content.dialogue(world.propDialogue(p)));
  }

  @Test
  void diagonalMovementIsNotFaster() {
    var straight = fresh();
    var diagonal = fresh();
    for (int i = 0; i < 15; i++) {
      straight.tick(1, 0);
      diagonal.tick(1, 1);
    }
    assertEquals(
        Math.hypot(straight.state.x - 240, straight.state.y - 190),
        Math.hypot(diagonal.state.x - 240, diagonal.state.y - 190),
        .002);
  }

  @Test
  void walkingIntoWallStopsWithoutTunnelling() {
    var w = fresh();
    for (int i = 0; i < 10000; i++) w.tick(1, 0);
    assertFalse(w.blocked(w.state.x, w.state.y));
    assertTrue(w.state.x < 416);
    assertTrue(w.state.x > 300);
  }

  @Test
  void equalTickCountsGiveEqualTimeAndDistance() {
    var a = fresh();
    var b = fresh();
    for (int frame = 0; frame < 30; frame++) {
      a.tick(-1, 0);
      a.tick(-1, 0);
    }
    for (int frame = 0; frame < 60; frame++) b.tick(-1, 0);
    assertEquals(a.state.x, b.state.x);
    assertEquals(a.state.elapsed, b.state.elapsed);
  }

  @Test
  void doorsHaveValidSpawnPositionsAndConnectEveryMap() {
    Set<String> visited = new HashSet<>();
    ArrayDeque<String> todo = new ArrayDeque<>();
    todo.add("room");
    while (!todo.isEmpty()) {
      String id = todo.remove();
      if (!visited.add(id)) continue;
      for (var p : content.map(id).objects)
        if (p.action.equals("door")) {
          GameState s = new GameState();
          s.map = id;
          s.x = content.map(id).spawnX;
          s.y = content.map(id).spawnY;
          World w = new World(content, s);
          w.enter(p);
          assertFalse(w.blocked(s.x, s.y), id + " -> " + p.target);
          todo.add(p.target);
          assertTrue(
              Arrays.stream(content.map(p.target).objects).anyMatch(q -> id.equals(q.target)),
              "Return door absent: " + id);
        }
    }
    assertEquals(content.maps.length, visited.size());
  }

  @Test
  void everyInteractionCanBeReachedFromMapEntry() {
    for (var map : content.maps) {
      GameState s = new GameState();
      s.map = map.id;
      s.x = map.spawnX;
      s.y = map.spawnY;
      World w = new World(content, s);
      int step = 4, cols = map.width / step, rows = map.height / step;
      boolean[] reachable = new boolean[cols * rows];
      ArrayDeque<Integer> queue = new ArrayDeque<>();
      int start = Math.round(s.y / step) * cols + Math.round(s.x / step);
      reachable[start] = true;
      queue.add(start);
      while (!queue.isEmpty()) {
        int at = queue.remove(), x = at % cols, y = at / cols;
        for (int[] d : new int[][] {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}}) {
          int nx = x + d[0], ny = y + d[1];
          if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= cols || ny >= rows) continue;
          int next = ny * cols + nx;
          if (!reachable[next] && !w.blocked(nx * step, ny * step)) {
            reachable[next] = true;
            queue.add(next);
          }
        }
      }
      for (var p : map.objects) {
        if (p.action.equals("decoration")) continue;
        boolean found = false;
        for (int i = 0; i < reachable.length; i++)
          if (reachable[i] && Math.hypot((i % cols) * step - p.x, (i / cols) * step - p.y) < 28) {
            found = true;
            break;
          }
        assertTrue(found, map.id + " unreachable: " + p.id);
      }
    }
  }

  @Test
  void npcRoutesStayWalkableAndDeterministic() {
    for (var map : content.maps) {
      GameState s = new GameState();
      s.map = map.id;
      s.x = map.spawnX;
      s.y = map.spawnY;
      World w = new World(content, s);
      var a = new World.Actor();
      var b = new World.Actor();
      for (int t = 0; t < 500; t++) {
        s.elapsed = t * .25;
        for (var npc : map.npcs) {
          w.position(npc, a);
          w.position(npc, b);
          assertEquals(a.x, b.x);
          assertEquals(a.y, b.y);
          assertFalse(w.blocked(a.x, a.y), npc.id + " intersects wall at " + a.x + "," + a.y);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  @Test
  void letterCanBeFoundBeforeQuestAndCannotBeDeliveredTwice() {
    var w = fresh();
    w.effect("deliverLetter");
    assertFalse(w.state.deliveredLetter);
    w.effect("takeLetter");
    assertTrue(w.state.hasLetter);
    w.effect("deliverLetter");
    w.effect("deliverLetter");
    w.effect("takeLetter");
    assertTrue(w.state.deliveredLetter);
    assertFalse(w.state.hasLetter);
    assertEquals("miraAgain", w.npcDialogue(content.map("stair").npcs[0]));
  }

  @Test
  void guideUsesReachableDoorsFromEveryMap() {
    var catalog = life.ctrl.content.LifeContent.load();
    for (var map : content.maps) {
      var state = new GameState();
      state.map = map.id;
      state.x = map.spawnX;
      state.y = map.spawnY;
      var w = new World(content, state);
      var g = Navigation.localGoal(w, Navigation.goal(w, catalog));
      assertEquals(map.id, g.map());
      assertTrue(
          Math.hypot(state.x - g.x(), state.y - g.y()) < 29
              || !Navigation.route(w, g.x(), g.y()).isEmpty(),
          map.id);
    }
  }

  @Test
  void nearbyNpcHasContextualDialogue() {
    var w = fresh();
    var npc = content.map("stair").npcs[0];
    assertEquals("mira", w.npcDialogue(npc));
    w.effect("takeLetter");
    assertEquals("miraFound", w.npcDialogue(npc));
  }

  @Test
  void malformedStatesAreRejected() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.x = Float.NaN;
    assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new World(content, s));
    s.x = 10;
    s.y = 10;
    assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new World(content, s));
    s.map = "absent";
    assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new World(content, s));
  }
}
END FILE: core/src/test/java/life/ctrl/WorldTest.java

package life.ctrl.ui;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class PinuxWindowManagerTest {
  @Test
  void windowsRetainIndependentFocusAndMinimization() {
    var wm = new PinuxWindowManager();
    assertNull(wm.front());
    wm.open(PinuxWindowManager.App.CODE);
    wm.open(PinuxWindowManager.App.TERMINAL);
    assertTrue(wm.isFront(PinuxWindowManager.App.TERMINAL));
    wm.minimize(PinuxWindowManager.App.TERMINAL);
    assertTrue(wm.isFront(PinuxWindowManager.App.CODE));
    assertTrue(wm.get(PinuxWindowManager.App.TERMINAL).visible);
    wm.open(PinuxWindowManager.App.TERMINAL);
    assertFalse(wm.get(PinuxWindowManager.App.TERMINAL).minimized);
    wm.close(PinuxWindowManager.App.TERMINAL);
    assertTrue(wm.isFront(PinuxWindowManager.App.CODE));
    assertFalse(wm.get(PinuxWindowManager.App.TERMINAL).visible);
  }

  @Test
  void draggingRestoresMaximizedWindowAndKeepsCaptionVisible() {
    var wm = new PinuxWindowManager();
    wm.open(PinuxWindowManager.App.CODE);
    wm.toggleMaximize(PinuxWindowManager.App.CODE);
    assertTrue(wm.isMaximizedCode());
    var w = wm.get(PinuxWindowManager.App.CODE);
    assertTrue(wm.pointerDown(w.x + 56, w.y + 8));
    assertTrue(wm.pointerDragged(265, 115));
    assertTrue(wm.pointerUp(265, 115));
    assertFalse(w.maximized);
    assertTrue(w.y >= PinuxWindowManager.TOP + 1);
    assertTrue(w.x >= 0);
    assertTrue(w.x + w.width <= 480);
    assertTrue(w.y + 25 <= PinuxWindowManager.BOTTOM);
  }

  @Test
  void captionButtonsMinimizeMaximizeAndCloseWithoutClickThrough() {
    var wm = new PinuxWindowManager();
    wm.open(PinuxWindowManager.App.FILES);
    var w = wm.get(PinuxWindowManager.App.FILES);
    assertTrue(wm.pointerDown(w.right() - 22, w.y + 8));
    assertTrue(w.maximized);
    assertTrue(wm.pointerDown(w.right() - 36, w.y + 8));
    assertTrue(w.minimized);
    wm.open(PinuxWindowManager.App.FILES);
    assertTrue(wm.pointerDown(w.right() - 7, w.y + 8));
    assertFalse(w.visible);
  }

  @Test
  void cornerResizeClampsToDesktopAndEdgeDraggingSnaps() {
    var wm = new PinuxWindowManager();
    wm.open(PinuxWindowManager.App.TERMINAL);
    var w = wm.get(PinuxWindowManager.App.TERMINAL);
    int initialWidth = w.width;
    assertTrue(wm.pointerDown(w.right() - 3, w.bottom() - 3));
    assertTrue(wm.pointerDragged(w.right() + 50, w.bottom() + 15));
    assertTrue(wm.pointerUp(w.right(), w.bottom()));
    assertTrue(w.width >= initialWidth);
    assertTrue(w.right() <= 480);
    assertTrue(w.bottom() <= PinuxWindowManager.BOTTOM);

    assertTrue(wm.pointerDown(w.x + 40, w.y + 8));
    assertTrue(wm.pointerDragged(1, 90));
    assertTrue(wm.pointerUp(1, 90));
    assertEquals(2, w.x);
    assertEquals(236, w.width);
  }

  @Test
  void clickingBackgroundWindowFocusesBeforeItsControls() {
    var wm = new PinuxWindowManager();
    wm.open(PinuxWindowManager.App.CODE);
    wm.open(PinuxWindowManager.App.FILES);
    var code = wm.get(PinuxWindowManager.App.CODE);
    // Far-right lower region belongs to CODE but is not covered by FILES.
    assertTrue(wm.pointerDown(code.right() - 8, code.bottom() - 12));
    assertTrue(wm.isFront(PinuxWindowManager.App.CODE));
  }
}
END FILE: core/src/test/java/life/ctrl/ui/PinuxWindowManagerTest.java

package life.ctrl.desktop;

import com.badlogic.gdx.*;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Input.Keys;
import com.badlogic.gdx.backends.lwjgl3.*;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.function.BooleanSupplier;
import life.ctrl.CtrlLife;
import life.ctrl.model.*;

/**
 * Actual input and rendering for the complete first day. Later intimacy UI uses an explicit
 * fixture.
 */
public final class ChapterSmoke extends CtrlLife {
  private record Step(String label, BooleanSupplier run) {}

  private final ArrayDeque<Step> steps = new ArrayDeque<>();
  private final ArrayList<float[]> route = new ArrayList<>();
  private int frames;
  private float timer, total;
  private boolean failed;
  private String current = "startup";
  private float beforeX, beforeY;
  private double pauseTime;

  public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
    Path dir = Path.of(System.getProperty("ctrl.dataDir"));
    Files.createDirectories(dir);
    for (String n : List.of("save.json", "save.backup.json", "settings.json"))
      Files.deleteIfExists(dir.resolve(n));
    Files.createDirectories(Path.of(System.getProperty("ctrl.shots")));
    var config = new Lwjgl3ApplicationConfiguration();
    config.setWindowedMode(1440, 810);
    config.setTitle("CTRL+LIFE 0.2 — test");
    config.disableAudio(Boolean.getBoolean("ctrl.noAudio"));
    config.setForegroundFPS(Integer.getInteger("ctrl.fps", 60));
    config.useVsync(false);
    ChapterSmoke game = new ChapterSmoke();
    new Lwjgl3Application(game, config);
    if (game.failed) throw new AssertionError(game.current);
    if (Boolean.getBoolean("ctrl.menuOnly")) {
      if (Files.exists(dir.resolve("save.json"))) throw new AssertionError("Menu created a save");
      System.out.println("MENU_ONLY_PASS");
    }
  }

  private void add(String name, BooleanSupplier f) {
    steps.add(new Step(name, f));
  }

  private void once(String name, Runnable f) {
    add(
        name,
        () -> {
          if (timer < .08f) return false;
          f.run();
          return true;
        });
  }

  private void tap(int key) {
    Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(key);
    Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyUp(key);
  }

  private void click(int x, int y) {
    Gdx.input
        .getInputProcessor()
        .touchDown(
            canvas().offsetX + x * canvas().scale,
            canvas().offsetY + y * canvas().scale,
            0,
            Input.Buttons.LEFT);
  }

  private void stop() {
    for (int k : new int[] {Keys.W, Keys.A, Keys.S, Keys.D}) Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyUp(k);
  }

  private void check(boolean ok, String message) {
    if (!ok) throw new AssertionError(message);
  }

  private void shot(String name) {
    canvas().screenshot(System.getProperty("ctrl.shots") + "/" + name + ".png");
  }

  private void waitFor(Mode mode) {
    add("wait " + mode, () -> mode() == mode);
  }

  private void pressE() {
    once(
        "interact",
        () -> {
          stop();
          tap(Keys.E);
        });
  }

  private void closeScene() {
    waitFor(Mode.SCENE);
    once("continue scene", () -> click(240, 237));
    waitFor(Mode.WORLD);
  }

  private void walk(float x, float y) {
    once(
        "plan " + x + "," + y,
        () -> {
          route.clear();
          route.addAll(Navigation.route(world(), x, y));
          check(
              !route.isEmpty() || Math.hypot(world().state.x - x, world().state.y - y) < 29,
              "No route");
        });
    add(
        "walk " + x + "," + y,
        () -> {
          stop();
          if (route.isEmpty()) return true;
          var p = route.get(0);
          float dx = p[0] - world().state.x, dy = p[1] - world().state.y;
          if (Math.abs(dx) < 1.6 && Math.abs(dy) < 1.6) {
            route.remove(0);
            return route.isEmpty();
          }
          if (Math.abs(dx) >= 1.3) Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(dx > 0 ? Keys.D : Keys.A);
          if (Math.abs(dy) >= 1.3) Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(dy > 0 ? Keys.S : Keys.W);
          return false;
        });
  }

  private void approachMira() {
    add(
        "approach Mira",
        () -> {
          stop();
          var npc =
              world().npcs().stream().filter(n -> n.id.equals("mira")).findFirst().orElseThrow();
          var a = new World.Actor();
          world().position(npc, a);
          float dx = a.x - world().state.x, dy = a.y - world().state.y;
          if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) < 22) {
            tap(Keys.E);
            return true;
          }
          if (Math.abs(dx) > 3) Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(dx > 0 ? Keys.D : Keys.A);
          if (Math.abs(dy) > 3) Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(dy > 0 ? Keys.S : Keys.W);
          return false;
        });
    waitFor(Mode.SOCIAL);
  }

  @Override
  public void create() {
    super.create();
    add("boot", () -> frames > 4);
    if (Boolean.getBoolean("ctrl.menuOnly")) {
      once("settings", () -> click(300, 193));
      waitFor(Mode.SETTINGS);
      once("close settings without game", () -> Gdx.app.exit());
      return;
    }
    once(
        "menu",
        () -> {
          shot("01-menu");
          tap(Keys.ENTER);
        });
    waitFor(Mode.CREATE);
    once(
        "new game",
        () -> {
          tap(Keys.DOWN);
          tap(Keys.ENTER);
        });
    waitFor(Mode.INTRO);
    once(
        "arrival",
        () -> {
          shot("02-arrival");
          tap(Keys.ENTER);
        });
    once("budget introduction", () -> tap(Keys.ENTER));
    once(
        "controls introduction",
        () -> {
          shot("03-onboarding");
          tap(Keys.ENTER);
        });
    waitFor(Mode.WORLD);
    once("poor room", () -> shot("04-room"));
    walk(187, 156);
    pressE();
    waitFor(Mode.IDE);
    once(
        "job board",
        () -> {
          shot("05-jobs");
          click(240, 122);
        });
    once("leave before algorithm", () -> tap(Keys.ESCAPE));
    waitFor(Mode.WORLD);
    pressE();
    waitFor(Mode.IDE);
    once(
        "reopen unstarted order",
        () -> {
          check(lifeScreens().codePage == 0, "Unstarted order stuck in editor");
          click(240, 122);
        });
    once(
        "algorithm choice",
        () -> {
          shot("06-algorithm");
          click(240, 160);
        });
    once(
        "typing start",
        () -> {
          beforeX = world().state.x;
          beforeY = world().state.y;
          for (int i = 0; i < 25; i++) tap(i % 2 == 0 ? Keys.W : Keys.E);
        });
    once(
        "typing isolated",
        () -> {
          check(beforeX == world().state.x && beforeY == world().state.y, "IDE moved player");
          click(180, 236);
        });
    once(
        "real compiler error",
        () -> {
          check(
              lifeScreens().report != null && !lifeScreens().report.compiled(),

          shot("07-error");

            check(

                "Night consent outcome not saved"));
    once("contacts", () -> tap(Keys.R));
    waitFor(Mode.CONTACTS);
    once(
        "contacts portraits",
        () -> {
          shot("19-contacts");
          tap(Keys.ESCAPE);
        });
    waitFor(Mode.WORLD);
    once("small window", () -> Gdx.graphics.setWindowedMode(960, 540));
    add("resize", () -> timer > .3);
    once("960 shot", () -> shot("20-window-960"));
    once("large window", () -> Gdx.graphics.setWindowedMode(1920, 1080));
    add("resize large", () -> timer > .3);
    once("1080 shot", () -> shot("21-window-1080"));
    add(
        "soak",
        () -> {
          check(!world().blocked(world().state.x, world().state.y), "Invalid world position");
          return total >= Float.parseFloat(System.getProperty("ctrl.soakSeconds", "0"));
        });
    once(
        "complete",
        () -> {
          System.out.println(
              "CHAPTER_SMOKE_PASS frames="
                  + frames
                  + " seconds="
                  + total
                  + " first day + labelled consent fixture");
          Gdx.app.exit();
        });
  }

  @Override
  public void render() {
    super.render();
    frames++;
    float dt = Gdx.graphics.getDeltaTime();
    timer += dt;
    total += dt;
    if (steps.isEmpty()) return;
    var step = steps.peek();
    current = step.label;
    try {
      if (step.run.getAsBoolean()) {
        System.out.println("GUI: " + current);
        steps.remove();
        timer = 0;
      } else if (timer > 60 && !current.equals("soak"))
        throw new AssertionError("Timeout " + current);
    } catch (Throwable e) {
      failed = true;
      shot("FAILED");
      e.printStackTrace();
      steps.clear();
      Gdx.app.exit();
    }
  }
}
END FILE: lwjgl3/src/smoke/java/life/ctrl/desktop/ChapterSmoke.java

package life.ctrl.desktop;

import com.badlogic.gdx.*;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Input.Keys;
import com.badlogic.gdx.backends.lwjgl3.*;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.function.BooleanSupplier;
import life.ctrl.CtrlLife;
import life.ctrl.model.Settings;
import life.ctrl.model.World;

/** Runs actual LWJGL3 rendering and keyboard input. Requires an OpenGL-capable display. */
public final class GuiSmoke extends CtrlLife {
  private final ArrayDeque<Step> script = new ArrayDeque<>();
  private final ArrayList<float[]> path = new ArrayList<>();
  private long started;
  private float stepTime, total, beforeMove;
  private double beforePause;
  private int frames;
  private boolean failed, soakRight = true;
  private String current = "startup";

  private record Step(String name, BooleanSupplier run) {}

  public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
    Path save = Path.of(System.getProperty("ctrl.dataDir"));
    Files.createDirectories(save);
    for (String f : new String[] {"save.json", "save.backup.json", "settings.json"})
      Files.deleteIfExists(save.resolve(f));
    Files.createDirectories(Path.of(System.getProperty("ctrl.shots")));
    var config = new Lwjgl3ApplicationConfiguration();
    config.setTitle("CTRL+LIFE — GUI smoke");
    config.setWindowedMode(1440, 810);
    config.disableAudio(Boolean.getBoolean("ctrl.noAudio"));
    config.setForegroundFPS(Integer.getInteger("ctrl.fps", 60));
    config.useVsync(false);
    GuiSmoke game = new GuiSmoke();
    new Lwjgl3Application(game, config);
    if (game.failed) throw new IllegalStateException("GUI smoke failed: " + game.current);
    if (Boolean.getBoolean("ctrl.menuOnly")) {
      if (Files.exists(save.resolve("save.json")))
        throw new AssertionError("Menu-only session created a save");
      System.out.println(
          "GUI_MENU_ONLY_PASS: closing settings without starting does not create a save");
    }
  }

  private void add(String name, BooleanSupplier f) {
    script.add(new Step(name, f));
  }

  private void tap(int key) {
    Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(key);
    Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyUp(key);
  }

  private void click(int x, int y) {
    Gdx.input
        .getInputProcessor()
        .touchDown(
            canvas().offsetX + x * canvas().scale,
            canvas().offsetY + y * canvas().scale,
            0,
            Input.Buttons.LEFT);
  }

  private void stop() {
    for (int k : new int[] {Keys.W, Keys.A, Keys.S, Keys.D}) Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyUp(k);
  }

  private void shot(String name) {
    canvas().screenshot(System.getProperty("ctrl.shots") + "/" + name + ".png");
  }

  private void check(boolean value, String error) {
    if (!value) throw new AssertionError(error);
  }

  private void once(String name, Runnable action) {
    add(
        name,
        () -> {
          action.run();
          return true;
        });
  }

  private void waitMode(Mode mode) {
    add("wait " + mode, () -> mode() == mode);
  }

  private void walk(float x, float y) {
    once("path to " + x + "," + y, () -> plan(x, y));
    add(
        "walk to " + x + "," + y,
        () -> {
          stop();
          if (path.isEmpty()) return true;
          var target = path.get(0);
          float dx = target[0] - world().state.x, dy = target[1] - world().state.y;
          if (Math.abs(dx) < 1.6 && Math.abs(dy) < 1.6) {
            path.remove(0);
            return path.isEmpty();
          }
          if (Math.abs(dx) > 1) Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(dx > 0 ? Keys.D : Keys.A);
          if (Math.abs(dy) > 1) Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(dy > 0 ? Keys.S : Keys.W);
          return false;
        });
  }

  private void choose(int n) {
    once("choose " + n, () -> tap(Keys.NUM_1 + n - 1));
  }

  private void interact() {
    once(
        "interact",
        () -> {
          stop();
          tap(Keys.E);
        });
  }

  private void plan(float tx, float ty) {
    path.clear();
    World w = world();
    int grid = 4, cols = w.map().width / grid, rows = w.map().height / grid;
    int start = Math.round(w.state.y / grid) * cols + Math.round(w.state.x / grid);
    int[] prev = new int[cols * rows];
    Arrays.fill(prev, -1);
    prev[start] = start;
    ArrayDeque<Integer> q = new ArrayDeque<>();
    q.add(start);
    int goal = -1;
    while (!q.isEmpty()) {
      int at = q.remove(), x = at % cols, y = at / cols;
      if (Math.hypot(x * grid - tx, y * grid - ty) < 3) {
        goal = at;
        break;
      }
      for (int[] d : new int[][] {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}}) {
        int nx = x + d[0], ny = y + d[1];
        if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= cols || ny >= rows) continue;
        int next = ny * cols + nx;
        if (prev[next] < 0
            && !w.blocked(nx * grid - 2, ny * grid - 2)
            && !w.blocked(nx * grid + 2, ny * grid - 2)
            && !w.blocked(nx * grid - 2, ny * grid + 2)
            && !w.blocked(nx * grid + 2, ny * grid + 2)) {
          prev[next] = at;
          q.add(next);
        }
      }
    }
    check(goal >= 0, "No path to " + tx + "," + ty);
    ArrayList<float[]> reverse = new ArrayList<>();
    for (int at = goal; at != start; at = prev[at])
      reverse.add(new float[] {at % cols * grid, at / cols * grid});
    Collections.reverse(reverse);
    path.addAll(reverse);
  }

  @Override
  public void create() {
    super.create();
    started = System.nanoTime();
    check(Boolean.getBoolean("ctrl.noAudio") || audioReady(), "Audio assets did not load");
    if (Boolean.getBoolean("ctrl.menuOnly")) {
      add("wait boot", () -> frames > 5);
      once("open settings", () -> click(300, 193));
      waitMode(Mode.SETTINGS);
      once("close without playing", () -> Gdx.app.exit());
      return;
    }
    add("wait boot", () -> frames > 5);
    once(
        "menu screenshot",
        () -> {
          shot("01-menu");
          tap(Keys.ENTER);
        });
    waitMode(Mode.CREATE);
    once(
        "create screenshot",
        () -> {
          shot("02-create");
          tap(Keys.DOWN);
          tap(Keys.ENTER);
        });
    waitMode(Mode.WORLD);

    walk(392, 116);
    interact();
    waitMode(Mode.DIALOGUE);
    choose(1);
    waitMode(Mode.WORLD);
    once("plant watered", () -> check(world().state.wateredPlant, "Watering did not persist"));
    walk(155, 240);
    interact();
    waitMode(Mode.DIALOGUE);
    choose(1);
    waitMode(Mode.WORLD);
    once("boxes unpacked", () -> check(world().state.unpacked, "Unpacking did not persist"));
    walk(110, 196);

        "outfit changed", () -> check(world().state.outfit == 1, "Wardrobe did not change outfit"));
    walk(128, 140);

    interact();

        "entered stair",
        () -> check(world().state.map.equals("stair"), "Door did not enter stair"));

    add(
        "approach Mira",
        () -> {
          stop();

          float dx = a.x - world().state.x, dy = a.y - world().state.y;
          if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) < 23) {
            tap(Keys.E);
            return true;

          if (Math.abs(dx) > 4) Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(dx > 0 ? Keys.D : Keys.A);
          if (Math.abs(dy) > 4) Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(dy > 0 ? Keys.S : Keys.W);
          return false;

    interact();
    once(

    waitMode(Mode.BAG);

    waitMode(Mode.WORLD);

          focusChanged(false);
          check(mode() == Mode.PAUSE, "Focus did not pause");
          check(world().state.x == x, "Unexpected movement");
          focusChanged(true);

        () -> {

          tap(Keys.DOWN);
          tap(Keys.DOWN);
          tap(Keys.ENTER);
        });
    waitMode(Mode.MENU);
    once("continue", () -> tap(Keys.ENTER));
    waitMode(Mode.WORLD);
    once(
        "restored",
        () -> {
          check(world().state.deliveredLetter, "Continue lost quest state");
          check(
              world().state.wateredPlant && world().state.unpacked && world().state.outfit == 1,
              "Continue lost object changes");
          check(world().state.map.equals("room"), "Continue lost map");
        });
    once(
        "move with remapped T",
        () -> {
          beforeMove = world().state.y;
          Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(Keys.T);
        });
    add("hold remapped key", () -> stepTime > .25);
    once(
        "remapped movement works",
        () -> {
          Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyUp(Keys.T);
          check(world().state.y < beforeMove - 8, "Remapped key did not move player");
        });
    once("960 resize", () -> Gdx.graphics.setWindowedMode(960, 540));
    add("resize settle", () -> stepTime > .3);
    once("small screenshot", () -> shot("15-window-960"));
    once("1080 resize", () -> Gdx.graphics.setWindowedMode(1920, 1080));
    add("large settle", () -> stepTime > .3);
    once("1080 screenshot", () -> shot("16-window-1080"));
    once("restore size", () -> Gdx.graphics.setWindowedMode(1440, 810));
    add(
        "soak",
        () -> {
          check(!world().blocked(world().state.x, world().state.y), "Invalid position during soak");
          stop();
          if (total >= Float.parseFloat(System.getProperty("ctrl.soakSeconds", "0"))) return true;
          if (world().state.x > 275) soakRight = false;
          if (world().state.x < 180) soakRight = true;
          Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(soakRight ? Keys.D : Keys.A);
          return false;
        });
    once(
        "finished",
        () -> {
          System.out.println(
              "GUI_SMOKE_PASS: movement, 3 maps, dialogues, quest, save/continue, focus, menus,"
                  + " resize. Frames="
                  + frames
                  + " seconds="
                  + total);
          Gdx.app.exit();
        });
  }

  @Override
  public void render() {
    super.render();
    frames++;
    float dt = Gdx.graphics.getDeltaTime();
    stepTime += dt;
    total = (System.nanoTime() - started) / 1e9f;
    if (script.isEmpty()) return;
    Step step = script.peek();
    current = step.name;
    try {
      if (step.run.getAsBoolean()) {
        System.out.println("GUI: " + current);
        script.remove();
        stepTime = 0;
      } else if (stepTime > 45 && !current.equals("soak"))
        throw new AssertionError("Timeout " + current);
    } catch (Throwable e) {
      failed = true;
      shot("FAILED");
      e.printStackTrace();
      Gdx.app.exit();
      script.clear();
    }
  }
}

package life.ctrl.desktop;

import com.badlogic.gdx.Gdx;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Input;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Input.Keys;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.Actor;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.ui.Window;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.ui.TextButton;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.Group;
import com.badlogic.gdx.math.Vector2;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.utils.ChangeListener;
import com.badlogic.gdx.backends.lwjgl3.Lwjgl3Application;
import com.badlogic.gdx.backends.lwjgl3.Lwjgl3ApplicationConfiguration;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import life.ctrl.CtrlLife;

/** Exercises a live Pinux desktop with GLFW input, rendering and window transitions. */
public final class PinuxSmoke extends CtrlLife {
  private final ArrayDeque<Runnable> steps = new ArrayDeque<>();
  private float timer;
  private boolean failed;
  private int nextShot;

  public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
    Path save = Path.of(System.getProperty("ctrl.dataDir"));
    Files.createDirectories(save);
    Path shots = Path.of(System.getProperty("ctrl.shots"));
    Files.createDirectories(shots);
    var config = new Lwjgl3ApplicationConfiguration();
    config.setWindowedMode(1440, 810);
    config.setForegroundFPS(60);
    config.useVsync(false);
    config.disableAudio(true);
    var test = new PinuxSmoke();
    new Lwjgl3Application(test, config);
    if (test.failed) throw new AssertionError("Pinux Astra window smoke failed");
  }

  private void tap(int key) {
    Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(key);
    Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyUp(key);
  }

  private void type(String text) {
    for (int i = 0; i < text.length(); i++)
      Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyTyped(text.charAt(i));
  }

  private void mouse(int operation, int x, int y) {
    int sx = canvas().offsetX + x * canvas().scale;
    int sy = canvas().offsetY + y * canvas().scale;
    switch (operation) {
      case 0 -> Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().touchDown(sx, sy, 0, Input.Buttons.LEFT);
      case 1 -> Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().touchDragged(sx, sy, 0);
      case 2 -> Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().touchUp(sx, sy, 0, Input.Buttons.LEFT);
      default -> throw new AssertionError("Unknown pointer operation");
    }
  }

  private void click(int x, int y) {
    mouse(0, x, y);
    mouse(2, x, y);
  }

  private void mouseAbsolute(int operation, int sx, int sy) {
    switch (operation) {
      case 0 -> Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().touchDown(sx, sy, 0, Input.Buttons.LEFT);
      case 1 -> Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().touchDragged(sx, sy, 0);
      case 2 -> Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().touchUp(sx, sy, 0, Input.Buttons.LEFT);
    }
  }

  private Window findWindow(String name) {
    for (Actor actor : lifeScreens().pcNativeStage().getRoot().getChildren()) {
      if (actor instanceof Window w && w.isVisible()
          && w.getTitleLabel().getText().toString().equals(name)) return w;
    }
    throw new AssertionError("Missing native VisUI window: " + name);
  }

  private TextButton findButton(Actor actor, String text) {
    if (actor instanceof TextButton b && b.getText().toString().equals(text)) return b;
    if (actor instanceof Group g)
      for (Actor child : g.getChildren()) {
        TextButton result = findButton(child, text);
        if (result != null) return result;
      }
    return null;
  }

  private void nativeAction(String text) {
    TextButton b = findButton(findWindow("Astra Code"), text);
    if (b == null || b.isDisabled()) throw new AssertionError("Missing native action: " + text);
    b.fire(new ChangeListener.ChangeEvent());
  }

  private void nativeClick(String text) {
    TextButton b = findButton(findWindow("Astra Code"), text);
    if (b == null || b.isDisabled()) throw new AssertionError("Missing native button: " + text);
    Vector2 p = b.localToStageCoordinates(new Vector2(b.getWidth() / 2, b.getHeight() / 2));
    mouseAbsolute(0, Math.round(p.x), Gdx.graphics.getHeight() - Math.round(p.y));
    mouseAbsolute(2, Math.round(p.x), Gdx.graphics.getHeight() - Math.round(p.y));
  }

  private void screenshot(String name) {
    canvas().screenshot(System.getProperty("ctrl.shots") + "/pinux-" + (++nextShot) + "-" + name + ".png");
  }

  @Override
  public void create() {
    super.create();
    // A test fixture: enter the computer directly, without navigating the prologue.
    lifeScreens().openCode();
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) steps.add(() -> {});
    steps.add(() -> screenshot("desktop"));
    steps.add(() -> {
      // Verify an actual native Scene2D shortcut click at 1440x810.
      click(30, 27);
      if (!lifeScreens().isLegacyEditorOpen())
        throw new AssertionError("Scene2D desktop shortcut did not open Astra Code");
    });
    steps.add(() -> screenshot("astra-code"));
    steps.add(() -> nativeClick("Открыть заказ")); // Real native hit test.
    steps.add(() -> screenshot("job-brief"));
    steps.add(() -> nativeAction(lifeScreens().data.job(world().state.activeJob).algorithms[0]));
    steps.add(() -> {
      if (lifeScreens().codePage != 2) throw new AssertionError("Astra Code editor not opened");
      screenshot("editor-typing");
    });
    steps.add(() -> { for (int i = 0; i < 80; i++) tap(Keys.A); });
    steps.add(() -> nativeAction("▶ Компиляция / тесты")); // Run ByteScript tests.
    steps.add(() -> {
      if (lifeScreens().report == null || lifeScreens().report.passed())
        throw new AssertionError("Expected the initial typo to fail tests");
      screenshot("editor-first-error");
    });
    steps.add(() -> { for (int i = 0; i < 80; i++) tap(Keys.S); });
    steps.add(() -> nativeAction("▶ Компиляция / тесты")); // Re-test corrected code.
    steps.add(() -> {
      if (lifeScreens().report == null || !lifeScreens().report.passed())
        throw new AssertionError("Automatic typed fix did not pass tests");
      screenshot("editor-fixed-tests");
    });
    steps.add(() -> nativeAction("Предпросмотр"));
    steps.add(() -> screenshot("code-preview"));
    steps.add(() -> nativeAction("← Вернуться к исходнику"));
    steps.add(() -> nativeAction("Лаборатория"));
    steps.add(() -> screenshot("code-profiler"));
    steps.add(() -> nativeAction("← К исходнику"));
    steps.add(() -> nativeAction("Git / версии"));
    steps.add(() -> screenshot("code-versions"));
    steps.add(() -> tap(Keys.F2));
    steps.add(() -> {
      Window terminal = findWindow("Terminal");
      if (!terminal.isMovable() || !terminal.isResizable())
        throw new AssertionError("The native terminal must support drag and resize");
      type("help\r");
    });
    steps.add(() -> screenshot("visui-terminal"));
    steps.add(() -> {
      Window terminal = findWindow("Terminal");
      float before = terminal.getX();
      int sx = Math.round(terminal.getX() + 110);
      int sy = Gdx.graphics.getHeight() - Math.round(terminal.getTop() - 17);
      mouseAbsolute(0, sx, sy);
      mouseAbsolute(1, sx + 75, sy + 26);
      mouseAbsolute(2, sx + 75, sy + 26);
      if (Math.abs(terminal.getX() - before) < 20)
        throw new AssertionError("Window title drag did not move VisWindow");
    });
    steps.add(() -> screenshot("visui-terminal-dragged"));
    steps.add(() -> {
      Window terminal = findWindow("Terminal");
      float oldWidth = terminal.getWidth(), oldHeight = terminal.getHeight();
      int sx = Math.round(terminal.getX() + terminal.getWidth() - 5);
      int sy = Gdx.graphics.getHeight() - Math.round(terminal.getY() + 5);
      mouseAbsolute(0, sx, sy);
      mouseAbsolute(1, sx + 64, sy + 30);
      mouseAbsolute(2, sx + 64, sy + 30);
      float dx = terminal.getWidth() - oldWidth, dy = terminal.getHeight() - oldHeight;
      if (Math.abs(dx) < 12 && Math.abs(dy) < 12)
        throw new AssertionError("Native window resize handle inactive. Initial="
            + oldWidth + "x" + oldHeight + ", final=" + terminal.getWidth()
            + "x" + terminal.getHeight() + ", stage-point=" + sx + "," + sy);
    });
    steps.add(() -> screenshot("visui-terminal-resized"));
    steps.add(() -> tap(Keys.F3));
    steps.add(() -> tap(Keys.F4));
    steps.add(() -> tap(Keys.F6));
    steps.add(() -> screenshot("multitasking"));
    steps.add(() -> tap(Keys.ESCAPE));
    steps.add(() -> tap(Keys.F1));
    steps.add(() -> screenshot("return-to-code"));
    steps.add(() -> {
      if (mode() != Mode.IDE) throw new AssertionError("Shell unexpectedly left IDE mode");
      System.out.println("PINUX_SMOKE_PASS: shell, code, terminal input, window drag, files, mail, settings, focus");
      Gdx.app.exit();
    });
  }

  @Override
  public void render() {
    super.render();
    timer += Gdx.graphics.getDeltaTime();
    if (steps.isEmpty() || timer < .15f) return;
    timer = 0;
    try {
      steps.removeFirst().run();
    } catch (Throwable t) {
      failed = true;
      t.printStackTrace();
      Gdx.app.exit();
    }
  }
}
END FILE: lwjgl3/src/smoke/java/life/ctrl/desktop/PinuxSmoke.java

package life.ctrl.desktop;

import com.badlogic.gdx.*;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Input.Keys;
import com.badlogic.gdx.backends.lwjgl3.*;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.Pixmap;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.*;
import life.ctrl.CtrlLife;

/** Explicit screenshot fixtures for every scene, lighting state and supported window size. */
public final class VisualSmoke extends CtrlLife {
  record Step(String name, Runnable run) {}

  final ArrayDeque<Step> steps = new ArrayDeque<>();
  float timer;
  boolean failed;
  String current = "boot";

  public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
    Path dir = Path.of(System.getProperty("ctrl.dataDir"));
    Files.createDirectories(dir);
    for (String f : List.of("save.json", "save.backup.json", "settings.json"))
      Files.deleteIfExists(dir.resolve(f));
    Files.createDirectories(Path.of(System.getProperty("ctrl.shots")));
    var cfg = new Lwjgl3ApplicationConfiguration();
    cfg.setWindowedMode(1440, 810);
    cfg.setForegroundFPS(60);
    cfg.useVsync(false);
    cfg.disableAudio(true);
    var g = new VisualSmoke();
    new Lwjgl3Application(g, cfg);
    if (g.failed) throw new AssertionError(g.current);
  }

  void step(String n, Runnable r) {
    steps.add(new Step(n, r));
  }

  void tap(int key) {
    Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(key);
    Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyUp(key);
  }

  void shot(String n) {
    canvas().screenshot(System.getProperty("ctrl.shots") + "/v4-" + n + ".png");
  }

  void fixture(String map, int hour, float x, float y) {
    var s = world().state;
    s.map = map;
    s.x = x;
    s.y = y;
    s.gameMinutes = 2 * 1440 + hour * 60;
    s.introSeen = true;
    s.health = 85;
    s.hunger = 75;
    s.energy = 80;
    switchMode(Mode.WORLD);
    new life.ctrl.model.World(life.ctrl.content.Content.load(), s);
    refreshState();
  }

  void noLightInVoid() {
    Pixmap p =
        Pixmap.createFromFrameBuffer(
            0, 0, Gdx.graphics.getBackBufferWidth(), Gdx.graphics.getBackBufferHeight());
    try {
      for (int x : new int[] {80, 400}) {
        int sx = canvas().offsetX + x * canvas().scale,
            sy = p.getHeight() - 1 - (canvas().offsetY + 135 * canvas().scale);
        int rgb = p.getPixel(sx, sy) >>> 8;
        if (rgb != 0x0e1a24)
          throw new AssertionError(
              "Effect leaked into scene void at " + x + ": " + Integer.toHexString(rgb));
      }
    } finally {
      p.dispose();
    }
  }

  @Override
  public void create() {
    super.create();
    step("menu", () -> shot("menu"));
    step("new", () -> tap(Keys.ENTER));
    step(
        "create",
        () -> {
          tap(Keys.DOWN);
          tap(Keys.ENTER);
        });
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) step("intro", () -> tap(Keys.ENTER));
    for (String map : List.of("room", "stair", "cafe", "diner", "workshop", "park", "street")) {
      for (int hour : new int[] {12, 22}) {
        String label = map + "-" + hour;
        step(
            "fixture " + label,
            () ->
                fixture(
                    map,
                    hour,
                    map.equals("street") ? 174 : 240,
                    map.equals("stair") ? 120 : map.equals("street") ? 250 : 190));
        step("settle " + label, () -> {});
        step(
            "capture " + label,
            () -> {
              shot(label);
              if (List.of("room", "cafe", "diner", "workshop").contains(map)) noLightInVoid();
            });
      }
    }
    for (float x : new float[] {470, 814, 900}) {
      step("street camera " + x, () -> fixture("street", 22, x, x == 900 ? 430 : 250));
      step("settle", () -> {});
      step("street capture", () -> shot("street-" + (int) x));
    }
    step("behind fridge", () -> fixture("diner", 12, 350, 133));
    step("settle occlusion", () -> {});
    step("fridge behind capture", () -> shot("fridge-behind"));
    step("in front of fridge", () -> fixture("diner", 12, 350, 160));
    step("settle occlusion", () -> {});
    step("fridge front capture", () -> shot("fridge-front"));
    step(
        "gear",
        () -> {
          world().state.upgrades.addAll(List.of("ssd", "chair"));
          fixture("room", 22, 240, 190);
        });
    step("settle", () -> {});
    step("room upgraded", () -> shot("upgraded"));
    step("open map", () -> tap(Keys.M));
    step(
        "map geometry",
        () -> {
          if (mode() != Mode.MAP) throw new AssertionError("Map did not open");
          shot("map");
          tap(Keys.ESCAPE);
        });
    step("workbench", () -> lifeScreens().openCode());
    step("workbench capture", () -> shot("workbench"));
    step(
        "stress long label",
        () -> {
          lifeScreens().codePage = 7;
          world().state.lastClientMessage =
              "Работа принята. Спасибо за внимательность к деталям и за быстрый результат!";
        });
    step("career capture", () -> shot("career"));
    for (int width : new int[] {960, 1280, 1920}) {
      step(
          "resize " + width,
          () -> Gdx.graphics.setWindowedMode(width, width == 1280 ? 800 : width * 9 / 16));
      step("settle resize", () -> {});
      step("size " + width, () -> shot("ui-" + width));
    }
    step(
        "done",
        () -> {
          System.out.println(
              "VISUAL_SMOKE_PASS: all seven maps, day/night, camera edges, upgrades, UI,"
                  + " 960/1280/1920, no lighting outside cutaways");
          Gdx.app.exit();
        });
  }

  @Override
  public void render() {
    super.render();
    timer += Gdx.graphics.getDeltaTime();
    if (timer < .6f || steps.isEmpty()) return;
    timer = 0;
    var s = steps.remove();
    current = s.name;
    try {
      s.run.run();
      System.out.println("VISUAL: " + current);
    } catch (Throwable e) {
      failed = true;
      shot("FAILED");
      e.printStackTrace();
      steps.clear();
      Gdx.app.exit();
    }
  }
}

package life.ctrl.desktop;

import com.badlogic.gdx.*;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Input.Keys;
import com.badlogic.gdx.backends.lwjgl3.*;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.*;
import life.ctrl.CtrlLife;
import life.ctrl.model.*;

/** Explicit mid-career fixtures; all workbench actions use actual mouse input and rendering. */
public final class WorkbenchSmoke extends CtrlLife {
  record Step(String name, Runnable run) {}

  final ArrayDeque<Step> steps = new ArrayDeque<>();
  float timer;
  boolean failed;
  String current = "boot";

  public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
    Path dir = Path.of(System.getProperty("ctrl.dataDir"));
    Files.createDirectories(dir);
    for (String name : List.of("save.json", "save.backup.json", "settings.json"))
      Files.deleteIfExists(dir.resolve(name));
    Files.createDirectories(Path.of(System.getProperty("ctrl.shots")));
    var cfg = new Lwjgl3ApplicationConfiguration();
    cfg.setWindowedMode(1440, 810);
    cfg.setForegroundFPS(60);
    cfg.useVsync(false);
    cfg.disableAudio(Boolean.getBoolean("ctrl.noAudio"));
    var game = new WorkbenchSmoke();
    new Lwjgl3Application(game, cfg);
    if (game.failed) throw new AssertionError(game.current);
  }

  void step(String name, Runnable r) {
    steps.add(new Step(name, r));
  }

  void click(int x, int y) {
    Gdx.input
        .getInputProcessor()
        .touchDown(
            canvas().offsetX + x * canvas().scale,
            canvas().offsetY + y * canvas().scale,
            0,
            Input.Buttons.LEFT);
  }

  void tap(int key) {
    Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(key);
    Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyUp(key);
  }

  void check(boolean b, String why) {
    if (!b) throw new AssertionError(why);
  }

  void shot(String name) {
    canvas().screenshot(System.getProperty("ctrl.shots") + "/v3-" + name + ".png");
  }

  void close() {
    step(
        "close result",
        () -> {
          check(mode() == Mode.SCENE, "Missing scene");
          click(240, 237);
        });
  }

  void project(int stage, int patch) {
    step("open side project " + stage, () -> lifeScreens().openCode());
    step("project tab", () -> click(280, 209));
    step("project brief", () -> click(240, 202));
    step("start stage", () -> click(240, 161));
    step("stage tests", () -> click(180, 237));
    step("stage debug", () -> click(280, 237));
    step("stage patch", () -> click(240, 161 + patch * 28));
    step("stage re-test", () -> click(180, 237));
    step(
        "stage preview",
        () -> {
          check(lifeScreens().report.passed(), "Stage tests failed");
          click(280, 237);
        });
    step("ship stage", () -> click(240, 210));
    close();
    step(
        "stage saved",
        () -> check(Career.projectStage(world().state) == stage, "Wrong side-project stage"));
  }

  @Override
  public void create() {
    super.create();
    step("new game", () -> tap(Keys.ENTER));
    step(
        "create",
        () -> {
          tap(Keys.DOWN);
          tap(Keys.ENTER);
        });
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) step("intro", () -> tap(Keys.ENTER));
    step(
        "career fixture",
        () -> {
          var s = world().state;
          s.codingXp = 8;
          s.completedOrders = 8;
          s.gameMinutes = 3 * 1440 + 480;
          s.rentThroughDay = 10;
          lifeScreens().openCode();
        });
    step("advanced listings", () -> click(70, 209));
    step(
        "rotated offers",
        () -> {
          shot("contracts");
          click(240, 122);
        });
    step("profile brief", () -> click(240, 161));
    step("slow program tests", () -> click(180, 237));
    step(
        "slow program rejected",
        () -> {
          check(
              lifeScreens().report.compiled() && !lifeScreens().report.passed(),
              "Slow correct code passed");
          shot("slow-code");
          click(340, 66);
        });
    step("custom input", () -> click(365, 86));
    step("probe one", () -> click(90, 237));
    step("custom input two", () -> click(420, 86));
    step("probe two", () -> click(90, 237));
    step(
        "novel cases counted",
        () -> {
          check(world().state.customCases.size() == 2, "Probe bonus count");
          shot("lab-before");
          click(380, 237);
        });
    step("profile patch view", () -> click(280, 237));
    step("select multiplication", () -> click(240, 189));
    step("git open", () -> click(425, 66));
    step("commit", () -> click(300, 66));
    close();
    step(
        "two revisions",
        () -> {
          check(world().state.revisions.size() == 2, "Missing commit");
          shot("git");
          click(120, 90);
        });
    close();
    step("back from git", () -> click(240, 240));
    step("old code tests", () -> click(180, 237));
    step(
        "rollback actually changed execution",
        () -> {
          check(!lifeScreens().report.passed(), "Rollback did not restore slow code");
          click(425, 66);
        });
    step("restore optimized commit", () -> click(130, 110));
    close();
    step("back restored", () -> click(240, 240));
    step("optimized tests", () -> click(180, 237));
    step(
        "optimized profile",
        () -> {
          check(lifeScreens().report.passed(), "Optimization failed");
          click(340, 66);
        });
    step(
        "profile screenshot",
        () -> {
          shot("lab-after");
          click(380, 237);
        });
    step("preview optimized", () -> click(280, 237));
    step("deliver optimized", () -> click(240, 210));
    close();
    step(
        "correct pay",
        () -> {
          check(world().state.money == 11550, "Expected 1450 + 100 bonus");
          lifeScreens().openCode();
        });
    step("upgrade tab", () -> click(390, 209));
    step("buy SSD", () -> click(390, 100));
    close();
    step("buy chair", () -> click(390, 174));
    close();
    step(
        "owned upgrades",
        () -> {
          check(world().state.money == 4550, "Upgrade costs");
          check(world().state.upgrades.size() == 2, "Upgrades absent");
          shot("upgrades");
          click(340, 240);
        });
    step(
        "upgraded desk",
        () -> {
          world().state.gameMinutes = 4 * 1440 + 21 * 60;
          refreshState();
        });
    step("night room", () -> shot("room-night"));
    project(1, 0);
    project(2, 1);
    project(3, 2);
    step("launched bot page", () -> lifeScreens().openCode());
    step("launched bot tab", () -> click(280, 209));
    step(
        "bot screenshot",
        () -> {
          shot("bot-live");
          click(340, 240);
        });
    step("save complete career", () -> tap(Keys.F5));
    step("pause", () -> tap(Keys.ESCAPE));
    step("return menu", () -> click(240, 190));
    step("continue", () -> tap(Keys.ENTER));
    step(
        "career survives load",
        () -> {
          check(mode() == Mode.WORLD, "Did not continue");
          check(Career.projectStage(world().state) == 3, "Project lost");
          check(world().state.upgrades.size() == 2, "Upgrades lost");
          lifeScreens().openCode();
        });
    step("career tab", () -> click(175, 209));
    step(
        "portfolio screenshot",
        () -> {
          shot("career");
          click(200, 213);
        });
    step(
        "portfolio list",
        () -> {
          check(lifeScreens().codePage == 11, "Portfolio did not open");
          shot("portfolio");
          click(200, 99);
        });
    step(
        "portfolio feedback",
        () -> {
          check(mode() == Mode.SCENE, "Project feedback did not open");
          click(240, 240);
        });
    step("back to career", () -> click(300, 230));
    step("leave career", () -> click(340, 240));
    step(
        "street daylight fixture",
        () -> {
          var s = world().state;
          s.map = "street";
          s.x = 174;
          s.y = 242;
          s.gameMinutes = 4 * 1440 + 12 * 60;
          refreshState();
        });
    step("camera settles", () -> {});
    step("street day", () -> shot("street-day"));
    step("street evening fixture", () -> world().state.gameMinutes = 4 * 1440 + 21 * 60);
    step("street night", () -> shot("street-night"));
    step(
        "complete",
        () -> {
          System.out.println(
              "WORKBENCH_SMOKE_PASS: mid-career fixture, profiler, custom inputs, commit/rollback,"
                  + " payout, gear, all project stages, persistence, lighting");
          Gdx.app.exit();
        });
  }

  @Override
  public void render() {
    super.render();
    timer += Gdx.graphics.getDeltaTime();
    if (timer < .25f || steps.isEmpty()) return;
    timer = 0;
    var step = steps.remove();
    current = step.name;
    try {
      step.run.run();
      System.out.println("WORKBENCH: " + current);
    } catch (Throwable e) {
      failed = true;
      shot("FAILED");
      e.printStackTrace();
      steps.clear();
      Gdx.app.exit();
    }
  }
}
